# Ink Core and Loadable Modules Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give Notolith handwriting: a runtime-loadable module tier, the open `.ink` format, the ink core library (model, modeler-smoothed pen input, variable-width outline rendering, tile cache, undo/redo), a handwritten-note tab, and the glyph studio shell, with the base app paying nothing when the packages are absent.

**Architecture:** A new static library `libs/modules` defines the `NotolithModule` plugin interface, the `ModuleHost` seam struct, a `ModuleView` widget base and a `ModuleRegistry` that discovers Qt plugins by `QPluginLoader::metaData()` (no dlopen) after first paint and loads one only on first use. The ink core is a shared library `libnotolith-inkcore.so` (model, format, input, outline, canvas); the handwritten-note tab (`notolith-ink.so`) and the glyph studio (`notolith-hwfont.so`) are modules that link only inkcore and Qt and resolve the app's static editor/theme/vault symbols from the executable at load (`ENABLE_EXPORTS`). Milestone A0 (Tasks 1-8, with the review-fix Task 2b between Tasks 2 and 3) builds the mechanism plus a single-page pen/eraser/undo canvas; then Track S1 (Tasks 9-19) and Track S1b (Tasks 20-21) run in parallel worktrees. Two install units ship: `notolith-ink` (inkcore plus the ink module) and `notolith-hwfont` (depends on `notolith-ink`).

**Tech Stack:** C++20, Qt 6.12 Widgets/Concurrent/Test, CMake 4 + Ninja, gtest 1.18, Google ink-stroke-modeler (Apache-2.0, FetchContent pinned to `f2388813b0b25bc3e33d143d369a8367ab2e30c8`, main HEAD on 2026-10-21) with system Abseil 20260817 (required: no Abseil, no ink core), zlib (gzip sniff), QJsonDocument.

**Rulings:** `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-21-ink-core/assessment-rulings.md` (R1-R15 and the Task 2b fix batch). Where a ruling and older plan text differ, the ruling and the spec win.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md` (approved; review rulings R1-R15 of 2026-10-22 and the PADD pen spike folded in). Research: `/home/jernej/Documents/AI/sessions/2026-10-21_handwritten-notes/` (`rnote-study.md`, `sol-ink-qt.md`, `sol-ink-modeler.md`, `code-map.md`, `plugin-map.md`, `NOTES.md`). Rnote fork with the seven page styles: `/home/jernej/Documents/AI/projects/rnote`, branch `jernej/features`, commit `fecb8f808a80abfef6d072c9afd123e7142e7ebc`.

## Global Constraints

- **Machine.** This plan runs on starbase-1 (hostname `starbase`, 24 threads). `taskset -c 6-11,18-23 chrt --idle 0` applies there (CCD1 only, SCHED_IDLE); on another machine drop the `taskset`/`chrt` prefix and keep `systemd-run`.
- **Builds and worktrees live in /tmp, never under ~/Documents.** Milestone A0 (Tasks 0-8) lives in the worktree `/tmp/noteAppC-wt-ink` (branch `ink/core`, which already holds Task 0 and this plan): every A0 `-S` is `/tmp/noteAppC-wt-ink`, the build dir is `/tmp/noteAppC-ink-build`. Track S1 uses worktree `/tmp/noteAppC-wt-s1` (branch `ink/s1`) and build dir `/tmp/noteAppC-ink-build-s1`; Track S1b uses `/tmp/noteAppC-wt-s1b` (branch `ink/s1b`) and `/tmp/noteAppC-ink-build-s1b`. **Both tracks branch from `ink/core`, not from `main`**; nothing is implemented on `main` without Jernej's consent, and the orchestrator merges `ink/core` into `main` only when he agrees (a later merge costs a rebase if he wants `main` earlier).
- **Every build and test is wrapped.** The three command shapes, used verbatim below (fish-safe: no `$(...)`, no `export`, no heredocs):
  - CONFIGURE: `systemd-run --user --scope -q -p MemoryMax=8G cmake -S <src> -B <build> -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=RelWithDebInfo -DBUILD_TESTING=ON`
  - BUILD: `systemd-run --user --scope -q -p MemoryMax=8G taskset -c 6-11,18-23 chrt --idle 0 cmake --build <build> --target <target>`
  - TEST: `env GTEST_FILTER='<Suite.Case>' systemd-run --user --scope -q -p MemoryMax=8G taskset -c 6-11,18-23 chrt --idle 0 ctest --test-dir <build> -R '^<ctest name>$' --output-on-failure`
  - ctest `-R` selects the registered test (`ink`, `modules`, `window`, ...), `GTEST_FILTER` the case. Tests run only through ctest: a test binary run directly skips nothing (IsolatedHome.cpp covers the XDG homes) but the ctest ENVIRONMENT (QT_QPA_PLATFORM, NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS) is what the suite was written against. From Task 2b on, every ctest entry sets `NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS` (an empty directory unless the test sets its own), so no test depends on what is installed on the machine.
- **Window tests are offscreen** (`QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen` through ctest). Nothing touches the real keyring, `~/.config`, `~/.cache`, `~/.local/share` or any vault under `~/Documents`.
- **Register new tests above the isolated-home `foreach` block** in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (the comment at line 350-356 says why).
- **Commits.** Conventional commits, every message ending with the line `Co-Authored-By: <executing model's name> <noreply@anthropic.com>` (the model that runs the step writes its own name; the commit steps below show the placeholder). Stage files explicitly by path: every commit step lists its `git add` paths; never `git commit -a`, never `git add -A`, never `git stash`. `demo/` and `HANDOFF.md` carry Jernej's unstaged edits and stay unstaged.
- **Naming is Modified camelCase**: initialisms stay upper-case, the next word is not forced to PascalCase: `userID`, `JSONdata`, `inkJSON`, `toJSON`, `ABItag`, `URLlist`. Never `userId`, `abiTag`, `toJson`. Qt-named overrides keep Qt's spelling.
- **No emojis** anywhere (code, comments, docs, commit messages).
- **Ported Rnote code carries this notice** at the top of the function or file (Rnote is GPL-3.0-or-later, as is Notolith):
  ```cpp
  // Ported from Rnote (<path in crates/>, <function>), copyright the Rnote
  // contributors, GPL-3.0-or-later, https://github.com/flxzt/rnote. The page
  // styles beyond the stock six are from Jernej's fork, branch jernej/features.
  ```
- **Performance rules** (docs/PERFORMANCE.md): nothing optional before first paint or on the keystroke path (rule 5); guard with ratios, not wall-clock numbers (rule 3); no UI-thread work for ink until the user touches an ink surface. The module scan runs on the thread pool after `m_painted = true`.
- **Module link rule.** Modules (`notolith-ink.so`, `notolith-hwfont.so`) link `notolith-inkcore` and Qt only. They include editor/theme/vault/settings/modules headers and resolve those symbols from the host executable: every executable that may load a module (`noteAppC`, `test_window`, `test_modules`, `test_ink`, `test_hwfont`, `noteAppC-bench`, `noteAppC_shot`) gets `ENABLE_EXPORTS ON`. The registry loads with `QLibrary::ResolveAllSymbolsHint`, so a missing symbol is a reported load failure, never a crash later. Nothing in `libs/editor`, `libs/index`, `libs/ui` links any ink library.
- **Host-side Qt types live in one binary.** `ModuleView` (Q_OBJECT) is moc'd only in `noteAppC_modules`; modules subclass it from the header and never list `ModuleHost.h` as their own source.
- **Units.** Document coordinates are CSS px at 96 dpi (A4 = 793.701 x 1122.52). Times fed to the modeler are seconds (`timestamp() / 1000.0`). Pressure is 0..1; mouse strokes use 0.5.
- **Spike-dependent constants** are named constants, never guesses silently baked in: `kHeldEraserButton`, `kHeldLassoButton`, `kTrustEraserPointerType`, `kInputRateHz`, `kFractionalScaleOffset` (all in `libs/ext/ink/core/InkInput.h`). The PADD spike is done (2026-10-22: himax-stylus, tip only, no eraser end, buttons dead, 120 Hz in contact, pressure maximum 0.657 with median 0.21, 128 equal-timestamp pairs and none backwards, device pixel ratio 2 with no offset), so the values are: `kInputRateHz = 120`, `kHeldEraserButton = Qt::MiddleButton` (BTN_STYLUS) and `kHeldLassoButton = Qt::RightButton` (BTN_STYLUS2) kept as bonus defaults, `kTrustEraserPointerType = true` (no eraser end seen; harmless), `kFractionalScaleOffset = QPointF{0, 0}`. Because PADD's pen has no working buttons and no eraser end, **every tool must be reachable from the toolbar and by a keyboard shortcut with no pen hardware**; held buttons are a bonus. Pressure is divided by a learned per-device maximum (setting `ink.pressureMax`, Tasks 6, 7 and 16).
- **Process (R13).** Every remaining task (2b onward) starts with a RED run: the test is built and seen failing before any implementation is written, and the failing output is pasted in the task's report. Each task gets an independent reviewer agent that did not write it.
- **System Abseil is required (R3).** The ink core needs `find_package(absl)`; when it is missing the ink core is not built (the option is forced off with a configure message). There is no bundled Abseil.
- **Android (R12).** Modules there are linked in as Qt static plugins (`Q_IMPORT_PLUGIN`) and `ModuleRegistry` also lists `QPluginLoader::staticPlugins()`. No Android build work is in this plan.
- **Jernej's files stay his.** `demo/`, `HANDOFF.md`, `TODO.md` at the repo root are not touched by this plan.

## Review Focus

1. **A `.ink` file truncated mid-write by a sync client** (valid gzip magic or JSON cut mid-document): the reader must return `ok = false` with a message naming the file, never a half-document the autosave then writes back over the original. Test pinned in Task 4.
2. **Zero-length and one-point strokes in the outline composer** (a tap): must paint a dot of the start width and never produce NaN offsets (normalising a zero vector). Test pinned in Task 5.
3. **Pressure outside 0..1 and timestamps that go backwards** (a compositor replaying buffered events): the input stage clamps pressure, skips strictly backwards samples and coalesces equal-timestamp ones (the newer replaces the older) instead of handing the modeler input it rejects. Tests pinned in Task 6.
4. **A non-plugin `.so` or a stray file in the module directory** (`libfoo.so.1`, `README`): the scan must skip it silently, not report it as a broken module. Test pinned in Task 1.
5. **Erase every stroke, autosave, then undo**: the spatial grid must come back with the strokes, the canvas must repaint them, and `isModified()` must be true after the undo (the file on disk now holds the erased state). Test pinned in Task 7.
6. **A pen that never reports above 0.657** (PADD): without the learned per-device maximum every stroke is thin. The normaliser must reach full width at the device's peak, must not distort mouse strokes (0.5), and a stored maximum must apply from the first sample. Tests pinned in Tasks 6 and 7.
7. **A file-less module tab with unsaved work** (the glyph studio): the tab shows the modified mark and close or quit saves or asks, exactly as for a file tab. Test pinned in Task 8.
8. **A module that claims a suffix already claimed, a manifest with no id, a library that is not a module, an unresolved symbol**: each yields one problem line in Settings and never a crash or a silent drop. Tests pinned in Task 2b.

## Task 0: Pen spike (`tools/inkspike`) - run by Jernej, then STOP

> **Done 2026-10-22.** Jernej ran the spike on PADD; the results are in the session NOTES.md and folded into Global Constraints, Tasks 6, 7 and 16 (R14). Kept below as the record of how it was built.

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/inkspike/CMakeLists.txt`
- Create: `tools/inkspike/main.cpp`
- Modify: `tools/CMakeLists.txt` (append one guarded `add_subdirectory(inkspike)`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Qt6::Widgets only.
- Produces: a log on stderr and in the window, copied by Jernej into `/home/jernej/Documents/AI/sessions/2026-10-21_handwritten-notes/NOTES.md` under a heading `## Pen spike results (Task 0)`.

**What later tasks read from the results** (Task 6 sets the constants in `InkInput.h`):
- `kTrustEraserPointerType`: true if the eraser end arrives as `pointerType() == Eraser` on both PADD and shuttlepod-1; false means the toolbar eraser is the only eraser and Task 6 skips the pointer-type switch.
- `kHeldEraserButton` / `kHeldLassoButton`: which `Qt::MouseButton` the lower and upper barrel buttons produce (spec defaults `MiddleButton` for BTN_STYLUS = held eraser, `RightButton` for BTN_STYLUS2 = held lasso; sol-ink-qt.md section 1).
- `kInputRateHz`: the measured steady event rate while scribbling (expected 120-240). It feeds only the modeler's `min_output_rate`; the wobble smoother timeout stays the 0.04 s preset constant.
- `kFractionalScaleOffset`: a `QPointF`, `{0, 0}` by default. If, at GNOME fractional scaling on PADD, the pen position and the drawn ink differ by a constant, the spike prints `event->position()` against `QCursor::pos()` mapped to the widget so the offset is visible; Task 7 subtracts it from the widget position of every tablet event.
- Whether `QTabletEvent::timestamp()` is monotonic and in ms (it should be). Task 6 coalesces two events that share a timestamp (the newer replaces the older) and skips a strictly backwards one; both are tested there.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the spike**

`tools/inkspike/CMakeLists.txt`:
```cmake
# Task 0 pen spike (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md section 11).
# A developer tool: logs tablet events and draws raw polylines, nothing else.
qt_add_executable(noteAppC-inkspike main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(noteAppC-inkspike PRIVATE Qt6::Widgets)
```

`tools/inkspike/main.cpp`:
```cpp
// Pen spike: logs every tablet event (device, pointer type, capabilities,
// pressure, tilt, buttons, timestamp, rate) and scribbles raw polylines.
// Run by hand on PADD (GNOME) and shuttlepod-1 (Hyprland); results go to the
// session NOTES.md. No ink code depends on this file.
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCursor>
#include <QElapsedTimer>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QMouseEvent>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPlainTextEdit>
#include <QPointingDevice>
#include <QSplitter>
#include <QTabletEvent>
#include <QTouchEvent>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QWidget>
#include <cstdio>

class Scribble : public QWidget {
public:
    QPlainTextEdit *log = nullptr;
    Scribble() { setTabletTracking(true); setAttribute(Qt::WA_AcceptTouchEvents); setMinimumSize(600, 400); }

protected:
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *) override
    {
        QPainter p(this);
        p.fillRect(rect(), Qt::white);
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
        for (const QPolygonF &line : m_lines)
            p.drawPolyline(line);
        p.drawPolyline(m_live);
        p.setPen(Qt::red);
        p.drawText(8, 16, QStringLiteral("rate %1 Hz  events %2").arg(m_rateHz, 0, 'f', 0).arg(m_events));
    }
    void tabletEvent(QTabletEvent *e) override
    {
        const QPointingDevice *dev = e->pointingDevice();
        const QPoint cursor = mapFromGlobal(QCursor::pos());
        const QString line = QStringLiteral("%1 dev=%2 type=%3 ptr=%4 caps=0x%5 pos=%6,%7 cursor=%8,%9 p=%10 tilt=%11,%12 btn=%13 btns=0x%14 t=%15")
            .arg(typeName(e->type()), dev ? dev->name() : QStringLiteral("?"))
            .arg(dev ? int(dev->type()) : -1).arg(int(e->pointerType()))
            .arg(dev ? int(dev->capabilities()) : 0, 0, 16)
            .arg(e->position().x(), 0, 'f', 2).arg(e->position().y(), 0, 'f', 2)
            .arg(cursor.x()).arg(cursor.y())
            .arg(e->pressure(), 0, 'f', 3).arg(e->xTilt(), 0, 'f', 1).arg(e->yTilt(), 0, 'f', 1)
            .arg(int(e->button())).arg(int(e->buttons()), 0, 16).arg(e->timestamp());
        emitLine(line);
        rate(e->timestamp());
        if (e->type() == QEvent::TabletPress) { m_live.clear(); m_live << e->position(); }
        else if (e->type() == QEvent::TabletMove && (e->buttons() & Qt::LeftButton)) m_live << e->position();
        else if (e->type() == QEvent::TabletRelease) { m_lines << m_live; m_live.clear(); }
        e->accept();
        update();
    }
    bool event(QEvent *e) override
    {
        if (e->type() == QEvent::TouchBegin || e->type() == QEvent::TouchUpdate || e->type() == QEvent::TouchEnd) {
            auto *t = static_cast<QTouchEvent *>(e);
            emitLine(QStringLiteral("%1 points=%2").arg(typeName(e->type())).arg(t->points().size()));
            e->accept();
            return true;
        }
        return QWidget::event(e);
    }
    void mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *e) override
    {
        emitLine(QStringLiteral("MousePress source=%1 pos=%2,%3 (synthesised from tablet: %4)")
                     .arg(int(e->source())).arg(e->position().x()).arg(e->position().y())
                     .arg(e->source() == Qt::MouseEventSynthesizedByQt ? "yes" : "no"));
    }

private:
    static QString typeName(QEvent::Type t)
    {
        switch (t) {
        case QEvent::TabletPress: return QStringLiteral("TabletPress");
        case QEvent::TabletMove: return QStringLiteral("TabletMove");
        case QEvent::TabletRelease: return QStringLiteral("TabletRelease");
        case QEvent::TouchBegin: return QStringLiteral("TouchBegin");
        case QEvent::TouchUpdate: return QStringLiteral("TouchUpdate");
        case QEvent::TouchEnd: return QStringLiteral("TouchEnd");
        default: return QString::number(int(t));
        }
    }
    void emitLine(const QString &line)
    {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", qPrintable(line));
        if (log) log->appendPlainText(line);
    }
    void rate(quint64 timestampMs)
    {
        ++m_events;
        if (m_windowStart == 0) m_windowStart = timestampMs;
        ++m_windowEvents;
        if (timestampMs - m_windowStart >= 1000) {
            m_rateHz = double(m_windowEvents) * 1000.0 / double(timestampMs - m_windowStart);
            m_windowStart = timestampMs;
            m_windowEvents = 0;
        }
    }
    QList<QPolygonF> m_lines;
    QPolygonF m_live;
    quint64 m_windowStart = 0;
    int m_windowEvents = 0;
    int m_events = 0;
    double m_rateHz = 0;
};

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    QSplitter window;
    auto *scribble = new Scribble;
    auto *log = new QPlainTextEdit;
    log->setReadOnly(true);
    log->setMaximumBlockCount(2000);
    scribble->log = log;
    window.addWidget(scribble);
    window.addWidget(log);
    window.setWindowTitle(QStringLiteral("inkspike - devicePixelRatio %1").arg(scribble->devicePixelRatioF()));
    window.resize(1200, 700);
    window.show();
    return app.exec();
}
```

- Modify: `tests/CMakeLists.txt`: fake modules + `test_modules`, inserted directly above the existing `window` test block (`add_executable(test_window ...)`), which is itself above the isolated-home block

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Settings`, `Theme`, `Vault` headers (for `ModuleHost` pointers only).
- Produces (used by Tasks 2, 8, 20, 21 and the fake module):

```cpp
// libs/modules/NotolithModule.h
namespace noteAppC {
struct ModuleHost;
class NotolithModule {
public:
    virtual ~NotolithModule() = default;
    virtual void attach(ModuleHost &host) = 0;
};
}
#define NotolithModule_iid "io.github.jernejmarcic.Notolith.Module/1"
Q_DECLARE_INTERFACE(noteAppC::NotolithModule, NotolithModule_iid)

// libs/modules/ModuleHost.h
namespace noteAppC {
class ModuleView : public QWidget {  // Q_OBJECT; moc'd only in noteAppC_modules
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    using QWidget::QWidget;
    virtual bool load(const QString &path, QString *error) = 0;
    virtual bool save(QString *error) = 0;
    virtual bool isModified() const = 0;
    virtual void setTheme(const Theme &theme) = 0;
    virtual void applySettings(const Settings &settings) { Q_UNUSED(settings) } // the window calls it after every settings change
signals:
    void modificationChanged(bool modified);
    void noteActivated(const QString &relativePath);
    void statusMessage(const QString &text, int timeoutMs);
};
struct ModuleHost {
    using ViewFactory = std::function<ModuleView *(const QString &path, QWidget *parent)>;
    using IndexReader = std::function<std::optional<QString>(const QByteArray &bytes)>;
    using TabletHandler = std::function<bool(QTabletEvent *event, const QString &path)>; // seam 2, declared only
    struct SettingRow { QString key; QVariant defaultValue; QString label; QString description;
                        QList<QPair<QString, QVariant>> options;
                        double minimum = 0; double maximum = 0; double step = 1; int decimals = 0; }; // decimals > 0: a double spin box
    std::function<void(const QStringList &suffixes, ViewFactory factory)> registerView;      // seam 1
    std::function<void(const QString &id, const QString &title, std::function<void()> run)> registerCommand;
    std::function<void(const SettingRow &row)> registerSetting;
    std::function<void(const QStringList &suffixes, IndexReader reader)> registerIndexReader; // seam 6 as declared: unused, .ink indexing is host-side (R1, Task 17)
    std::function<void(const QStringList &suffixes, TabletHandler handler)> registerTabletHandler; // null in Spec 1
    std::function<void(ModuleView *view, const QString &title)> showTab;
    std::function<void(const QString &text, int timeoutMs)> showStatus;
    std::function<bool(const QString &absolutePath)> openFile;
    std::function<QString()> currentFolder;  // folder of the active note, else the vault root; empty without a vault
    Settings *settings = nullptr;
    const Theme *theme = nullptr;
    const Vault *vault = nullptr;
};
}

// libs/modules/ModuleRegistry.h
namespace noteAppC {
struct ModuleInfo {
    QString id, featureID, name, description, path, ABItag;
    QStringList suffixes, indexSuffixes, dependencies; ///< indexSuffixes: loaded after the scan when the vault holds such files
    struct Command { QString id; QString title; };
    QList<Command> commands;
    QString problem; ///< empty when loadable; else why not ("built for ...", "needs module ...")
};
class ModuleRegistry : public QObject {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit ModuleRegistry(QObject *parent = nullptr);
    static QString ABItag();                       // NOTEAPPC_VERSION "-" NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH "-" QT_VERSION_STR
    static QStringList defaultDirectories();       // NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS (':'-separated) else $XDG_DATA_HOME/noteAppC/modules, <libdir>/noteAppC/modules
    using SuffixPredicate = std::function<bool(const QString &suffix)>;
    /// Metadata only; any thread. A module that claims a suffix `isBuiltInSuffix` says the app opens itself gets a `problem` and is never loaded.
    static QList<ModuleInfo> scan(const QStringList &directories, const SuffixPredicate &isBuiltInSuffix = {});
    void setModules(const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules);
    const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules() const { return m_modules; }
    void setEnabledCheck(std::function<bool(const QString &featureID)> check);
    bool isEnabled(const ModuleInfo &module) const;
    const ModuleInfo *moduleForSuffix(const QString &path) const;    // enabled, no problem
    const ModuleInfo *moduleForCommand(const QString &commandID) const;
    bool ensureLoaded(const ModuleInfo &module, ModuleHost &host, QString *error); // UI thread; once per id
    bool isLoaded(const QString &id) const;
};
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake module and its manifest**

`tests/fakemodule/manifest.json.in`:
```json
{
    "id": "@FAKE_ID@",
    "feature": "@FAKE_ID@",
    "name": "Fake module (@FAKE_ID@)",
    "description": "A module built by the test suite.",
    "suffixes": ["@FAKE_SUFFIX@"],
    "indexSuffixes": [@FAKE_INDEX_SUFFIXES@],
    "commands": [{"id": "@FAKE_ID@:hello", "title": "Fake: say hello"}, {"id": "@FAKE_ID@:where", "title": "Fake: show the current folder"}],
    "dependencies": [@FAKE_DEPS@],
    "abi": "@FAKE_ABI@"
}
```

`tests/fakemodule/FakeModule.cpp`:
```cpp
// The test suite's module: discovered by metadata, loaded only on first use.
// Its static initializer leaves a mark, so a test can prove that reading the
// manifest did not load the library (spec section 4, "Gate").
#include <QFile>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QtPlugin>

#include "ModuleHost.h"
#include "NotolithModule.h"

namespace {
const bool marked = [] {
    const QByteArray mark = qgetenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULE_MARK");
    if (!mark.isEmpty()) {
        QFile file(QString::fromLocal8Bit(mark));
        if (file.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Append))
            file.write("loaded\n");
    }
    return true;
}();

class FakeView : public noteAppC::ModuleView {
public:
    explicit FakeView(QWidget *parent) : ModuleView(parent)
    {
        setObjectName(QStringLiteral("fakeView"));
        auto *layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
        layout->addWidget(m_label = new QLabel(this));
    }
    bool load(const QString &path, QString *) override { m_path = path; m_label->setText(path); return true; }
    bool save(QString *) override { return true; }
    bool isModified() const override { return false; }
    void setTheme(const noteAppC::Theme &) override {}
private:
    QLabel *m_label = nullptr;
    QString m_path;
};
} // namespace

class FakeModule : public QObject, public noteAppC::NotolithModule {
    Q_OBJECT
    Q_PLUGIN_METADATA(IID NotolithModule_iid FILE "manifest.json")
    Q_INTERFACES(noteAppC::NotolithModule)
public:
    void attach(noteAppC::ModuleHost &host) override
    {
        host.registerView({QStringLiteral(FAKE_SUFFIX)},
                          [](const QString &, QWidget *parent) -> noteAppC::ModuleView * { return new FakeView(parent); });
        const auto status = host.showStatus;
        const auto folder = host.currentFolder;
        host.registerCommand(QStringLiteral(FAKE_ID ":hello"), QStringLiteral("Fake: say hello"),
                             [status] { if (status) status(QStringLiteral("hello from the fake module"), 2000); });
        host.registerCommand(QStringLiteral(FAKE_ID ":where"), QStringLiteral("Fake: show the current folder"),
                             [status, folder] { if (status && folder) status(folder(), 2000); });
        host.registerSetting({QStringLiteral("fake.greeting"), true, QStringLiteral("Fake: greet"),
                              QStringLiteral("A toggle the fake module registered."), {}, 0, 0});
        // A decimal row (spec section 4, settings rows): a double spin box, not an integer one.
        host.registerSetting({QStringLiteral("fake.width"), 2.0, QStringLiteral("Fake: width"),
                              QStringLiteral("A decimal the fake module registered."), {}, 0.5, 40.0, 0.5, 1});
    }
};

#include "FakeModule.moc"
```
`FAKE_ID` and `FAKE_SUFFIX` are compile definitions per target (see Step 3), so the four fake modules register distinct command ids and suffixes.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing gate tests**

`tests/test_modules.cpp`:
```cpp
// The loadable-module tier (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md
// section 4): discovery by metadata does not load the library; a module loads
// on first use; a wrong ABI tag or a missing dependency is reported, not loaded.
#include <gtest/gtest.h>

#include <link.h>

#include <QApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QTemporaryDir>

#include "ModuleHost.h"
#include "ModuleRegistry.h"

using namespace noteAppC;

namespace {

/// Every shared object mapped into this process, by absolute path.
QStringList loadedObjects()
{
    QStringList out;
    dl_iterate_phdr(
        [](dl_phdr_info *info, size_t, void *data) {
            if (info->dlpi_name && *info->dlpi_name)
                static_cast<QStringList *>(data)->append(QFileInfo(QString::fromLocal8Bit(info->dlpi_name)).canonicalFilePath());
            return 0;
        },
        &out);
    return out;
}

QString fakeDir() { return QStringLiteral(NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULES_DIR); }
QString fakePath(const char *name) { return QDir(fakeDir()).filePath(QLatin1String(name)); }

const ModuleInfo *byID(const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules, const char *id)
{
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : modules)
        if (module.id == QLatin1String(id))
            return &module;
    return nullptr;
}

} // namespace

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, ScanReadsManifestsWithoutLoadingTheLibrary)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    const QString mark = tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("mark"));
    qputenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULE_MARK", mark.toLocal8Bit());
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *good = byID(modules, "fake");
    ASSERT_NE(good, nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(good->suffixes, QStringList{QStringLiteral("fake")});
    EXPECT_TRUE(good->indexSuffixes.isEmpty());
    ASSERT_EQ(good->commands.size(), 2);
    EXPECT_EQ(good->commands.first().id, QStringLiteral("fake:hello"));
    const ModuleInfo *indexed = byID(modules, "fake-indexed");
    ASSERT_NE(indexed, nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(indexed->indexSuffixes, QStringList{QStringLiteral("fakeidx")});
    EXPECT_EQ(good->ABItag, ModuleRegistry::ABItag());
    EXPECT_TRUE(good->problem.isEmpty()) << good->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_FALSE(QFile::exists(mark)) << "the static initializer ran: metaData() loaded the library";
    EXPECT_FALSE(loadedObjects().contains(QFileInfo(good->path).canonicalFilePath()));
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, StrayFilesInTheDirectoryAreSkippedSilently)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    QFile(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("README"))).open(QIODevice::WriteOnly);
    QFile(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("libfoo.so.1"))).open(QIODevice::WriteOnly);
    QFile notAPlugin(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("junk.so")));
    ASSERT_TRUE(notAPlugin.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly));
    notAPlugin.write("not an ELF file");
    notAPlugin.close();
    EXPECT_TRUE(ModuleRegistry::scan({tmp.path()}).isEmpty());
    EXPECT_TRUE(ModuleRegistry::scan({tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("missing"))}).isEmpty());
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, LoadsOnFirstUseAndAttachesOnce)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    const QString mark = tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("mark"));
    qputenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULE_MARK", mark.toLocal8Bit());
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    registry.setModules(ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}));
    registry.setEnabledCheck([](const QString &) { return true; });
    const ModuleInfo *good = registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("/v/a.fake"));
    ASSERT_NE(good, nullptr);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(good->id));

    int views = 0, commands = 0, settings = 0;  // the fake registers 2 commands and 2 setting rows
    ModuleHost host;
    host.registerView = [&](const QStringList &suffixes, ModuleHost::ViewFactory) { views += suffixes.size(); };
    host.registerCommand = [&](const QString &, const QString &, std::function<void()>) { ++commands; };
    host.registerSetting = [&](const ModuleHost::SettingRow &) { ++settings; };
    QString error;
    ASSERT_TRUE(registry.ensureLoaded(*good, host, &error)) << error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(registry.isLoaded(good->id));
    EXPECT_TRUE(QFile::exists(mark));
    EXPECT_TRUE(loadedObjects().contains(QFileInfo(good->path).canonicalFilePath()));
    EXPECT_EQ(views, 1);
    EXPECT_EQ(commands, 2);
    EXPECT_EQ(settings, 2);
    ASSERT_TRUE(registry.ensureLoaded(*good, host, &error)); // a second call attaches nothing again
    EXPECT_EQ(views, 1);
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, WrongABItagIsRefusedWithANoticeNamingTheModule)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *bad = byID(modules, "fake-badabi");
    ASSERT_NE(bad, nullptr);
    EXPECT_FALSE(bad->problem.isEmpty());
    EXPECT_TRUE(bad->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("fake-badabi")));
    EXPECT_TRUE(bad->problem.contains(ModuleRegistry::ABItag()));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    registry.setModules(modules);
    registry.setEnabledCheck([](const QString &) { return true; });
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*bad, host, &error));
    EXPECT_EQ(error, bad->problem);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(bad->id));
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, MissingDependencyIsReportedAndNotLoaded)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *dep = byID(modules, "fake-missingdep");
    ASSERT_NE(dep, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(dep->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("nonexistent-module")));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    registry.setModules(modules);
    registry.setEnabledCheck([](const QString &) { return true; });
    EXPECT_EQ(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("x.fake"))->id, QStringLiteral("fake")); // the good one wins
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*dep, host, &error));
    EXPECT_EQ(error, dep->problem);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(dep->id));
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, AModuleMayNotClaimASuffixTheAppOpensItself)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules =
        ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}, [](const QString &suffix) { return suffix == QLatin1String("fake"); });
    const ModuleInfo *claimant = byID(modules, "fake");
    ASSERT_NE(claimant, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(claimant->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("built-in file type .fake"))) << claimant->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(claimant->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("fake")));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    registry.setModules(modules);
    registry.setEnabledCheck([](const QString &) { return true; });
    EXPECT_EQ(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fake")), nullptr) << "the built-in kind keeps the suffix";
    const ModuleInfo *other = byID(modules, "fake-indexed");
    ASSERT_NE(other, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(other->problem.isEmpty()) << "a module with other suffixes is not affected";
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, DisabledFeatureUnregistersTheSuffixAndCommand)
{
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    registry.setModules(ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}));
    bool on = true;
    registry.setEnabledCheck([&on](const QString &featureID) { return featureID != QLatin1String("fake") || on; });
    EXPECT_NE(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.FAKE")), nullptr);
    EXPECT_NE(registry.moduleForCommand(QStringLiteral("fake:hello")), nullptr);
    on = false;
    EXPECT_EQ(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fake")), nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(registry.moduleForCommand(QStringLiteral("fake:hello")), nullptr);
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, DefaultDirectoriesHonourTheOverride)
{
    qputenv("NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS", "/tmp/a:/tmp/b");
    EXPECT_EQ(ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories(), (QStringList{QStringLiteral("/tmp/a"), QStringLiteral("/tmp/b")}));
    qunsetenv("NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS");
    const QStringList dirs = ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories();
    ASSERT_EQ(dirs.size(), 2);
    EXPECT_TRUE(dirs.first().endsWith(QStringLiteral("/noteAppC/modules")));
}

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    qputenv("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen");
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // views are widgets
    ::testing::InitGoogleTest(&argc, argv);
    return RUN_ALL_TESTS();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: CMake for the library, the fake modules and the test**

`libs/modules/CMakeLists.txt`:
```cmake
# The loadable-module tier (docs/PLUGINS.md, "Tier 1.5"; spec section 4):
# the plugin interface a module implements, the ModuleHost seam struct the
# window fills, the ModuleView widget base, and the registry that discovers
# modules by metadata and loads one on first use.
#
# Two targets on purpose. noteAppC_modules_headers is INTERFACE: a module
# links it for the headers alone and resolves ModuleView's metaobject from
# the executable (ENABLE_EXPORTS), so there is exactly one copy. noteAppC_ui
# links the STATIC noteAppC_modules, which carries the moc and the registry.
add_library(noteAppC_modules_headers INTERFACE)
target_include_directories(noteAppC_modules_headers INTERFACE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR})
target_link_libraries(noteAppC_modules_headers INTERFACE Qt6::Widgets)

add_library(noteAppC_modules STATIC
    ModuleHost.h
    ModuleRegistry.cpp
    ModuleRegistry.h
    NotolithModule.h)
set_target_properties(noteAppC_modules PROPERTIES AUTOMOC ON)
target_link_libraries(noteAppC_modules PUBLIC noteAppC_modules_headers noteAppC_settings noteAppC_theme noteAppC_vault)
target_compile_definitions(noteAppC_modules PRIVATE
    NOTEAPPC_VERSION="${PROJECT_VERSION}"
    NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH="${NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH}"
    NOTEAPPC_MODULE_LIBDIR="${CMAKE_INSTALL_FULL_LIBDIR}/noteAppC/modules")
```

Root `CMakeLists.txt`, insert after line 22 (`qt_standard_project_setup()`):
```cmake
# The ABI tag every loadable module carries (spec section 4): same build only.
# The git hash is read at configure time; a commit without a reconfigure
# leaves app and modules on the same stale hash, which is still consistent.
include(GNUInstallDirs)
execute_process(COMMAND git -C ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR} rev-parse --short=12 HEAD
    OUTPUT_VARIABLE NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE
    RESULT_VARIABLE noteappc_git_result ERROR_QUIET)
if(NOT noteappc_git_result EQUAL 0)
    set(NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH "nogit")
endif()
set(NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG "${PROJECT_VERSION}-${NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH}-${Qt6_VERSION}")
```
and after line 291 (`add_subdirectory(libs/settings)`):
```cmake
add_subdirectory(libs/modules)
```
(The existing `include(GNUInstallDirs)` at line 375 stays; including twice is harmless.)

`tests/CMakeLists.txt`, insert directly above the existing `window` test block (`add_executable(test_window ...)`; Task 2 adds its own lines after that block's `add_test`):
```cmake
# The loadable-module tier (libs/modules): four fake modules from one source,
# differing only in their configure_file'd manifest - a good one, one built
# for another Notolith (wrong ABI tag), one needing a module that is not there,
# and one declaring index suffixes (loaded after the scan when the vault holds
# such files). Headers only from the host: no host static library is linked.
function(noteappc_fake_module target id abi deps suffix indexSuffixes)
    set(FAKE_ID ${id})
    set(FAKE_ABI ${abi})
    set(FAKE_DEPS "${deps}")
    set(FAKE_SUFFIX ${suffix})
    set(FAKE_INDEX_SUFFIXES "${indexSuffixes}")
    configure_file(fakemodule/manifest.json.in ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${target}/manifest.json @ONLY)
    add_library(${target} MODULE fakemodule/FakeModule.cpp)
    target_include_directories(${target} PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${target})
    target_compile_definitions(${target} PRIVATE FAKE_ID="${id}" FAKE_SUFFIX="${suffix}")
    target_link_libraries(${target} PRIVATE noteAppC_modules_headers Qt6::Widgets)
    set_target_properties(${target} PROPERTIES AUTOMOC ON PREFIX ""
        LIBRARY_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/fakemodules)
endfunction()
noteappc_fake_module(fake_good fake "${NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG}" "" fake "")
noteappc_fake_module(fake_badabi fake-badabi "0.0.0-deadbeef-6.0.0" "" fake "")
noteappc_fake_module(fake_missingdep fake-missingdep "${NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG}" "\"nonexistent-module\"" fake "")
noteappc_fake_module(fake_indexed fake-indexed "${NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG}" "" fakeidx "\"fakeidx\"")

add_executable(test_modules test_modules.cpp)
target_link_libraries(test_modules PRIVATE noteAppC_modules Qt6::Widgets GTest::gtest)
target_compile_definitions(test_modules PRIVATE NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULES_DIR="${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/fakemodules")
set_target_properties(test_modules PROPERTIES ENABLE_EXPORTS ON) # the fake module resolves ModuleView here
add_dependencies(test_modules fake_good fake_badabi fake_missingdep fake_indexed)
add_test(NAME modules COMMAND test_modules)
set_tests_properties(modules PROPERTIES ENVIRONMENT "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen")
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the headers**

`libs/modules/NotolithModule.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <QtPlugin>

namespace noteAppC {

struct ModuleHost;

/// What a loadable module implements (spec section 4). One method: the
/// module registers its views, commands and settings rows with the host.
/// Called on the UI thread, once, on first use.
class NotolithModule {
public:
    virtual ~NotolithModule() = default;
    virtual void attach(ModuleHost &host) = 0;
};

} // namespace noteAppC

#define NotolithModule_iid "io.github.jernejmarcic.Notolith.Module/1"
Q_DECLARE_INTERFACE(noteAppC::NotolithModule, NotolithModule_iid)
```

`libs/modules/ModuleHost.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <functional>
#include <optional>

#include <QList>
#include <QPair>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVariant>
#include <QWidget>

class QTabletEvent;

namespace noteAppC {

class Settings;
class Theme;
class Vault;

/// A tab's widget supplied by a module: what MainWindow::openView needs from
/// a view it cannot name (save, modified, theme), as PdfView and CanvasView
/// each provide under their own names. Its moc is compiled into
/// noteAppC_modules only; a module subclasses it from this header.
class ModuleView : public QWidget {
    Q_OBJECT

public:
    using QWidget::QWidget;
    virtual bool load(const QString &path, QString *error) = 0;
    virtual bool save(QString *error) = 0;
    virtual bool isModified() const = 0;
    virtual void setTheme(const Theme &theme) = 0;
    /// Settings is not a QObject and has no change signal: MainWindow's
    /// settings pass calls this on every module view after a change.
    virtual void applySettings(const Settings &settings) { Q_UNUSED(settings) }

signals:
    void modificationChanged(bool modified);
    /// A link inside the view, resolved by MainWindow::openRelative.
    void noteActivated(const QString &relativePath);
    void statusMessage(const QString &text, int timeoutMs);
};

/// The seams a module attaches to (spec section 6) plus the three
/// registrations (views by suffix, commands, settings rows). Plain
/// std::functions filled by MainWindow; every one is null when the host does
/// not offer it, so a module checks before calling.
struct ModuleHost {
    using ViewFactory = std::function<ModuleView *(const QString &path, QWidget *parent)>;
    using IndexReader = std::function<std::optional<QString>(const QByteArray &bytes)>;
    /// Seam 2: declared for the host struct's shape; wired by Spec 3.
    using TabletHandler = std::function<bool(QTabletEvent *event, const QString &path)>;

    /// A settings row: a toggle when `defaultValue` is a bool, a combo when
    /// `options` is non-empty, a spin box when `maximum > minimum` (a double
    /// spin box with `decimals` places and `step` when `decimals > 0`).
    struct SettingRow {
        QString key;
        QVariant defaultValue;
        QString label;
        QString description;
        QList<QPair<QString, QVariant>> options;
        double minimum = 0;
        double maximum = 0;
        double step = 1;
        int decimals = 0;
    };

    std::function<void(const QStringList &suffixes, ViewFactory factory)> registerView;
    std::function<void(const QString &id, const QString &title, std::function<void()> run)> registerCommand;
    std::function<void(const SettingRow &row)> registerSetting;
    std::function<void(const QStringList &suffixes, IndexReader reader)> registerIndexReader;
    std::function<void(const QStringList &suffixes, TabletHandler handler)> registerTabletHandler;
    /// Shows a view that belongs to no file (the glyph studio) in a new tab.
    std::function<void(ModuleView *view, const QString &title)> showTab;
    std::function<void(const QString &text, int timeoutMs)> showStatus;
    std::function<bool(const QString &absolutePath)> openFile;
    /// The folder of the active note, else the vault root; empty without a vault.
    std::function<QString()> currentFolder;

    Settings *settings = nullptr;
    const Theme *theme = nullptr;
    const Vault *vault = nullptr;
};

} // namespace noteAppC
```

`libs/modules/ModuleRegistry.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <functional>

#include <QHash>
#include <QList>
#include <QObject>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>

class QPluginLoader;

namespace noteAppC {

struct ModuleHost;

/// One module's manifest (Q_PLUGIN_METADATA JSON), read without loading it.
struct ModuleInfo {
    QString id;
    QString featureID;
    QString name;
    QString description;
    QString path; ///< absolute path of the .so
    QString ABItag;
    QStringList suffixes;      ///< lower-case, no dot
    QStringList indexSuffixes; ///< lower-case, no dot: the module is loaded after the scan when the vault holds such files, so its index reader registers
    QStringList dependencies;  ///< module ids
    struct Command {
        QString id;
        QString title;
    };
    QList<Command> commands;
    /// Empty when the module can be loaded; otherwise the notice Settings
    /// shows ("built for another Notolith", "needs module X").
    QString problem;
};

/// Discovers modules (metadata only, any thread), answers which module owns a
/// suffix or a command, and loads a module on first use (UI thread).
class ModuleRegistry : public QObject {
    Q_OBJECT

public:
    explicit ModuleRegistry(QObject *parent = nullptr);
    ~ModuleRegistry() override;

    /// PROJECT_VERSION "-" git hash "-" QT_VERSION_STR: same build only.
    static QString ABItag();
    /// NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS (':'-separated) when set - the tests and the
    /// bench - else $XDG_DATA_HOME/noteAppC/modules then <libdir>/noteAppC/modules.
    static QStringList defaultDirectories();
    using SuffixPredicate = std::function<bool(const QString &suffix)>;
    /// Reads every *.so's manifest with QPluginLoader::metaData(), which does
    /// not load the library. Files without a Notolith manifest are skipped.
    /// A module that claims a suffix `isBuiltInSuffix` accepts gets a
    /// `problem` (the app's own kinds keep their suffixes) and never loads.
    static QList<ModuleInfo> scan(const QStringList &directories, const SuffixPredicate &isBuiltInSuffix = {});

    void setModules(const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules);
    const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules() const { return m_modules; }

    /// The feature switch: MainWindow::featureOn. Without one every module is on.
    void setEnabledCheck(std::function<bool(const QString &featureID)> check);
    bool isEnabled(const ModuleInfo &module) const;

    /// Enabled, loadable modules only: a disabled module owns nothing.
    const ModuleInfo *moduleForSuffix(const QString &path) const;
    const ModuleInfo *moduleForCommand(const QString &commandID) const;

    /// QPluginLoader::instance() then attach(host), once per module id. False
    /// with `error` (the manifest's problem, or the loader's message) when it
    /// cannot. A loaded library stays loaded until the process ends.
    bool ensureLoaded(const ModuleInfo &module, ModuleHost &host, QString *error);
    bool isLoaded(const QString &id) const { return m_loaded.contains(id); }

private:
    QList<ModuleInfo> m_modules;
    QHash<QString, QPluginLoader *> m_loaded;
    std::function<bool(const QString &)> m_enabled;
};

} // namespace noteAppC
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the registry**

`libs/modules/ModuleRegistry.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "ModuleRegistry.h"

#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QJsonArray>
#include <QJsonObject>
#include <QLibrary>
#include <QPluginLoader>
#include <QStandardPaths>
#include <QtGlobal>

#include "ModuleHost.h"
#include "NotolithModule.h"

namespace noteAppC {

ModuleRegistry::ModuleRegistry(QObject *parent) : QObject(parent) {}

ModuleRegistry::~ModuleRegistry() = default; // loaders are parented to this; libraries stay mapped

QString ModuleRegistry::ABItag()
{
    return QStringLiteral(NOTEAPPC_VERSION "-" NOTEAPPC_GIT_HASH "-" QT_VERSION_STR);
}

QStringList ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories()
{
    const QByteArray override = qgetenv("NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS");
    if (!override.isEmpty())
        return QString::fromLocal8Bit(override).split(QLatin1Char(':'), Qt::SkipEmptyParts);
    return {QStandardPaths::writableLocation(QStandardPaths::GenericDataLocation) + QStringLiteral("/noteAppC/modules"),
            QStringLiteral(NOTEAPPC_MODULE_LIBDIR)};
}

QList<ModuleInfo> ModuleRegistry::scan(const QStringList &directories, const SuffixPredicate &isBuiltInSuffix)
{
    QList<ModuleInfo> found;
    for (const QString &directory : directories) {
        const QFileInfoList files = QDir(directory).entryInfoList({QStringLiteral("*.so")}, QDir::Files, QDir::Name);
        for (const QFileInfo &file : files) {
            // metaData() reads the .qtmetadata section from the file; it does not dlopen.
            QPluginLoader loader(file.absoluteFilePath());
            const QJsonObject outer = loader.metaData();
            if (outer.value(QStringLiteral("IID")).toString() != QLatin1String(NotolithModule_iid))
                continue; // not a Notolith module: some other library, or not a plugin at all
            const QJsonObject meta = outer.value(QStringLiteral("MetaData")).toObject();
            ModuleInfo info;
            info.id = meta.value(QStringLiteral("id")).toString();
            if (info.id.isEmpty())
                continue;
            info.featureID = meta.value(QStringLiteral("feature")).toString(info.id);
            info.name = meta.value(QStringLiteral("name")).toString(info.id);
            info.description = meta.value(QStringLiteral("description")).toString();
            info.path = file.absoluteFilePath();
            info.ABItag = meta.value(QStringLiteral("abi")).toString();
            for (const QJsonValue &suffix : meta.value(QStringLiteral("suffixes")).toArray())
                info.suffixes << suffix.toString().toLower();
            for (const QJsonValue &suffix : meta.value(QStringLiteral("indexSuffixes")).toArray())
                info.indexSuffixes << suffix.toString().toLower();
            for (const QJsonValue &dependency : meta.value(QStringLiteral("dependencies")).toArray())
                info.dependencies << dependency.toString();
            for (const QJsonValue &command : meta.value(QStringLiteral("commands")).toArray()) {
                const QJsonObject object = command.toObject();
                info.commands.append({object.value(QStringLiteral("id")).toString(),
                                      object.value(QStringLiteral("title")).toString()});
            }
            if (info.ABItag != ABItag()) {
                info.problem = tr("Module %1 was built for another Notolith (%2; this one is %3) and was not loaded.")
                                   .arg(info.id, info.ABItag, ABItag());
            } else if (isBuiltInSuffix) {
                for (const QString &suffix : std::as_const(info.suffixes)) {
                    if (isBuiltInSuffix(suffix)) {
                        info.problem = tr("Module %1 claims the built-in file type .%2 and was not loaded.").arg(info.id, suffix);
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            found << info;
        }
    }
    QSet<QString> ids;
    for (const ModuleInfo &info : found)
        if (info.problem.isEmpty())
            ids.insert(info.id);
    for (ModuleInfo &info : found) {
        for (const QString &dependency : info.dependencies) {
            if (!ids.contains(dependency) && info.problem.isEmpty())
                info.problem = tr("Module %1 needs module %2, which is not installed, and was not loaded.")
                                   .arg(info.id, dependency);
        }
    }
    return found;
}

void ModuleRegistry::setModules(const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules) { m_modules = modules; }

void ModuleRegistry::setEnabledCheck(std::function<bool(const QString &)> check) { m_enabled = std::move(check); }

bool ModuleRegistry::isEnabled(const ModuleInfo &module) const
{
    return !m_enabled || m_enabled(module.featureID);
}

const ModuleInfo *ModuleRegistry::moduleForSuffix(const QString &path) const
{
    const QString suffix = QFileInfo(path).suffix().toLower();
    if (suffix.isEmpty())
        return nullptr;
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : m_modules)
        if (module.problem.isEmpty() && isEnabled(module) && module.suffixes.contains(suffix))
            return &module;
    return nullptr;
}

const ModuleInfo *ModuleRegistry::moduleForCommand(const QString &commandID) const
{
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : m_modules) {
        if (!module.problem.isEmpty() || !isEnabled(module))
            continue;
        for (const ModuleInfo::Command &command : module.commands)
            if (command.id == commandID)
                return &module;
    }
    return nullptr;
}

bool ModuleRegistry::ensureLoaded(const ModuleInfo &module, ModuleHost &host, QString *error)
{
    if (m_loaded.contains(module.id))
        return true;
    if (!module.problem.isEmpty()) {
        if (error)
            *error = module.problem;
        return false;
    }
    auto *loader = new QPluginLoader(module.path, this);
    // Every symbol now: a module missing one of the host's symbols fails here
    // with its name, instead of crashing the first time that symbol is called.
    loader->setLoadHints(QLibrary::ResolveAllSymbolsHint);
    QObject *instance = loader->instance();
    auto *plugin = instance ? qobject_cast<NotolithModule *>(instance) : nullptr;
    if (!plugin) {
        if (error)
            *error = tr("Module %1 could not be loaded: %2").arg(module.id, loader->errorString());
        delete loader;
        return false;
    }
    m_loaded.insert(module.id, loader);
    plugin->attach(host);
    return true;
}

} // namespace noteAppC
```

```cpp
TEST(SettingsTest, RegisteredDefaultsAndFeaturesAreLiveAndSaved)
{
    Settings::registerDefaults({{QStringLiteral("fake.greeting"), true}});
    Settings::registerFeature(QStringLiteral("fake"), QStringLiteral("Fake module"), QStringLiteral("A test module."));
    EXPECT_EQ(Settings::defaultValue(QStringLiteral("fake.greeting")), QVariant(true));
    EXPECT_EQ(Settings::defaultValue(Settings::featureKey("fake")), QVariant(true));
    bool listed = false;
    for (const Settings::Feature &feature : Settings::features())
        listed = listed || QByteArray(feature.id) == "fake";
    EXPECT_TRUE(listed);
    Settings settings = Settings::load();
    EXPECT_TRUE(settings.boolean(QStringLiteral("fake.greeting"))); // the registered default applies
    settings.setValue(QStringLiteral("fake.greeting"), false);
    QString error;
    ASSERT_TRUE(settings.save(&error)) << error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_FALSE(Settings::load().boolean(QStringLiteral("fake.greeting")));
    // Registering again changes nothing: a second scan is not a second row.
    Settings::registerFeature(QStringLiteral("fake"), QStringLiteral("Fake module"), QStringLiteral("A test module."));
    int count = 0;

        count += QByteArray(feature.id) == "fake";

TEST(SettingsTest, StoredValuesOfKeysRegisteredAfterLoadAreAdopted)
{

    writeFile(Settings::configFile(), QStringLiteral("[late]\ngreeting = false\nwidth = 3.5\n"));

    Settings::registerDefaults({{QStringLiteral("late.greeting"), true}, {QStringLiteral("late.width"), 2.0}});
    EXPECT_TRUE(settings.boolean(QStringLiteral("late.greeting"))) << "still the default until adopted";

    settings.setValue(QStringLiteral("late.greeting"), true);
    settings.adoptStored();
    EXPECT_TRUE(settings.boolean(QStringLiteral("late.greeting"))) << "an in-memory choice is not overwritten";
    QFile::remove(Settings::configFile());
}
```

```cpp
    /// A loadable module's own defaults and feature switch, registered when
    /// the module is discovered (after first paint, on the UI thread). Live:
    /// every Settings reads them from then on, and save() persists them.
    /// Registering a key or a feature twice is a no-op.
    static void registerDefaults(const QVariantMap &defaults);
    static void registerFeature(const QString &id, const QString &name, const QString &description);
    /// Load() ran before the keys of a module existed, so their stored lines
    /// were skipped. This re-reads settings.ini and vaults.ini for the keys
    /// registered since; a key this object already holds keeps its value.
    void adoptStored();
```

`libs/settings/Settings.cpp`: change line 24-26 from `const QVariantMap &defaults() { static const QVariantMap map = [] {` to

```cpp

```
with, in the anonymous namespace near the top of the file:
```cpp
/// Module feature ids are built at runtime; Feature::id is a const char*, so
/// the strings live here for the rest of the process (a deque never moves them).
std::deque<QByteArray> &internedFeatureStrings()
{
    static std::deque<QByteArray> strings;
    return strings;
}
```
and the body of the old `features()` renamed to `static QList<Settings::Feature> &mutableFeatures()` returning `static QList<Feature> list = { ...unchanged entries... }; return list;`. Add `#include <deque>`. Then, after `featureKey` (line 740):
```cpp
void Settings::registerDefaults(const QVariantMap &values)
{
    for (auto it = values.cbegin(); it != values.cend(); ++it)
        if (!defaults().contains(it.key()))
            defaults().insert(it.key(), it.value());
}

void Settings::registerFeature(const QString &id, const QString &name, const QString &description)
{
    for (const Feature &feature : features())
        if (id == QLatin1String(feature.id))
            return;
    auto &strings = internedFeatureStrings();
    strings.push_back(id.toUtf8());
    const char *idChars = strings.back().constData();
    strings.push_back(name.toUtf8());
    const char *nameChars = strings.back().constData();
    strings.push_back(description.toUtf8());
    const char *descriptionChars = strings.back().constData();
    mutableFeatures().append({idChars, nameChars, descriptionChars, false});
    defaults().insert(featureKey(idChars), true); // installing the package is the opt-in
}

void Settings::adoptStored()
{
    const QVariantMap stored = readINI(configFile());
    for (auto it = stored.constBegin(); it != stored.constEnd(); ++it) {
        if (defaults().contains(it.key()) && !m_user.contains(it.key()))
            m_user.insert(it.key(), valueOf(it.value().toString(), defaultValue(it.key())));
    }
    const QVariantMap index = readINI(indexFile());
    for (auto it = index.constBegin(); it != index.constEnd(); ++it) {
        if (defaults().contains(it.key()) && !m_index.contains(it.key()))
            m_index.insert(it.key(), valueOf(it.value().toString(), defaultValue(it.key())));
    }
}
```

```cpp
        return text.toDouble();
```

```cpp
}
```

```cpp
QList<QPair<QString, QString>> MainWindow::moduleProblems() const
{

            problems.append({module.id, module.problem});
```

The scan, after `m_painted = true;` (line 5251), before `syncPlugins();`:
```cpp
    startModuleScan(); // PERFORMANCE.md rule 5: a directory listing, off the UI thread, after the first frame
```
```cpp
void MainWindow::startModuleScan()
{
    if (m_modulesScanned)
        return;
    const QStringList directories = ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories();
    auto *watcher = new QFutureWatcher<QList<ModuleInfo>>(this);
    connect(watcher, &QFutureWatcherBase::finished, this, [this, watcher] {
        if (!m_modulesScanned) { // scanModulesNow() may have run meanwhile
            m_modules->setModules(watcher->result());
            onModulesScanned();
        }
        watcher->deleteLater();
    });
    watcher->setFuture(QtConcurrent::run([directories] { return ModuleRegistry::scan(directories, isBuiltInSuffix); }));
}

void MainWindow::scanModulesNow()
{
    m_modules->setModules(ModuleRegistry::scan(ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories(), isBuiltInSuffix));
    onModulesScanned();
}

void MainWindow::onModulesScanned()
{
    m_modulesScanned = true;
    if (m_modules->modules().isEmpty())
        return; // nothing installed: no feature row, no command, no rebuild - the base app pays nothing (spec section 1, criteria 1-2)
    bool featureRegistered = false;
    bool commandAdded = false;
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : m_modules->modules()) {
        if (!module.problem.isEmpty()) {
            qWarning("%s", qPrintable(module.problem));
            continue;
        }
        Settings::registerFeature(module.featureID, module.name, module.description);
        featureRegistered = true;
        for (const ModuleInfo::Command &moduleCommand : module.commands) {
            if (!command(moduleCommand.id)) { // a rescan does not duplicate
                addModuleCommand(moduleCommand.id, moduleCommand.title);
                commandAdded = true;
            }
        }
    }
    m_settings.adoptStored(); // a saved feature switch for a key registered just now (load() ran before the scan)
    m_settingsPage->setModules(m_moduleSettingRows, moduleProblems());
    if (commandAdded)
        rebuildHotkeyCommands(); // extracted from the command builder: everything from `QList<SettingsPage::HotkeyCommand> rebindable;` (about MainWindow.cpp:3440) through the closing `applyHotkeys();`
    if (featureRegistered)
        applyFeatures(); // greys a disabled module's commands by their id prefix, as for every feature
    loadIndexModules();
}

void MainWindow::loadIndexModules()
{
    if (!m_modulesScanned || !m_vault.isValid())
        return;
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : m_modules->modules()) {
        if (module.indexSuffixes.isEmpty() || !module.problem.isEmpty() || !m_modules->isEnabled(module)
            || m_modules->isLoaded(module.id) || !m_index.hasFileWithSuffix(module.indexSuffixes))
            continue;
        // Indexing counts as use: load it (UI thread, queued behind whatever is painting) so its index
        // reader registers and the indexer re-reads those files. A vault without such files pays nothing.
        QTimer::singleShot(0, this, [this, id = module.id] {
            for (const ModuleInfo &candidate : m_modules->modules()) {
                QString error;
                if (candidate.id == id && !m_modules->ensureLoaded(candidate, m_moduleHost, &error))
                    qWarning("%s", qPrintable(error));
            }
        });
    }
}

QAction *MainWindow::addModuleCommand(const QString &id, const QString &title)
{
    auto *action = new QAction(title, this);
    action->setObjectName(id);
    action->setData(QString());
    action->setShortcutContext(Qt::WindowShortcut);
    connect(action, &QAction::triggered, this, [this, id] {
        const ModuleInfo *module = m_modules->moduleForCommand(id);
        if (!module)
            return;
        QString error;
        if (!m_modules->ensureLoaded(*module, m_moduleHost, &error)) {
            statusBar()->showMessage(error, 5000);
            return;
        }
        if (const auto run = m_moduleCommands.value(id))
            run();
    });
    addAction(action);
    m_commands << action;
    return action;
}
```
`command(id)` is the public lookup over `m_commands` by `objectName()` defined above (the window had no such member; the test-file helper `command(MainWindow &, const char *)` is a free function). Call `loadIndexModules()` also from the `Indexer::finished` handler (MainWindow.cpp:436), as its last statement: the first index pass usually lands after the scan, and this is when the vault's files are known. At the top of `MainWindow.cpp`'s anonymous namespace, next to `mediaKindOf` (line 164), add the predicate the scan uses to refuse a module claiming a suffix the app opens itself:
```cpp
bool isBuiltInSuffix(const QString &suffix)
{
    static const QStringList own = {QStringLiteral("md"),     QStringLiteral("mdlock"), QStringLiteral("epub"),
                                    QStringLiteral("canvas"), QStringLiteral("base"),   QStringLiteral("pdf"),
                                    QStringLiteral("ipynb")};
    return own.contains(suffix) || !mediaKindOf(QStringLiteral("x.") + suffix).isEmpty();
}
```
and in `libs/index/Index.h` (beside `attachmentCount()`) / `libs/index/Index.cpp`:
```cpp
    /// True when the index holds any file - note or attachment - ending in one of `suffixes` (lower-case, no dot).
    bool hasFileWithSuffix(const QStringList &suffixes) const;
```
```cpp
bool Index::hasFileWithSuffix(const QStringList &suffixes) const
{
    QSqlQuery query(m_db);
    query.prepare(QStringLiteral("SELECT 1 FROM files WHERE path LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' LIMIT 1")); // LIKE is case-insensitive for ASCII
    for (const QString &suffix : suffixes) {
        QString escaped = suffix;
        escaped.replace(QLatin1Char('\\'), QStringLiteral("\\\\")).replace(QLatin1Char('%'), QStringLiteral("\\%")).replace(QLatin1Char('_'), QStringLiteral("\\_"));
        query.bindValue(0, QStringLiteral("%.") + escaped);
        if (query.exec() && query.next())
            return true;
    }
    return false;
}
```

Seam 1. A module's suffix is consulted after every built-in kind, never before (spec section 6, seam 1; the registry already refused a module that claims a built-in suffix). In `isViewFile` (line 6293) replace the two last lines (`const QString media = ...; return media == ...;`) with:
```cpp

    if (media == QLatin1String("image") || (!media.isEmpty() && featureOn("media-embeds")))
        return true;
    return m_modules && m_modules->moduleForSuffix(path) != nullptr;
```
In `openView`, before `if (!view) {` (line 6411):
```cpp
    if (!view)
        openModuleView(path, &error, &view);
```
```cpp
bool MainWindow::openModuleView(const QString &path, QString *error, QWidget **view)
{
    const ModuleInfo *module = m_modules ? m_modules->moduleForSuffix(path) : nullptr;
    if (!module)
        return false;
    if (!m_modules->ensureLoaded(*module, m_moduleHost, error))
        return false;
    const ModuleHost::ViewFactory factory = m_moduleViews.value(QFileInfo(path).suffix().toLower());
    if (!factory) {
        *error = tr("Module %1 registered no view for %2").arg(module->id, path);
        return false;
    }
    ModuleView *moduleView = factory(path, m_active->area);
    if (!moduleView)
        return false;
    moduleView->setTheme(m_theme);
    if (!moduleView->load(path, error)) {
        delete moduleView;
        return false;
    }
    Leaf *leaf = m_active;
    connect(moduleView, &ModuleView::modificationChanged, this, [this, leaf, moduleView](bool modified) {
        if (leaf == m_active)
            updateTitle();
        else
            syncTabs(leaf->group);
        if (modified && m_settings.boolean(QStringLiteral("files.autosave")))
            armAutosave(); // an ink note is kept like a note
    });
    connect(moduleView, &ModuleView::noteActivated, this, &MainWindow::openRelative);
    connect(moduleView, &ModuleView::statusMessage, this,
            [this](const QString &text, int timeout) { statusBar()->showMessage(text, timeout); });
    *view = moduleView;
    return true;
}

void MainWindow::showModuleTab(ModuleView *view, const QString &title)
{
    newTab();
    closeView(m_active);
    m_active->note = Note{};
    m_active->editor->setNoteText(QString());
    m_active->editor->setEnabled(false);
    m_active->editor->hide();
    m_active->inlineTitle->hide();
    view->setParent(m_active->area);
    view->setTheme(m_theme);
    m_active->view = view;
    m_active->viewTitle = title;
    static_cast<QVBoxLayout *>(m_active->area->layout())->addWidget(view, 1);
    view->setFocus();
    syncTabs(m_active->group);
    updateTitle();
}
```

### Task 2b: Review fixes to Tasks 1-2

Source: the two reviews of 2026-10-22 and rulings R1, R2 and R7 (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-21-ink-core/assessment-rulings.md`, "Fix batch on existing code"). This task changes code that Tasks 1 and 2 already committed (`45e0071`, `7ca8424`) and keeps the A0 scope: no ink code. Where the Task 1 and Task 2 text above differs from this task, this task and the code win.

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/modules/ModuleABI.h.in`, `tests/fakemodule/NotAModule.cpp`
- Modify: `CMakeLists.txt` (root, the ABI tag block lines 24-34), `libs/modules/CMakeLists.txt`, `libs/modules/ModuleRegistry.h`, `libs/modules/ModuleRegistry.cpp`, `libs/modules/NotolithModule.h`
- Modify: `libs/index/Index.h` (line 121-122), `libs/index/Index.cpp` (lines 678-690): delete `hasFileWithSuffix`
- Modify: `libs/settings/Settings.h` (comment only)
- Modify: `libs/ui/MainWindow.h` (line 641 and the `moduleProblems` neighbourhood), `libs/ui/MainWindow.cpp` (`isBuiltInSuffix` 179-186; module host wiring 319-346; `Indexer::finished` handler 484; `startModuleScan` 5312; `onModulesScanned` 5340-5363; `loadIndexModules` 5365-5388; `addModuleCommand` 5390; `moduleProblems` 5408; `isViewFile` 6462; `openModuleView` 6465)
- Modify: `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (fake modules, ctest entries, the isolated-home block), `tests/fakemodule/FakeModule.cpp`, `tests/fakemodule/manifest.json.in`, `tests/test_modules.cpp`, `tests/test_window.cpp` (module tests, lines 7622-7747), `tests/test_settings.cpp` (two suite names, lines 564 and 588)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `ModuleRegistry`, `ModuleInfo`, `NotolithModule`; Task 2's `MainWindow` module wiring.
- Produces:
  - `ModuleInfo` loses `indexSuffixes` and `dependencies`; the manifest loses the keys `indexSuffixes` and `dependencies`. Nothing replaces them: indexing never loads a module (R1) and package dependencies belong to the package manager (R2).
  - `std::optional<ModuleInfo> ModuleRegistry::moduleForSuffix(const QString &path) const;` and `moduleForCommand(const QString &commandID) const` return a copy, not a pointer into `m_modules`.
  - `QString ModuleRegistry::loadFailure(const QString &id) const;` the recorded message of a failed `ensureLoaded` (empty if none). A second `ensureLoaded` of a failed module returns the same message without retrying.
  - `ModuleRegistry::scan` gives a module a `problem` when: its manifest has no id (`"<file>: manifest has no id; not loaded."`, the id shown is the file's base name), or it claims a suffix a module before it already claims (`"Module %1 claims .%2, which module %3 already claims, and was not loaded."`, first problem-free claimant wins, scan order is directory order then file name). The dependency pass is gone.
  - `ModuleRegistry::ABItag()` is `NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG` from the generated `ModuleABI.h`: `PROJECT_VERSION "-" <16 hex chars, random once per build directory> "-" Qt6_VERSION`.
  - `ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories()` is `{<libdir>/noteAppC/modules}` unless `NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS` is set.
  - `bool MainWindow::loadModule(const ModuleInfo &module, QString *error)` (private): `ensureLoaded`, then once after a fresh attach `m_settings.adoptStored()` and a Settings page refresh; on failure it refreshes the page too, so the notice appears.
- Fix items, each with its file and function (the steps below carry the code):

| Item | File and function |
|---|---|
| M1 gate isolation | `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (`modules_gate`, `modules`, `settings_modules`, `settings` entries); `tests/test_modules.cpp` (`ModuleGateTest`, `EnvGuard`); `tests/test_window.cpp` (`AModuleCommandLoadsTheModuleOnFirstUse` assertion, `qWait(100)`); `tests/test_settings.cpp` (suite `SettingsModuleTest`) |
| M2 built-in suffixes | `MainWindow.cpp`, `isBuiltInSuffix` |
| M3 first claim wins | `ModuleRegistry.cpp`, `scan`; `MainWindow.cpp`, the `registerView` lambda |
| M4 popout commands | `MainWindow.cpp`, `addModuleCommand` |
| M5 one adoptStored | `MainWindow.cpp`, `loadModule` and the `registerSetting` lambda |
