Replace the whole of `tests/test_modules.cpp` with:

```cpp
// The loadable-module tier (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md
// section 4): discovery by metadata does not load the library; a module loads
// on first use; a wrong ABI tag, a duplicate suffix claim, a manifest without
// an id, a library that is not a module and an unresolvable library are each
// reported, never loaded or dropped silently.
#include <gtest/gtest.h>

#include <link.h>

#include <QApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QStandardPaths>
#include <QTemporaryDir>

#include "ModuleHost.h"
#include "ModuleRegistry.h"

using namespace noteAppC;

namespace {

/// Every shared object mapped into this process, by absolute path.
QStringList loadedObjects()
{
    QStringList out;
    dl_iterate_phdr(
        [](dl_phdr_info *info, size_t, void *data) {
            if (info->dlpi_name && *info->dlpi_name)
                static_cast<QStringList *>(data)->append(QFileInfo(QString::fromLocal8Bit(info->dlpi_name)).canonicalFilePath());
            return 0;
        },
        &out);
    return out;
}

QString fakeDir() { return QStringLiteral(NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULES_DIR); }

const ModuleInfo *byID(const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules, const char *id)
{
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : modules)
        if (module.id == QLatin1String(id))
            return &module;
    return nullptr;
}

/// Sets an environment variable for one scope and puts the previous state back
/// after it: a mark or a module directory must not leak into the next test.
class EnvGuard {
public:
    EnvGuard(const char *name, const QByteArray &value)
        : m_name(name), m_hadPrevious(qEnvironmentVariableIsSet(name)), m_previous(qgetenv(name))
    {
        qputenv(name, value);
    }
    ~EnvGuard()
    {
        if (m_hadPrevious)
            qputenv(m_name, m_previous);
        else
            qunsetenv(m_name);
    }
    EnvGuard(const EnvGuard &) = delete;
    EnvGuard &operator=(const EnvGuard &) = delete;

private:
    const char *m_name;
    bool m_hadPrevious;
    QByteArray m_previous;
};

/// A registry over `modules` with every feature on (a QObject: it cannot be returned by value).
void useModules(ModuleRegistry &registry, const QList<ModuleInfo> &modules)
{
    registry.setModules(modules);
    registry.setEnabledCheck([](const QString &) { return true; });
}

} // namespace

// Its own ctest entry (`modules_gate`, tests/CMakeLists.txt): no other test in the
// process may have mapped fake_good first, or "the scan did not load it" proves nothing.
TEST(ModuleGateTest, ScanReadsManifestsWithoutLoadingTheLibrary)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    const QString mark = tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("mark"));
    EnvGuard markGuard("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULE_MARK", mark.toLocal8Bit());
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *good = byID(modules, "fake");
    ASSERT_NE(good, nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(good->suffixes, QStringList{QStringLiteral("fake")});
    ASSERT_EQ(good->commands.size(), 2);
    EXPECT_EQ(good->commands.first().id, QStringLiteral("fake:hello"));
    EXPECT_EQ(good->ABItag, ModuleRegistry::ABItag());
    EXPECT_TRUE(good->problem.isEmpty()) << good->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_FALSE(QFile::exists(mark)) << "the static initializer ran: metaData() loaded the library";
    EXPECT_FALSE(loadedObjects().contains(QFileInfo(good->path).canonicalFilePath()));
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, StrayFilesInTheDirectoryAreSkippedSilently)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    ASSERT_TRUE(QFile(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("README"))).open(QIODevice::WriteOnly));
    ASSERT_TRUE(QFile(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("libfoo.so.1"))).open(QIODevice::WriteOnly));
    QFile notAPlugin(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("junk.so")));
    ASSERT_TRUE(notAPlugin.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly));
    notAPlugin.write("not an ELF file");
    notAPlugin.close();
    EXPECT_TRUE(ModuleRegistry::scan({tmp.path()}).isEmpty());
    EXPECT_TRUE(ModuleRegistry::scan({tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("missing"))}).isEmpty());
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, LoadsOnFirstUseAndAttachesOnce)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    const QString mark = tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("mark"));
    EnvGuard markGuard("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_MODULE_MARK", mark.toLocal8Bit());
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}));
    const std::optional<ModuleInfo> good = registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("/v/a.fake"));
    ASSERT_TRUE(good.has_value());
    EXPECT_EQ(good->id, QStringLiteral("fake"));
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(good->id));

    int views = 0, commands = 0, settings = 0;  // the fake registers 2 commands and 2 setting rows
    ModuleHost host;
    host.registerView = [&](const QStringList &suffixes, ModuleHost::ViewFactory) { views += suffixes.size(); };
    host.registerCommand = [&](const QString &, const QString &, std::function<void()>) { ++commands; };
    host.registerSetting = [&](const ModuleHost::SettingRow &) { ++settings; };
    QString error;
    ASSERT_TRUE(registry.ensureLoaded(*good, host, &error)) << error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(registry.isLoaded(good->id));
    EXPECT_TRUE(QFile::exists(mark));
    EXPECT_TRUE(loadedObjects().contains(QFileInfo(good->path).canonicalFilePath()));
    EXPECT_EQ(views, 1);
    EXPECT_EQ(commands, 2);
    EXPECT_EQ(settings, 2);
    ASSERT_TRUE(registry.ensureLoaded(*good, host, &error)); // a second call attaches nothing again
    EXPECT_EQ(views, 1);
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, WrongABItagIsRefusedWithANoticeNamingTheModule)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *bad = byID(modules, "fake-badabi");
    ASSERT_NE(bad, nullptr);
    EXPECT_FALSE(bad->problem.isEmpty());
    EXPECT_TRUE(bad->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("fake-badabi")));
    EXPECT_TRUE(bad->problem.contains(ModuleRegistry::ABItag()));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, modules);
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*bad, host, &error));
    EXPECT_EQ(error, bad->problem);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(bad->id));
}

// M3: fake_badabi sorts before fake_good and claims the same suffix, but it has a
// problem, so it never owns the suffix; fake_good is the first problem-free claimant
// and fake_twin, which comes later, is refused.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, ASecondModuleClaimingAClaimedSuffixIsRefusedAndTheFirstKeepsIt)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *first = byID(modules, "fake");
    const ModuleInfo *twin = byID(modules, "fake-twin");
    ASSERT_NE(first, nullptr);
    ASSERT_NE(twin, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(first->problem.isEmpty()) << first->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(twin->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("claims .fake"))) << twin->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(twin->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("already claims")));
    EXPECT_TRUE(twin->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("fake-twin")));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, modules);
    const std::optional<ModuleInfo> owner = registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fake"));
    ASSERT_TRUE(owner.has_value());
    EXPECT_EQ(owner->id, QStringLiteral("fake"));
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*twin, host, &error));
    EXPECT_EQ(error, twin->problem);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(twin->id));
}

// M6: a manifest with an empty id is listed, named by its file, not dropped silently.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, AManifestWithoutAnIDIsReportedAndNeverLoaded)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules = ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()});
    const ModuleInfo *noID = byID(modules, "fake_noid"); // the id shown is the file's base name
    ASSERT_NE(noID, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(noID->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("manifest has no id"))) << noID->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(noID->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("fake_noid.so")));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, modules);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fakenoid")).has_value());
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*noID, host, &error));
    EXPECT_EQ(error, noID->problem);
}

// M6: a Qt plugin with the right IID that does not implement NotolithModule.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, ALibraryThatIsNotAModuleIsReportedAndStaysUnattached)
{
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}));
    const std::optional<ModuleInfo> module = registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fakenotmod"));
    ASSERT_TRUE(module.has_value());
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*module, host, &error));
    EXPECT_TRUE(error.contains(QStringLiteral("not a Notolith module"))) << error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(error.contains(QStringLiteral("fake-notmodule")));
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(module->id));
    EXPECT_EQ(registry.loadFailure(module->id), error);
}

// R2: no dependency check. A module whose library cannot resolve a symbol (an absent
// inkcore, in life) fails with the loader's message, which Settings shows.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, AnUnresolvedSymbolFailsTheLoadWithTheLoadersMessage)
{
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}));
    const std::optional<ModuleInfo> module = registry.moduleForCommand(QStringLiteral("fake-unresolved:hello"));
    ASSERT_TRUE(module.has_value());
    EXPECT_TRUE(module->problem.isEmpty()) << "nothing is wrong until the library is opened";
    EXPECT_TRUE(registry.loadFailure(module->id).isEmpty());
    ModuleHost host;
    QString error;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*module, host, &error));
    EXPECT_TRUE(error.contains(QStringLiteral("noteappcFakeMissingSymbol"))) << error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_EQ(registry.loadFailure(module->id), error);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.isLoaded(module->id));
    QString again;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.ensureLoaded(*module, host, &again));
    EXPECT_EQ(again, error) << "a failed library is not retried";
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, AModuleMayNotClaimASuffixTheAppOpensItself)
{
    const QList<ModuleInfo> modules =
        ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}, [](const QString &suffix) { return suffix == QLatin1String("fake"); });
    const ModuleInfo *claimant = byID(modules, "fake");
    ASSERT_NE(claimant, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(claimant->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("built-in file type .fake"))) << claimant->problem.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(claimant->problem.contains(QStringLiteral("fake")));
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    useModules(registry, modules);
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fake")).has_value()) << "the built-in kind keeps the suffix";
    const ModuleInfo *other = byID(modules, "fake-notmodule");
    ASSERT_NE(other, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(other->problem.isEmpty()) << "a module with other suffixes is not affected";
}

TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, DisabledFeatureUnregistersTheSuffixAndCommand)
{
    ModuleRegistry registry;
    registry.setModules(ModuleRegistry::scan({fakeDir()}));
    bool on = true;
    registry.setEnabledCheck([&on](const QString &featureID) { return featureID != QLatin1String("fake") || on; });
    EXPECT_TRUE(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.FAKE")).has_value());
    EXPECT_TRUE(registry.moduleForCommand(QStringLiteral("fake:hello")).has_value());
    on = false;
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.moduleForSuffix(QStringLiteral("a.fake")).has_value());
    EXPECT_FALSE(registry.moduleForCommand(QStringLiteral("fake:hello")).has_value());
}

// R7: the tag is version, a random token made once per build directory, and the Qt version.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, ABItagIsVersionTokenAndQtVersion)
{
    const QStringList parts = ModuleRegistry::ABItag().split(QLatin1Char('-'));
    ASSERT_EQ(parts.size(), 3) << ModuleRegistry::ABItag().toStdString();
    EXPECT_FALSE(parts[0].isEmpty());
    EXPECT_TRUE(QRegularExpression(QStringLiteral("^[0-9a-f]{16}$")).match(parts[1]).hasMatch()) << parts[1].toStdString();
    EXPECT_EQ(parts[2], QStringLiteral(QT_VERSION_STR));
}

// M10: no per-user module directory.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, DefaultDirectoriesAreTheOverrideOrTheLibdirAlone)
{
    {
        EnvGuard guard("NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS", "/tmp/a:/tmp/b");
        EXPECT_EQ(ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories(), (QStringList{QStringLiteral("/tmp/a"), QStringLiteral("/tmp/b")}));
    }
    EnvGuard unset("NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS", QByteArray());
    const QStringList dirs = ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories();
    ASSERT_EQ(dirs.size(), 1);
    EXPECT_TRUE(dirs.first().endsWith(QStringLiteral("/noteAppC/modules")));
    EXPECT_FALSE(dirs.first().startsWith(QStandardPaths::writableLocation(QStandardPaths::GenericDataLocation)))
        << "$XDG_DATA_HOME is not searched";
}

// M8: ctest hands every test an empty module directory unless it sets its own, so
// what is installed on the machine never reaches a test.
TEST(ModuleRegistryTest, TheTestEnvironmentPointsTheRegistryAtAnEmptyDirectory)
{
    ASSERT_TRUE(qEnvironmentVariableIsSet("NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS")) << "run through ctest";
    EXPECT_TRUE(ModuleRegistry::scan(ModuleRegistry::defaultDirectories()).isEmpty());
}

}
```

```cpp
// R1: indexing never counts as use. A vault file with a module's suffix leaves the module
// unloaded until the file is opened. (No fixed wait: the index pass is awaited, then the
// absence is asserted.)
TEST_F(WindowTest, AVaultFileOfAModuleSuffixDoesNotLoadTheModuleUntilItIsOpened)
{
    write(QStringLiteral("notes.fake"), "anything");
    MainWindow window;
    window.show();
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.scanModulesNow();
    WAIT_TRUE(window.index()->attachmentCount() >= 1, 5000); // the first index pass has landed
    EXPECT_FALSE(window.modules()->isLoaded(QStringLiteral("fake")));
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.openNote(path(QStringLiteral("notes.fake"))));
    EXPECT_TRUE(window.modules()->isLoaded(QStringLiteral("fake")));
}

// M4: a command the scan adds reaches a pop-out window that already exists. The main window
// is not shown, so no first paint starts the automatic scan before the explicit one.
TEST_F(WindowTest, AModuleCommandAppearsInAnAlreadyOpenPopoutWindow)
{
    MainWindow window;
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.openNote(path(QStringLiteral("Welcome.md")));
    command(window, "workspace:open-in-new-window")->trigger();
    const auto popouts = popoutWindows();
    ASSERT_EQ(popouts.size(), 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(command(window, "fake:hello"), nullptr) << "the scan has not run yet";
    window.scanModulesNow();
    QAction *hello = command(window, "fake:hello");
    ASSERT_NE(hello, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(popouts.first()->actions().contains(hello));
}

// M5: a stored value of a module's setting key is adopted once the module has loaded (the
// file was read when the window was made, before the key existed). This is a guard: the
// behaviour already held; Task 2b only reduces the adoptStored calls from one per row to one.
TEST_F(WindowTest, AStoredValueOfAModuleSettingIsAdoptedWhenTheModuleLoads)
{
    const QString configPath = noteAppC::Settings::configFile();
    auto cleanup = qScopeGuard([configPath] { QFile::remove(configPath); }); // declared first: destroyed after the window
    QDir().mkpath(QFileInfo(configPath).absolutePath());
    QFile config(configPath);
    ASSERT_TRUE(config.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate));
    config.write("[fake]\nwidth = 7.5\n");
    config.close();
    MainWindow window;
    window.show();
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.scanModulesNow();
    command(window, "fake:hello")->trigger();
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(window.settings().value(QStringLiteral("fake.width")).toDouble(), 7.5);
}

        ASSERT_TRUE(toggle->isChecked());

}
```

```cpp
// A Qt plugin that carries a Notolith manifest and the Notolith IID but does not
// implement NotolithModule: ModuleRegistry::ensureLoaded must say so (M6).
#include <QObject>
#include <QtPlugin>

#include "NotolithModule.h"

class NotAModule : public QObject {
    Q_OBJECT
    Q_PLUGIN_METADATA(IID NotolithModule_iid FILE "manifest.json")
};

#include "NotAModule.moc"
```

In `tests/fakemodule/FakeModule.cpp`, add the unresolved-symbol hook. After `#include "NotolithModule.h"`:

```cpp
// Defined nowhere. A module built with FAKE_UNRESOLVED therefore cannot be opened
// with QLibrary::ResolveAllSymbolsHint (RTLD_NOW): dlopen fails with this name in
// the message, which is what a module whose libnotolith-inkcore.so is absent gets.

#endif
```

In `tests/CMakeLists.txt` replace the whole "loadable-module tier" block (from its comment line `# The loadable-module tier (libs/modules): four fake modules...` through `set_tests_properties(modules PROPERTIES ENVIRONMENT "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen")`) with:

```cmake
# The loadable-module tier (libs/modules): fake modules built from one or two
# sources, differing in their configure_file'd manifest and a compile flag - a
# good one, one built for another Notolith (wrong ABI tag), a twin claiming the
# same suffix, one whose manifest has no id, a Qt plugin that is not a Notolith
# module, and one whose library cannot resolve a symbol. Headers only from the
# host: no host static library is linked.
function(noteappc_fake_module target id abi suffix source)
    set(FAKE_ID "${id}")
    set(FAKE_ABI "${abi}")
    set(FAKE_SUFFIX "${suffix}")
    configure_file(fakemodule/manifest.json.in ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${target}/manifest.json @ONLY)
    add_library(${target} MODULE fakemodule/${source})
    target_include_directories(${target} PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${target})
    target_compile_definitions(${target} PRIVATE FAKE_ID="${id}" FAKE_SUFFIX="${suffix}" ${ARGN})
    target_link_libraries(${target} PRIVATE noteAppC_modules_headers Qt6::Widgets)
    set_target_properties(${target} PROPERTIES AUTOMOC ON PREFIX ""
        LIBRARY_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/fakemodules)
endfunction()
noteappc_fake_module(fake_good fake "${NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG}" fake FakeModule.cpp)
```

```cpp
std::optional<ModuleInfo> ModuleRegistry::moduleForSuffix(const QString &path) const
{
    const QString suffix = QFileInfo(path).suffix().toLower();
    if (suffix.isEmpty())
        return std::nullopt;
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : m_modules)
        if (module.problem.isEmpty() && isEnabled(module) && module.suffixes.contains(suffix))
            return module;
    return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<ModuleInfo> ModuleRegistry::moduleForCommand(const QString &commandID) const
{
    for (const ModuleInfo &module : m_modules) {
        if (!module.problem.isEmpty() || !isEnabled(module))
            continue;
        for (const ModuleInfo::Command &command : module.commands)
            if (command.id == commandID)
                return module;
    }
    return std::nullopt;
}
```

Then, replacing the deleted dependency pass, directly before `return found;`:

```cpp
    // First claim wins (M3): the first module without a problem to claim a suffix owns it, in
    // scan order (directories as given, files by name). A later claimant is listed with a
    // problem and never loads, so the registry and the window's view map cannot disagree.
    QHash<QString, QString> owner; // suffix -> id
    for (ModuleInfo &info : found) {
        if (!info.problem.isEmpty())
            continue;
        for (const QString &suffix : std::as_const(info.suffixes)) {
            const auto taken = owner.constFind(suffix);
            if (taken != owner.cend()) {
                info.problem = tr("Module %1 claims .%2, which module %3 already claims, and was not loaded.")
                                   .arg(info.id, suffix, *taken);
                break;
            }
        }
        if (info.problem.isEmpty())
            for (const QString &suffix : std::as_const(info.suffixes))
                owner.insert(suffix, info.id);
    }
```

`ensureLoaded` (M6, R2):

```cpp
bool ModuleRegistry::ensureLoaded(const ModuleInfo &module, ModuleHost &host, QString *error)
{
    if (m_loaded.contains(module.id))
        return true;
    if (!module.problem.isEmpty()) {
        if (error)
            *error = module.problem;
        return false;
    }
    if (const auto failed = m_failed.constFind(module.id); failed != m_failed.cend()) {
        if (error)
            *error = *failed; // a library that failed to open fails the same way again: not retried
        return false;
    }
    auto *loader = new QPluginLoader(module.path, this);
    // Every symbol now: a module missing one of the host's symbols - or libnotolith-inkcore.so
    // itself - fails here with its name, instead of crashing the first time it is called.
    loader->setLoadHints(QLibrary::ResolveAllSymbolsHint);
    QObject *instance = loader->instance();
    auto *plugin = instance ? qobject_cast<NotolithModule *>(instance) : nullptr;
    if (!plugin) {
        const QString message = instance ? tr("Module %1 is not a Notolith module.").arg(module.id)
                                         : tr("Module %1 could not be loaded: %2").arg(module.id, loader->errorString());
        m_failed.insert(module.id, message);
        if (error)
            *error = message;
        delete loader;
        return false;
    }
    m_loaded.insert(module.id, loader);
    plugin->attach(host);
    return true;
}
```
Fix the comment above `defaultDirectories` in the header: `/// NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS (':'-separated) when set - the tests and the bench - else <libdir>/noteAppC/modules alone.`

M2: replace `isBuiltInSuffix` (lines 179-186) with a version that follows the build options exactly as `isViewFile` does:

```cpp
/// A suffix the app opens itself: a module may not claim it (the registry gives
/// such a module a problem notice and never loads it). Per kind, under the same
/// compile-time option as isViewFile, so a build without EPUB or PDF does not
/// forbid a module those suffixes.
bool isBuiltInSuffix(const QString &suffix)
{
    if (suffix == QLatin1String("md") || suffix == QLatin1String("mdlock"))
        return true;
#ifdef NOTEAPPC_HAVE_EPUB
    if (suffix == QLatin1String("epub"))
        return true;
#endif
#ifdef NOTEAPPC_HAVE_CANVAS
    if (suffix == QLatin1String("canvas"))
        return true;
#endif
#ifdef NOTEAPPC_HAVE_BASES
    if (suffix == QLatin1String("base"))
        return true;
#endif
#ifdef NOTEAPPC_HAVE_PDFVIEW
    if (suffix == QLatin1String("pdf"))
        return true;
#endif
#ifdef NOTEAPPC_HAVE_NOTEBOOK
    if (suffix == QLatin1String("ipynb"))
        return true;
#endif
    return !mediaKindOf(QStringLiteral("x.") + suffix).isEmpty();
}
```

```cpp
bool MainWindow::loadModule(const ModuleInfo &module, QString *error)
{
    if (m_modules->isLoaded(module.id))
        return true; // attached already: nothing new to pick up
    const bool ok = m_modules->ensureLoaded(module, m_moduleHost, error);
    if (ok)
        m_settings.adoptStored(); // the module's keys exist now; load() ran before them (once, not per row)
    m_settingsPage->setModules(m_moduleSettingRows, moduleProblems()); // its rows, or its load failure
    return ok;
}
```

```cpp
QList<QPair<QString, QString>> MainWindow::moduleProblems() const
```

```cpp
/// What a loadable module implements (spec section 4). One method: the
/// module registers its views, commands and settings rows with the host.
/// Called on the UI thread, once per registry, on first use. A process can hold
/// several windows, each with its own registry and ModuleHost, but the library is
/// mapped once and QPluginLoader hands every registry the same object: a real
/// module keeps no state of its own, or keeps it per host (in the lambdas and
/// views it registers), because a second window's attach() runs on the object
/// the first window's attach() ran on.
```

```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
enum class PressureCurve { Const, Linear, Sqrt, Cbrt, Pow2, Pow3 };
QString pressureCurveName(PressureCurve curve);
PressureCurve pressureCurveFromName(const QString &name, bool *ok = nullptr);
double applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve curve, double width, double pressure); // pressure clamped to 0..1

QString layerName(Layer layer);
Layer layerFromName(const QString &name);
struct StrokeStyle { double width = 2.0; QColor color{0x1e, 0x1e, 0x1e}; PressureCurve pressureCurve = PressureCurve::Linear; };
struct StrokePoint { double x = 0; double y = 0; double pressure = 0.5; };
struct Stroke { StrokeStyle style; std::vector<StrokePoint> points; QRectF bounds() const; double widthAt(size_t index) const; };
enum class ShapeKind { Line, Arrow, Rect, Ellipse, Polyline, Polygon };

    qint64 addItem(Item item);                               // assigns t, returns it
    bool removeItem(qint64 t);
    bool replaceItem(qint64 t, Item item);                   // keeps t
    const Item *item(qint64 t) const;
    std::vector<const Item *> paintOrder(const QRectF &area) const; // intersecting `area`, ascending (layer, t)
    std::vector<qint64> itemsIntersecting(const QRectF &area) const;
    QRectF contentBounds() const;
    void commit(); bool undo(); bool redo(); bool canUndo() const; bool canRedo() const;
    quint64 revision() const; void markSaved(); bool isModified() const;
    int pageCount() const; QRectF pageRect(int index) const;
    bool growToContain(const QRectF &bounds);                // ContinuousVertical only in A0; true when the extent changed
    void addPage();
    void reset(DocumentSettings settings, std::vector<Item> items, QJsonObject extraTop); // a load: history cleared
};
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing model tests**

`tests/test_ink.cpp`:
```cpp
// The ink core (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md section 7
// and 12): model and history, the .ink format, the outline composer, input,
// the canvas. Pure functions and widgets on the offscreen platform.
#include <gtest/gtest.h>

#include <QApplication>

#include "InkDocument.h"
#include "InkTypes.h"
#include "SpatialGrid.h"

using namespace noteAppC::ink;

namespace {

std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> line(double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1, double width = 2.0)
{
    auto stroke = std::make_shared<Stroke>();
    stroke->style.width = width;
    stroke->points = {{x0, y0, 0.5}, {x1, y1, 0.5}};
    return stroke;
}

} // namespace

TEST(InkTypesTest, PressureCurvesMatchRnote)
{
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Const, 4.0, 0.25), 4.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Linear, 4.0, 0.25), 1.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Sqrt, 4.0, 0.25), 2.0);
    EXPECT_NEAR(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Cbrt, 8.0, 0.125), 4.0, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Pow2, 4.0, 0.5), 1.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Pow3, 8.0, 0.5), 1.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Linear, 4.0, 7.0), 4.0) << "pressure above 1 clamps";
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve::Linear, 4.0, -1.0), 0.0) << "pressure below 0 clamps";
    for (PressureCurve curve : {PressureCurve::Const, PressureCurve::Linear, PressureCurve::Sqrt, PressureCurve::Cbrt,
                                PressureCurve::Pow2, PressureCurve::Pow3})
        EXPECT_EQ(pressureCurveFromName(pressureCurveName(curve)), curve);
    bool ok = true;
    pressureCurveFromName(QStringLiteral("wobbly"), &ok);
    EXPECT_FALSE(ok);
}

TEST(InkTypesTest, StrokeBoundsIncludeHalfTheWidth)
{
    const auto stroke = line(10, 20, 30, 20, 4.0);
    EXPECT_EQ(stroke->bounds(), QRectF(8, 18, 24, 4));
    Stroke tap;
    tap.style.width = 6.0;
    tap.points = {{5, 5, 1.0}};
    EXPECT_EQ(tap.bounds(), QRectF(2, 2, 6, 6));
    EXPECT_TRUE(Stroke().bounds().isNull());
}

TEST(InkTypesTest, ShapeBoundsCoverEveryPointAndAnArrowsHead)
{
    Shape line;
    line.kind = ShapeKind::Line;
    line.style.width = 4.0;
    line.points = {QPointF(0, 0), QPointF(100, 50)};
    EXPECT_EQ(line.bounds(), QRectF(-2, -2, 104, 54)) << "every point, grown by half the width";
    Shape arrow = line;
    arrow.kind = ShapeKind::Arrow;
    EXPECT_EQ(arrow.bounds(), QRectF(-14, -14, 128, 78)) << "the head reaches 3 widths back from the tip, sideways too";
    EXPECT_TRUE(Shape().bounds().isNull());
}

TEST(SpatialGridTest, QueriesReturnEachItemOnceAcrossCells)
{
    SpatialGrid grid(100.0);
    grid.insert(1, QRectF(50, 50, 200, 10)); // spans three cells
    grid.insert(2, QRectF(900, 900, 5, 5));
    std::vector<qint64> hits = grid.query(QRectF(0, 0, 300, 100));
    EXPECT_EQ(hits, (std::vector<qint64>{1}));
    EXPECT_TRUE(grid.query(QRectF(400, 400, 10, 10)).empty());
    grid.remove(1, QRectF(50, 50, 200, 10));
    EXPECT_TRUE(grid.query(QRectF(0, 0, 300, 100)).empty());
    EXPECT_EQ(grid.query(QRectF(-1000, -1000, 3000, 3000)), (std::vector<qint64>{2}));
}

TEST(InkDocumentTest, ItemsKeepCreationOrderAndPaintByLayerThenT)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    const qint64 a = doc.addStroke(line(0, 0, 10, 0), Layer::User);
    const qint64 b = doc.addStroke(line(0, 5, 10, 5), Layer::Highlighter);
    const qint64 c = doc.addStroke(line(0, 9, 10, 9), Layer::User);
    EXPECT_LT(a, b);
    EXPECT_LT(b, c);
    const auto order = doc.paintOrder(QRectF(-100, -100, 200, 200));
    ASSERT_EQ(order.size(), 3u);
    EXPECT_EQ(order[0]->t, b); // highlighter under the pen strokes
    EXPECT_EQ(order[1]->t, a);
    EXPECT_EQ(order[2]->t, c);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.itemsIntersecting(QRectF(0, 8, 10, 2)), (std::vector<qint64>{c}));
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.removeItem(a));
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.removeItem(a));
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.item(a), nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 2u);
    EXPECT_GT(doc.addStroke(line(0, 0, 1, 1)), c) << "t never goes back, even after a removal";
}

TEST(InkDocumentTest, UndoRedoAreSnapshotsAndRedoClearsOnANewEdit)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.canUndo());
    const qint64 a = doc.addStroke(line(0, 0, 10, 0));
    doc.commit();
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.canUndo());
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.canRedo());
    const qint64 b = doc.addStroke(line(0, 0, 10, 10));
    doc.commit();
    ASSERT_TRUE(doc.undo());
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 1u);
    EXPECT_NE(doc.item(a), nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.item(b), nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.canRedo());
    ASSERT_TRUE(doc.redo());
    EXPECT_NE(doc.item(b), nullptr);
    ASSERT_TRUE(doc.undo());
    doc.addStroke(line(5, 5, 6, 6)); // a new edit
    doc.commit();
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.canRedo()) << "redo is cleared by a new edit";
    doc.commit(); // nothing changed since: nothing is pushed
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.undo());
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.undo());
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.undo()) << "two undos, not three: the empty commit pushed nothing";
}

TEST(InkDocumentTest, HistoryIsCappedAtOneHundred)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    for (int i = 0; i < InkDocument::kHistoryCap + 20; ++i) {
        doc.addStroke(line(i, 0, i + 1, 0));
        doc.commit();
    }
    int undone = 0;
    while (doc.undo())
        ++undone;
    EXPECT_EQ(undone, InkDocument::kHistoryCap);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 20u);
}

TEST(InkDocumentTest, ModifiedTracksTheSavedRevisionAcrossUndo)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.isModified());
    doc.addStroke(line(0, 0, 1, 1));
    doc.commit();
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.isModified());
    doc.markSaved();
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.isModified());
    ASSERT_TRUE(doc.undo());
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.isModified()) << "the file holds the stroke, the document does not";
}

TEST(InkDocumentTest, ContinuousVerticalGrowsByWholePagesAndFixedDoesNot)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    DocumentSettings settings;
    settings.format = {100, 200, 96};
    settings.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 100, 200);
    doc.setSettings(settings);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageCount(), 1);
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.growToContain(QRectF(10, 390, 10, 20))); // crosses into page 3
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageCount(), 3);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageRect(2), QRectF(0, 400, 100, 200));
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.growToContain(QRectF(10, 10, 10, 10)));
    settings.layout = LayoutKind::Fixed;
    settings.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 100, 200);
    doc.setSettings(settings);
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.growToContain(QRectF(10, 390, 10, 20)));
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageCount(), 1);
    doc.addPage();
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageCount(), 2);
}

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    qputenv("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen");
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    ::testing::InitGoogleTest(&argc, argv);
    return RUN_ALL_TESTS();
}
```

```cmake
# Handwriting (docs/INK.md, spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md):
# the ink core as a shared library and the handwritten-note tab as a loadable
# module (libs/ext/ink), the glyph studio as another (libs/ext/hwfont). Off,
# nothing ink is built and the app has no ink symbol; on, the modules are
# separate install units the base binary never links (spec section 3).
# ink-stroke-modeler is fetched and pinned in libs/ext/ink/core/CMakeLists.txt.
option(NOTEAPPC_WITH_INK "Build the ink core and the handwritten-note module (libs/ext/ink)" ON)
option(NOTEAPPC_WITH_HWFONT "Build the handwriting-font glyph studio module (libs/ext/hwfont)" ON)
```
after the imageview `add_subdirectory` block (line 325):
```cmake
if(NOTEAPPC_WITH_INK AND NOT ANDROID)
    # System Abseil is required (R3): there is no bundled copy. Without it the ink
    # core and the note module are not built, and the rest of the app is unchanged.
    find_package(absl QUIET)
    if(absl_FOUND)
        add_subdirectory(libs/ext/ink)
    else()
        message(STATUS "Abseil not found: the ink core and the handwritten-note module are not built (install abseil-cpp)")
        set(NOTEAPPC_WITH_INK OFF)
    endif()
endif()

    add_subdirectory(libs/ext/hwfont)
endif()
```

```cmake
# Handwriting: the core library (core/) that the ink and hwfont modules link.

```

`libs/ext/ink/core/CMakeLists.txt`:
```cmake
# The ink core (spec section 7): the model, the .ink format, pen input through
# ink-stroke-modeler, the outline renderer and the canvas widget. A SHARED
# library on purpose: notolith-ink.so and notolith-hwfont.so both link it and
# find it next to themselves (RPATH $ORIGIN); the base app never does.
find_package(ZLIB REQUIRED) # the gzip sniff (spec section 5): qUncompress reads no gzip header

# Static archives linked into a shared library need PIC - set before the
# modeler and its Abseil are declared, so they inherit it.
set(CMAKE_POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)
set(INK_STROKE_MODELER_BUILD_TESTING OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(INK_STROKE_MODELER_ENABLE_INSTALL OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
# Abseil is the system one (20260817 on Arch) and required: the root CMakeLists
# checked find_package(absl) before adding this directory (R3). The modeler is
# told to use it and brings none of its own. If the modeler does not build
# against the system Abseil, the ink core stays off until the modeler pin is
# moved (spec section 14).
set(INK_STROKE_MODELER_FIND_DEPENDENCIES ON CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(INK_STROKE_MODELER_FIND_ABSL ON CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
include(FetchContent)
FetchContent_Declare(ink_stroke_modeler
    GIT_REPOSITORY https://github.com/google/ink-stroke-modeler.git
    GIT_TAG f2388813b0b25bc3e33d143d369a8367ab2e30c8 # main, 2026-10-21; Apache-2.0
    EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL)
FetchContent_MakeAvailable(ink_stroke_modeler)

add_library(notolith-inkcore SHARED
    InkDocument.cpp
    InkDocument.h
    InkTypes.cpp
    InkTypes.h
    SpatialGrid.cpp
    SpatialGrid.h)
set_target_properties(notolith-inkcore PROPERTIES
    AUTOMOC ON
    OUTPUT_NAME notolith-inkcore
    LIBRARY_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules
    INSTALL_RPATH "$ORIGIN"
    BUILD_RPATH_USE_ORIGIN ON)
target_include_directories(notolith-inkcore PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR})
target_link_libraries(notolith-inkcore
    PUBLIC Qt6::Widgets Qt6::Concurrent
    PRIVATE InkStrokeModeler::stroke_modeler ZLIB::ZLIB)
install(TARGETS notolith-inkcore LIBRARY DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/noteAppC/modules)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the types**

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkTypes.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include <QColor>
#include <QJsonObject>
#include <QPointF>
#include <QRectF>
#include <QSizeF>
#include <QString>

/// The ink model (spec section 5 and 7). Units are CSS px at 96 dpi, as in
/// Rnote; A4 is 793.701 x 1122.52. Strokes are immutable once finished and
/// shared, so a history snapshot is a vector of pointers.
namespace noteAppC::ink {

enum class PressureCurve { Const, Linear, Sqrt, Cbrt, Pow2, Pow3 };
QString pressureCurveName(PressureCurve curve);
PressureCurve pressureCurveFromName(const QString &name, bool *ok = nullptr);
/// `width * curve(pressure)`, pressure clamped to 0..1.
double applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve curve, double width, double pressure);

/// Paint order is ascending (layer, t): document < image < highlighter < user.
enum class Layer { Document = 0, Image = 1, Highlighter = 2, User = 3 };
QString layerName(Layer layer);
Layer layerFromName(const QString &name);

struct StrokeStyle {
    double width = 2.0;
    QColor color{0x1e, 0x1e, 0x1e};
    PressureCurve pressureCurve = PressureCurve::Linear;
    bool operator==(const StrokeStyle &) const = default;
};

struct StrokePoint {
    double x = 0;
    double y = 0;
    double pressure = 0.5;
};

struct Stroke {
    StrokeStyle style;
    std::vector<StrokePoint> points;
    /// The points' box grown by half the style width (the widest the ink can be).
    QRectF bounds() const;
    double widthAt(size_t index) const;
};

enum class ShapeKind { Line, Arrow, Rect, Ellipse, Polyline, Polygon };
QString shapeKindName(ShapeKind kind);
ShapeKind shapeKindFromName(const QString &name, bool *ok = nullptr);

/// An Arrow's open head reaches this many stroke widths back from its tip.
inline constexpr double kArrowHeadLengthInWidths = 3.0;

struct Shape {
    ShapeKind kind = ShapeKind::Line;
    StrokeStyle style;
    std::vector<QPointF> points; ///< line/arrow: 2; rect/ellipse: 2 corners; polyline/polygon: n (model types only: no tool draws the last two, .rnote files carry them)
    QRectF bounds() const;       ///< the points' box grown by half the width, and by the arrowhead for an Arrow
};

struct TextBox {
    QRectF rect;
    QString markdown;
};

struct ImageRef {
    QRectF rect;
    QString path; ///< vault-relative
};

struct Item {
    enum class Type { Stroke, Shape, TextBox, Image, Group, Unknown };
    Type type = Type::Unknown;
    qint64 t = 0;
    Layer layer = Layer::User;
    std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> stroke;
    std::shared_ptr<const Shape> shape;
    std::shared_ptr<const TextBox> textBox;
    std::shared_ptr<const ImageRef> image;
    /// The item as read from the file, every key. Saving starts from it and
    /// overwrites the keys the model owns, so unknown keys and unknown types
    /// pass through (spec section 5).
    QJsonObject raw;
    QRectF bounds() const;
};

enum class LayoutKind { Fixed, ContinuousVertical, SemiInfinite, Infinite };
QString layoutName(LayoutKind layout);
LayoutKind layoutFromName(const QString &name);

enum class Pattern { None, Lines, Grid, Dots, LinesMargin, GridMajor, MusicStaff, Calligraphy, Seyes, Cornell, HexGrid };
QString patternName(Pattern pattern);
Pattern patternFromName(const QString &name);

struct PageFormat {
    double width = 793.701;
    double height = 1122.52;
    double dpi = 96;
};

struct Background {
    QColor color = Qt::white;
    Pattern pattern = Pattern::Lines;
    QSizeF patternSize{32, 32};
    QColor patternColor{0xc0, 0xc8, 0xd0};
};

struct DocumentSettings {
    PageFormat format;
    Background background;
    LayoutKind layout = LayoutKind::ContinuousVertical;
    QRectF extent{0, 0, 793.701, 1122.52};
    QJsonObject extra; ///< document keys the model does not own (a studio sample's "glyph")
};

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkTypes.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "InkTypes.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

namespace {
template <typename Enum, size_t N>
QString nameOf(const std::array<std::pair<Enum, const char *>, N> &table, Enum value)
{
    for (const auto &[key, name] : table)
        if (key == value)
            return QLatin1String(name);
    return QString();
}
template <typename Enum, size_t N>
Enum fromName(const std::array<std::pair<Enum, const char *>, N> &table, const QString &name, Enum fallback, bool *ok)
{
    for (const auto &[key, text] : table) {
        if (name == QLatin1String(text)) {
            if (ok) *ok = true;
            return key;
        }
    }
    if (ok) *ok = false;
    return fallback;
}
constexpr std::array<std::pair<PressureCurve, const char *>, 6> kCurves{{
    {PressureCurve::Const, "const"}, {PressureCurve::Linear, "linear"}, {PressureCurve::Sqrt, "sqrt"},
    {PressureCurve::Cbrt, "cbrt"}, {PressureCurve::Pow2, "pow2"}, {PressureCurve::Pow3, "pow3"}}};
constexpr std::array<std::pair<Layer, const char *>, 4> kLayers{{
    {Layer::Document, "document"}, {Layer::Image, "image"}, {Layer::Highlighter, "highlighter"}, {Layer::User, "user"}}};
constexpr std::array<std::pair<ShapeKind, const char *>, 6> kShapes{{
    {ShapeKind::Line, "line"}, {ShapeKind::Arrow, "arrow"}, {ShapeKind::Rect, "rect"},
    {ShapeKind::Ellipse, "ellipse"}, {ShapeKind::Polyline, "polyline"}, {ShapeKind::Polygon, "polygon"}}};
constexpr std::array<std::pair<LayoutKind, const char *>, 4> kLayouts{{
    {LayoutKind::Fixed, "fixed"}, {LayoutKind::ContinuousVertical, "continuousVertical"},
    {LayoutKind::SemiInfinite, "semiInfinite"}, {LayoutKind::Infinite, "infinite"}}};
constexpr std::array<std::pair<Pattern, const char *>, 11> kPatterns{{
    {Pattern::None, "none"}, {Pattern::Lines, "lines"}, {Pattern::Grid, "grid"}, {Pattern::Dots, "dots"},
    {Pattern::LinesMargin, "linesMargin"}, {Pattern::GridMajor, "gridMajor"}, {Pattern::MusicStaff, "musicStaff"},
    {Pattern::Calligraphy, "calligraphy"}, {Pattern::Seyes, "seyes"}, {Pattern::Cornell, "cornell"},
    {Pattern::HexGrid, "hexGrid"}}};
} // namespace

QString pressureCurveName(PressureCurve curve) { return nameOf(kCurves, curve); }
PressureCurve pressureCurveFromName(const QString &name, bool *ok) { return fromName(kCurves, name, PressureCurve::Linear, ok); }
QString layerName(Layer layer) { return nameOf(kLayers, layer); }
Layer layerFromName(const QString &name) { return fromName(kLayers, name, Layer::User, nullptr); }
QString shapeKindName(ShapeKind kind) { return nameOf(kShapes, kind); }
ShapeKind shapeKindFromName(const QString &name, bool *ok) { return fromName(kShapes, name, ShapeKind::Line, ok); }
QString layoutName(LayoutKind layout) { return nameOf(kLayouts, layout); }
LayoutKind layoutFromName(const QString &name) { return fromName(kLayouts, name, LayoutKind::ContinuousVertical, nullptr); }
QString patternName(Pattern pattern) { return nameOf(kPatterns, pattern); }
Pattern patternFromName(const QString &name) { return fromName(kPatterns, name, Pattern::Lines, nullptr); }

// Ported from Rnote (rnote-compose/src/style/mod.rs, PressureCurve::apply),
// copyright the Rnote contributors, GPL-3.0-or-later, https://github.com/flxzt/rnote.
double applyPressureCurve(PressureCurve curve, double width, double pressure)
{
    const double p = std::clamp(pressure, 0.0, 1.0);
    switch (curve) {
    case PressureCurve::Const: return width;
    case PressureCurve::Linear: return width * p;
    case PressureCurve::Sqrt: return width * std::sqrt(p);
    case PressureCurve::Cbrt: return width * std::cbrt(p);
    case PressureCurve::Pow2: return width * p * p;
    case PressureCurve::Pow3: return width * p * p * p;
    }
    return width * p;
}

double Stroke::widthAt(size_t index) const
{
    return index < points.size() ? applyPressureCurve(style.pressureCurve, style.width, points[index].pressure) : 0.0;
}

QRectF Stroke::bounds() const
{
    if (points.empty())
        return QRectF();
    double x0 = points[0].x, y0 = points[0].y, x1 = x0, y1 = y0;
    for (const StrokePoint &p : points) {
        x0 = std::min(x0, p.x); y0 = std::min(y0, p.y);
        x1 = std::max(x1, p.x); y1 = std::max(y1, p.y);
    }
    const double half = style.width * 0.5;
    return QRectF(x0 - half, y0 - half, (x1 - x0) + style.width, (y1 - y0) + style.width);
}

QRectF Shape::bounds() const
{
    if (points.empty())
        return QRectF();
    // min/max by hand: QRectF::operator| returns the other operand when one is null, and a
    // rectangle built from one point is null, so a union of points would keep only the last.
    double x0 = points[0].x(), y0 = points[0].y(), x1 = x0, y1 = y0;
    for (const QPointF &p : points) {
        x0 = std::min(x0, p.x()); y0 = std::min(y0, p.y());
        x1 = std::max(x1, p.x()); y1 = std::max(y1, p.y());
    }
    // An arrow's open head reaches kArrowHeadLengthInWidths widths back from the tip, sideways too:
    // pad that far so the spatial grid and every invalidation see the whole head.
    const double pad = style.width * 0.5 + (kind == ShapeKind::Arrow ? style.width * kArrowHeadLengthInWidths : 0.0);
    return QRectF(QPointF(x0 - pad, y0 - pad), QPointF(x1 + pad, y1 + pad));
}

QRectF Item::bounds() const
{
    switch (type) {
    case Type::Stroke: return stroke ? stroke->bounds() : QRectF();
    case Type::Shape: return shape ? shape->bounds() : QRectF();
    case Type::TextBox: return textBox ? textBox->rect : QRectF();
    case Type::Image: return image ? image->rect : QRectF();
    case Type::Group:
    case Type::Unknown: {
        const QJsonArray rect = raw.value(QStringLiteral("rect")).toArray();
        if (rect.size() == 4)
            return QRectF(rect[0].toDouble(), rect[1].toDouble(), rect[2].toDouble(), rect[3].toDouble());
        return QRectF();
    }
    }
    return QRectF();
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the grid and the document**

`libs/ext/ink/core/SpatialGrid.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#include <QRectF>
#include <QtGlobal>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

/// A uniform grid of item bounds: viewport, eraser and lasso queries without a
/// walk over every item. Cells are `cell` document px; an item is listed in
/// every cell its bounds touch and reported once.
class SpatialGrid {
public:
    explicit SpatialGrid(double cell = 256.0) : m_cell(cell) {}
    void insert(qint64 t, const QRectF &bounds);
    void remove(qint64 t, const QRectF &bounds);
    /// Sorted, unique.
    std::vector<qint64> query(const QRectF &area) const;
    void clear() { m_cells.clear(); }

private:
    static qint64 keyOf(int cx, int cy) { return (qint64(cx) << 32) ^ qint64(quint32(cy)); }
    template <typename Visit> void eachCell(const QRectF &area, Visit visit) const;
    double m_cell;
    std::unordered_map<qint64, std::vector<qint64>> m_cells;
};

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/SpatialGrid.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "SpatialGrid.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

template <typename Visit>
void SpatialGrid::eachCell(const QRectF &area, Visit visit) const
{
    if (!area.isValid())
        return;
    const int x0 = int(std::floor(area.left() / m_cell)), x1 = int(std::floor(area.right() / m_cell));
    const int y0 = int(std::floor(area.top() / m_cell)), y1 = int(std::floor(area.bottom() / m_cell));
    for (int cy = y0; cy <= y1; ++cy)
        for (int cx = x0; cx <= x1; ++cx)
            visit(keyOf(cx, cy));
}

void SpatialGrid::insert(qint64 t, const QRectF &bounds)
{
    eachCell(bounds, [&](qint64 key) { m_cells[key].push_back(t); });
}

void SpatialGrid::remove(qint64 t, const QRectF &bounds)
{
    eachCell(bounds, [&](qint64 key) {
        auto it = m_cells.find(key);
        if (it == m_cells.end())
            return;
        std::erase(it->second, t);
        if (it->second.empty())
            m_cells.erase(it);
    });
}

std::vector<qint64> SpatialGrid::query(const QRectF &area) const
{
    std::vector<qint64> out;
    eachCell(area, [&](qint64 key) {
        auto it = m_cells.find(key);
        if (it != m_cells.end())
            out.insert(out.end(), it->second.begin(), it->second.end());
    });
    std::sort(out.begin(), out.end());
    out.erase(std::unique(out.begin(), out.end()), out.end());
    return out;
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkDocument.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <deque>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "InkTypes.h"
#include "SpatialGrid.h"

namespace noteAppC::ink {

/// The document: settings plus items in creation order, a spatial grid over
/// their bounds, and undo/redo as snapshots (spec section 7). Edits mutate the
/// live state; commit() closes an edit (pen-up, an erase, a lasso commit) and
/// is what undo returns to. Snapshots share the stroke pointers.
class InkDocument {
public:
    static constexpr int kHistoryCap = 100;

    InkDocument();

    const DocumentSettings &settings() const { return m_settings; }
    void setSettings(const DocumentSettings &settings);
    const QJsonObject &extraTopLevel() const { return m_extraTop; }
    void setExtraTopLevel(const QJsonObject &extra) { m_extraTop = extra; }

    const std::vector<Item> &items() const { return m_items; }
    qint64 addStroke(std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> stroke, Layer layer = Layer::User);
    qint64 addItem(Item item);
    bool removeItem(qint64 t);
    bool replaceItem(qint64 t, Item item);
    const Item *item(qint64 t) const;
    std::vector<const Item *> paintOrder(const QRectF &area) const;
    std::vector<qint64> itemsIntersecting(const QRectF &area) const;
    QRectF contentBounds() const;

    void commit();
    bool undo();
    bool redo();
    bool canUndo() const { return !m_undo.empty(); }
    bool canRedo() const { return !m_redo.empty(); }

    quint64 revision() const { return m_revision; }
    void markSaved() { m_savedRevision = m_revision; }
    bool isModified() const { return m_revision != m_savedRevision; }

    int pageCount() const;
    QRectF pageRect(int index) const;
    bool growToContain(const QRectF &bounds);
    void addPage();

    /// A load: replaces everything, clears the history, counts as saved.
    void reset(DocumentSettings settings, std::vector<Item> items, QJsonObject extraTop);

private:
    struct Snapshot {
        std::vector<Item> items;
        DocumentSettings settings;
        qint64 nextT = 1;
        quint64 revision = 0;
    };
    Snapshot snapshot() const { return {m_items, m_settings, m_nextT, m_revision}; }
    void restore(const Snapshot &snapshot);
    void reindex();
    std::vector<Item>::iterator find(qint64 t);
    void touch() { ++m_revision; }

    DocumentSettings m_settings;
    QJsonObject m_extraTop;
    std::vector<Item> m_items; ///< ascending t
    SpatialGrid m_grid;
    qint64 m_nextT = 1;
    quint64 m_revision = 0;
    quint64 m_savedRevision = 0;
    Snapshot m_committed;
    std::deque<Snapshot> m_undo;
    std::deque<Snapshot> m_redo;
};

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkDocument.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "InkDocument.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

InkDocument::InkDocument() { m_committed = snapshot(); }

void InkDocument::setSettings(const DocumentSettings &settings)
{
    m_settings = settings;
    touch();
}

std::vector<Item>::iterator InkDocument::find(qint64 t)
{
    auto it = std::lower_bound(m_items.begin(), m_items.end(), t, [](const Item &item, qint64 value) { return item.t < value; });
    return it != m_items.end() && it->t == t ? it : m_items.end();
}

qint64 InkDocument::addStroke(std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> stroke, Layer layer)
{
    Item item;
    item.type = Item::Type::Stroke;
    item.layer = layer;
    item.stroke = std::move(stroke);
    return addItem(std::move(item));
}

qint64 InkDocument::addItem(Item item)
{
    item.t = m_nextT++;
    m_grid.insert(item.t, item.bounds());
    m_items.push_back(std::move(item));
    touch();
    return m_items.back().t;
}

bool InkDocument::removeItem(qint64 t)
{
    auto it = find(t);
    if (it == m_items.end())
        return false;
    m_grid.remove(t, it->bounds());
    m_items.erase(it);
    touch();
    return true;
}

bool InkDocument::replaceItem(qint64 t, Item item)
{
    auto it = find(t);
    if (it == m_items.end())
        return false;
    m_grid.remove(t, it->bounds());
    item.t = t;
    *it = std::move(item);
    m_grid.insert(t, it->bounds());
    touch();
    return true;
}

const Item *InkDocument::item(qint64 t) const
{
    auto it = const_cast<InkDocument *>(this)->find(t);
    return it == m_items.end() ? nullptr : &*it;
}

std::vector<qint64> InkDocument::itemsIntersecting(const QRectF &area) const
{
    std::vector<qint64> out;
    for (qint64 t : m_grid.query(area))
        if (const Item *found = item(t); found && found->bounds().intersects(area))
            out.push_back(t);
    return out;
}

std::vector<const Item *> InkDocument::paintOrder(const QRectF &area) const
{
    std::vector<const Item *> out;
    for (qint64 t : itemsIntersecting(area))
        out.push_back(item(t));
    std::stable_sort(out.begin(), out.end(), [](const Item *a, const Item *b) {
        return a->layer != b->layer ? a->layer < b->layer : a->t < b->t;
    });
    return out;
}

QRectF InkDocument::contentBounds() const
{
    QRectF box;
    for (const Item &item : m_items)
        box |= item.bounds();
    return box;
}

void InkDocument::commit()
{
    if (m_revision == m_committed.revision)
        return;
    m_undo.push_back(m_committed);
    while (int(m_undo.size()) > kHistoryCap)
        m_undo.pop_front();
    m_redo.clear();
    m_committed = snapshot();
}

bool InkDocument::undo()
{
    if (m_undo.empty())
        return false;
    m_redo.push_back(m_committed);
    restore(m_undo.back());
    m_undo.pop_back();
    m_committed = snapshot();
    return true;
}

bool InkDocument::redo()
{
    if (m_redo.empty())
        return false;
    m_undo.push_back(m_committed);
    restore(m_redo.back());
    m_redo.pop_back();
    m_committed = snapshot();
    return true;
}

void InkDocument::restore(const Snapshot &snapshot)
{
    m_items = snapshot.items;
    m_settings = snapshot.settings;
    m_nextT = snapshot.nextT;
    reindex();
    touch(); // a new revision: the document differs from whatever was saved last
}

void InkDocument::reindex()
{
    m_grid.clear();
    for (const Item &item : m_items)
        m_grid.insert(item.t, item.bounds());
}

int InkDocument::pageCount() const
{
    const double h = m_settings.format.height;
    return h > 0 ? std::max(1, int(std::ceil(m_settings.extent.height() / h - 1e-9))) : 1;
}

QRectF InkDocument::pageRect(int index) const
{
    return QRectF(m_settings.extent.x(), m_settings.extent.y() + index * m_settings.format.height,
                  m_settings.format.width, m_settings.format.height);
}

bool InkDocument::growToContain(const QRectF &bounds)
{
    if (m_settings.layout != LayoutKind::ContinuousVertical || !bounds.isValid())
        return false; // Fixed never grows; SemiInfinite and Infinite come with Track S1 Task 9
    bool grew = false;
    while (bounds.bottom() > m_settings.extent.bottom()) {
        m_settings.extent.setHeight(m_settings.extent.height() + m_settings.format.height);
        grew = true;
    }
    if (grew)
        touch();
    return grew;
}

void InkDocument::addPage()
{
    m_settings.extent.setHeight(m_settings.extent.height() + m_settings.format.height);
    touch();
}

void InkDocument::reset(DocumentSettings settings, std::vector<Item> items, QJsonObject extraTop)
{
    m_settings = std::move(settings);
    m_items = std::move(items);
    m_extraTop = std::move(extraTop);
    std::sort(m_items.begin(), m_items.end(), [](const Item &a, const Item &b) { return a.t < b.t; });
    m_nextT = m_items.empty() ? 1 : m_items.back().t + 1;
    reindex();
    m_undo.clear();
    m_redo.clear();
    touch();
    m_savedRevision = m_revision;
    m_committed = snapshot();
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

### Task 4: The `.ink` format, version 1

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/ext/ink/core/InkFormat.h`, `libs/ext/ink/core/InkFormat.cpp`
- Modify: `libs/ext/ink/core/CMakeLists.txt` (add the two files to `add_library`)
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 types and `InkDocument::reset`.
- Produces:
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
struct InkReadResult { bool ok = false; bool readOnly = false; int version = 0; QString error; };
InkReadResult readInk(const QByteArray &bytes, InkDocument &out, const QString &nameForErrors = QString());
QByteArray writeInk(const InkDocument &document);           // QJsonDocument::toJson(Compact) of the whole document, numbers rounded to 3 decimals (R4)
bool loadInkFile(const QString &path, InkDocument &out, InkReadResult *result = nullptr);
bool saveInkFile(const QString &path, const InkDocument &document, QString *error = nullptr); // tests and Vault-less tools only: temp file + rename
constexpr qint64 kOffThreadParseBytes = 256 * 1024;        // InkView parses above this on the pool (S1 Task 11)
double roundThree(double value);                            // std::round(value * 1000) / 1000
QString colorName(const QColor &color);                     // "#rrggbb", "#rrggbbaa" when translucent
QJsonObject settingsToJSON(const DocumentSettings &settings);
DocumentSettings settingsFromJSON(const QJsonObject &object);
}
```
**Writer rule (R4).** There is no hand-rolled emitter. `writeInk` builds one `QJsonObject` (unknown top-level keys first, then `format`, `version`, `document`, `items`) and returns `QJsonDocument(top).toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact)`. Every number the model writes goes through `roundThree`, so the file has at most three decimals. Qt writes keys sorted, so a re-save of an unchanged document is byte-identical and a line-based diff of a note is one long line: that is the price of having no custom emitter, and it is accepted. Reading is unchanged (gzip sniff included).

**Text box schema the host-side reader depends on (Task 17).** Top level: `"format": "notolith-ink"`, `"items": [...]`. A text box item: `{"type": "textbox", "t": <int>, "layer": "user", "rect": [x, y, w, h], "markdown": "<source text>"}`. Task 17's reader in `libs/ui` parses exactly these keys with `QJsonDocument` and duplicates them as string literals with a comment pointing here; a change to them in this task's writer must change that reader and its test.

Spec section 5 sends writes through the vault's atomic replace: **InkView (Task 8), `ink:new` and the glyph studio (Tasks 20-21) save with `writeInk()` + `Vault::writeBytes(path, bytes, &error)`** (a module may link host symbols; inkcore itself must not link `noteAppC_vault`). `saveInkFile` (a `QSaveFile`, the same atomic replace) stays for the tests and for tools that have no `Vault`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing format tests**

Append to `tests/test_ink.cpp` (add `#include "InkFormat.h"`, `#include <QDir>`, `#include <QJsonArray>`, `#include <QJsonDocument>`, `#include <QJsonObject>`, `#include <QTemporaryDir>`, `#include <zlib.h>`; `test_ink` links `ZLIB::ZLIB`, Task 3):
```cpp
namespace {

/// A gzip member: zlib's own framing (windowBits 16 + MAX_WBITS), no hand-rolled CRC.
QByteArray gzipBytes(const QByteArray &plain)
{
    z_stream stream{};
    deflateInit2(&stream, Z_BEST_COMPRESSION, Z_DEFLATED, 16 + MAX_WBITS, 8, Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY);
    QByteArray out(int(deflateBound(&stream, uLong(plain.size()))), Qt::Uninitialized);
    stream.next_in = reinterpret_cast<Bytef *>(const_cast<char *>(plain.constData()));
    stream.avail_in = uInt(plain.size());
    stream.next_out = reinterpret_cast<Bytef *>(out.data());
    stream.avail_out = uInt(out.size());
    deflate(&stream, Z_FINISH);
    out.resize(int(stream.total_out));
    deflateEnd(&stream);
    return out;
}

InkDocument sampleDocument()
{
    InkDocument doc;
    auto stroke = std::make_shared<Stroke>();
    stroke->style = {2.0, QColor(0x1e, 0x1e, 0x1e), PressureCurve::Linear};
    stroke->points = {{12.5, 40.25, 0.43}, {13.0, 41.5, 0.51}, {13.337, 42.001, 0.6}};
    doc.addStroke(stroke);
    Item box;
    box.type = Item::Type::TextBox;
    box.textBox = std::make_shared<TextBox>(TextBox{QRectF(100, 200, 400, 60), QStringLiteral("See [[Lecture 3]] #exam")});
    doc.addItem(std::move(box));
    doc.commit();
    return doc;
}

} // namespace

TEST(InkFormatTest, WritesCompactJSONWithThreeDecimals)
{
    const QByteArray bytes = writeInk(sampleDocument());
    const QJsonDocument json = QJsonDocument::fromJson(bytes);
    ASSERT_TRUE(json.isObject()) << "the whole thing is valid JSON";
    EXPECT_EQ(bytes, json.toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact)) << "the writer is QJsonDocument's compact form and nothing else";
    EXPECT_FALSE(bytes.contains('\n')) << "compact: no line breaks";
    const QJsonObject top = json.object();
    EXPECT_EQ(top.value(QStringLiteral("format")).toString(), QStringLiteral("notolith-ink"));
    EXPECT_EQ(top.value(QStringLiteral("version")).toInt(), 1);
    const QJsonArray items = top.value(QStringLiteral("items")).toArray();
    ASSERT_EQ(items.size(), 2);
    const QJsonObject stroke = items[0].toObject();
    EXPECT_EQ(stroke.value(QStringLiteral("type")).toString(), QStringLiteral("stroke"));
    EXPECT_EQ(stroke.value(QStringLiteral("t")).toInt(), 1);
    EXPECT_EQ(stroke.value(QStringLiteral("layer")).toString(), QStringLiteral("user"));
    const QJsonArray points = stroke.value(QStringLiteral("points")).toArray();
    ASSERT_EQ(points.size(), 9);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(points[6].toDouble(), 13.337) << "three decimals survive";
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(points[7].toDouble(), 42.001);
    EXPECT_TRUE(bytes.contains("13.337,42.001,0.6")) << bytes.toStdString();
    EXPECT_TRUE(bytes.contains("\"rect\":[100,200,400,60]"));
    const QJsonObject box = items[1].toObject();
    EXPECT_EQ(box.value(QStringLiteral("type")).toString(), QStringLiteral("textbox"));
    EXPECT_EQ(box.value(QStringLiteral("markdown")).toString(), QStringLiteral("See [[Lecture 3]] #exam"));
}

TEST(InkFormatTest, NumbersAreRoundedToThreeDecimals)
{
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(roundThree(1.23456), 1.235);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(roundThree(2.0004), 2.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(roundThree(-0.0004), 0.0);
    InkDocument doc;
    auto stroke = std::make_shared<Stroke>();
    stroke->points = {{1.23456, 2.00049, 0.123456}, {3.0, 4.0, 0.5}};
    doc.addStroke(stroke);
    doc.commit();
    EXPECT_TRUE(writeInk(doc).contains("[1.235,2,0.123,3,4,0.5]")) << writeInk(doc).toStdString();
}

TEST(InkFormatTest, RoundTripsAndKeepsUnknownKeysAndItemTypes)
{
    const QByteArray original = QByteArrayLiteral(
        "{\"format\":\"notolith-ink\",\"version\":1,\"document\":{\"format\":{\"width\":100,\"height\":200,\"dpi\":96},"
        "\"background\":{\"color\":\"#ffffff\",\"pattern\":\"cornell\",\"patternSize\":[120,32],\"patternColor\":\"#c0c8d0\"},"
        "\"layout\":\"fixed\",\"extent\":{\"x\":0,\"y\":0,\"width\":100,\"height\":400},"
        "\"glyph\":{\"codepoint\":97,\"baseline\":150}},\"futureKey\":true,\"items\":[\n"
        "{\"type\":\"stroke\",\"t\":1,\"layer\":\"highlighter\",\"style\":{\"width\":12,\"color\":\"#ffee0080\",\"pressureCurve\":\"const\"},"
        "\"points\":[1,2,0.5,3,4,0.5],\"mood\":\"happy\"},\n"
        "{\"type\":\"section\",\"id\":\"s1\",\"t\":2,\"items\":[1]},\n"
        "{\"type\":\"shape\",\"t\":3,\"layer\":\"user\",\"kind\":\"rect\",\"style\":{\"width\":2,\"color\":\"#000000\",\"pressureCurve\":\"linear\"},\"points\":[10,10,50,30]}\n"
        "]}\n");
    InkDocument doc;
    const InkReadResult read = readInk(original, doc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(read.ok) << read.error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_FALSE(read.readOnly);
    EXPECT_EQ(read.version, 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 3u);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items()[0].layer, Layer::Highlighter);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items()[0].stroke->style.pressureCurve, PressureCurve::Const);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items()[0].stroke->style.color, QColor(0xff, 0xee, 0x00, 0x80));
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items()[1].type, Item::Type::Unknown);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items()[2].shape->kind, ShapeKind::Rect);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.settings().layout, LayoutKind::Fixed);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.settings().background.pattern, Pattern::Cornell);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.settings().background.patternSize, QSizeF(120, 32));
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageCount(), 2);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.settings().extra.value(QStringLiteral("glyph")).toObject().value(QStringLiteral("codepoint")).toInt(), 97);
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.extraTopLevel().value(QStringLiteral("futureKey")).toBool());

    const QByteArray written = writeInk(doc);
    const QJsonObject writtenTop = QJsonDocument::fromJson(written).object();
    const QJsonArray writtenItems = writtenTop.value(QStringLiteral("items")).toArray();
    ASSERT_EQ(writtenItems.size(), 3);
    EXPECT_EQ(writtenItems[0].toObject().value(QStringLiteral("mood")).toString(), QStringLiteral("happy"));
    EXPECT_EQ(writtenItems[1].toObject(),
              QJsonDocument::fromJson(QByteArrayLiteral("{\"type\":\"section\",\"id\":\"s1\",\"t\":2,\"items\":[1]}")).object())
        << "an unknown item is written back as it was read: no key added, none changed";
    const QJsonObject writtenDocument = writtenTop.value(QStringLiteral("document")).toObject();
    EXPECT_EQ(writtenDocument.value(QStringLiteral("glyph")).toObject().value(QStringLiteral("codepoint")).toInt(), 97);
    EXPECT_TRUE(writtenTop.value(QStringLiteral("futureKey")).toBool());
    EXPECT_EQ(writtenDocument.value(QStringLiteral("background")).toObject().value(QStringLiteral("patternSize")).toArray(),
              (QJsonArray{120, 32}));
    InkDocument again;
    ASSERT_TRUE(readInk(written, again).ok);
    EXPECT_EQ(writeInk(again), written) << "a second round trip is byte-stable";
}

TEST(InkFormatTest, ReadsAGzipWrappedFile)
{
    const QByteArray plain = writeInk(sampleDocument());
    InkDocument doc;
    const InkReadResult read = readInk(gzipBytes(plain), doc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(read.ok) << read.error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 2u);
    EXPECT_EQ(writeInk(doc), plain) << "saved back as plain JSON: gzip is a reader feature in v1";
}

TEST(InkFormatTest, AVersionTwoFileOpensReadOnly)
{
    QByteArray bytes = writeInk(sampleDocument());
    bytes.replace("\"version\":1", "\"version\":2");
    InkDocument doc;
    const InkReadResult read = readInk(bytes, doc);
    EXPECT_TRUE(read.ok);
    EXPECT_TRUE(read.readOnly);
    EXPECT_EQ(read.version, 2);
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 2u) << "read as far as v1 understands";
}

TEST(InkFormatTest, TruncatedAndForeignFilesAreRefusedWithTheName)
{
    const QByteArray plain = writeInk(sampleDocument());
    InkDocument doc;
    InkReadResult read = readInk(plain.left(plain.size() / 2), doc, QStringLiteral("Lecture 3.ink"));
    EXPECT_FALSE(read.ok);
    EXPECT_TRUE(read.error.contains(QStringLiteral("Lecture 3.ink")));
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.items().empty()) << "a failed read leaves the document untouched";
    const QByteArray gz = gzipBytes(plain);
    read = readInk(gz.left(gz.size() - 12), doc, QStringLiteral("x.ink"));
    EXPECT_FALSE(read.ok) << "a truncated gzip stream is an error, not a shorter note";
    read = readInk(QByteArrayLiteral("{\"format\":\"something-else\",\"version\":1,\"items\":[]}"), doc);
    EXPECT_FALSE(read.ok);
    read = readInk(QByteArrayLiteral("{\"format\":\"notolith-ink\",\"version\":1,\"items\":[{\"type\":\"stroke\",\"t\":1,\"points\":[1,2]}]}"), doc);
    EXPECT_FALSE(read.ok) << "points must be x,y,pressure triples";
}

TEST(InkFormatTest, SavesAndLoadsThroughAnAtomicReplace)
{
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    const QString path = tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("note.ink"));
    QString error;
    ASSERT_TRUE(saveInkFile(path, sampleDocument(), &error)) << error.toStdString();
    InkDocument doc;
    InkReadResult read;
    ASSERT_TRUE(loadInkFile(path, doc, &read)) << read.error.toStdString();
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.items().size(), 2u);
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.isModified());
    EXPECT_TRUE(QDir(tmp.path()).entryList(QDir::Files | QDir::Hidden).size() == 1) << "no temp file left behind";
    EXPECT_FALSE(loadInkFile(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("missing.ink")), doc, &read));
    EXPECT_TRUE(read.error.contains(QStringLiteral("missing.ink")));
}
```
