`libs/ext/ink/core/InkFormat.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <QByteArray>
#include <QJsonObject>
#include <QString>

#include "InkDocument.h"

/// The .ink format, version 1 (spec section 5): UTF-8 JSON written compact by
/// QJsonDocument (no custom emitter), numbers with three decimals, unknown keys
/// and item types kept and written back.
/// A reader seeing the gzip magic (1f 8b) inflates first; a version above
/// 1 opens read-only. Documented for users in docs/INK.md.
namespace noteAppC::ink {

struct InkReadResult {
    bool ok = false;
    bool readOnly = false;
    int version = 0;
    QString error;
};

/// Files above this parse off the UI thread (InkView, Track S1 Task 11).
constexpr qint64 kOffThreadParseBytes = 256 * 1024;

InkReadResult readInk(const QByteArray &bytes, InkDocument &out, const QString &nameForErrors = QString());
QByteArray writeInk(const InkDocument &document);
bool loadInkFile(const QString &path, InkDocument &out, InkReadResult *result = nullptr);
/// Temporary file and rename: for tests and tools without a Vault. The
/// modules save through writeInk() + Vault::writeBytes (spec section 5).
bool saveInkFile(const QString &path, const InkDocument &document, QString *error = nullptr);

double roundThree(double value);
QString colorName(const QColor &color);
QColor colorFromName(const QString &name, const QColor &fallback);
QJsonObject settingsToJSON(const DocumentSettings &settings);
DocumentSettings settingsFromJSON(const QJsonObject &object);

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkFormat.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "InkFormat.h"

#include <cmath>

#include <zlib.h>

#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QJsonArray>
#include <QJsonDocument>
#include <QSaveFile>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

namespace {

const QSet<QString> kDocumentKeys = {QStringLiteral("format"), QStringLiteral("background"), QStringLiteral("layout"),
                                     QStringLiteral("extent")};
const QSet<QString> kTopKeys = {QStringLiteral("format"), QStringLiteral("version"), QStringLiteral("document"),
                                QStringLiteral("items")};

bool inflateGzip(const QByteArray &in, QByteArray *out, QString *error)
{
    z_stream stream{};
    if (inflateInit2(&stream, 16 + MAX_WBITS) != Z_OK) {
        *error = QStringLiteral("zlib could not start");
        return false;
    }
    stream.next_in = reinterpret_cast<Bytef *>(const_cast<char *>(in.constData()));
    stream.avail_in = uInt(in.size());
    char buffer[1 << 16];
    int rc = Z_OK;
    while (rc != Z_STREAM_END) {
        stream.next_out = reinterpret_cast<Bytef *>(buffer);
        stream.avail_out = sizeof buffer;
        rc = inflate(&stream, Z_NO_FLUSH);
        if (rc != Z_OK && rc != Z_STREAM_END) {
            inflateEnd(&stream);
            *error = rc == Z_BUF_ERROR ? QStringLiteral("the gzip stream ends early") : QStringLiteral("the gzip stream is damaged");
            return false;
        }
        out->append(buffer, int(sizeof buffer - stream.avail_out));
    }
    inflateEnd(&stream);
    return true;
}

QJsonValue number(double value) { return QJsonValue(roundThree(value)); }

QJsonArray rectToJSON(const QRectF &rect)
{
    return {number(rect.x()), number(rect.y()), number(rect.width()), number(rect.height())};
}

QRectF rectFromJSON(const QJsonArray &array)
{
    return array.size() == 4 ? QRectF(array[0].toDouble(), array[1].toDouble(), array[2].toDouble(), array[3].toDouble()) : QRectF();
}

QJsonObject styleToJSON(const StrokeStyle &style)
{
    return {{QStringLiteral("width"), number(style.width)},
            {QStringLiteral("color"), colorName(style.color)},
            {QStringLiteral("pressureCurve"), pressureCurveName(style.pressureCurve)}};
}

StrokeStyle styleFromJSON(const QJsonObject &object)
{
    StrokeStyle style;
    style.width = object.value(QStringLiteral("width")).toDouble(style.width);
    style.color = colorFromName(object.value(QStringLiteral("color")).toString(), style.color);
    style.pressureCurve = pressureCurveFromName(object.value(QStringLiteral("pressureCurve")).toString(QStringLiteral("linear")));
    return style;
}

bool itemFromJSON(const QJsonObject &object, Item *item, QString *error)
{
    item->raw = object;
    const double t = object.value(QStringLiteral("t")).toDouble(-1);
    if (t < 0) {
        *error = QStringLiteral("an item has no t");
        return false;
    }
    item->t = qint64(t);
    item->layer = layerFromName(object.value(QStringLiteral("layer")).toString(QStringLiteral("user")));
    const QString type = object.value(QStringLiteral("type")).toString();
    if (type == QLatin1String("stroke")) {
        auto stroke = std::make_shared<Stroke>();
        stroke->style = styleFromJSON(object.value(QStringLiteral("style")).toObject());
        const QJsonArray points = object.value(QStringLiteral("points")).toArray();
        if (points.size() % 3 != 0) {
            *error = QStringLiteral("stroke %1: points are not x, y, pressure triples").arg(item->t);
            return false;
        }
        stroke->points.reserve(size_t(points.size() / 3));
        for (qsizetype i = 0; i + 2 < points.size(); i += 3)
            stroke->points.push_back({points[i].toDouble(), points[i + 1].toDouble(), points[i + 2].toDouble()});
        item->type = Item::Type::Stroke;
        item->stroke = std::move(stroke);
    } else if (type == QLatin1String("shape")) {
        auto shape = std::make_shared<Shape>();
        bool ok = false;
        shape->kind = shapeKindFromName(object.value(QStringLiteral("kind")).toString(), &ok);
        if (!ok) {
            item->type = Item::Type::Unknown; // a shape kind this version does not draw: kept, not drawn
            return true;
        }
        shape->style = styleFromJSON(object.value(QStringLiteral("style")).toObject());
        const QJsonArray points = object.value(QStringLiteral("points")).toArray();
        for (qsizetype i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); i += 2)
            shape->points.emplace_back(points[i].toDouble(), points[i + 1].toDouble());
        item->type = Item::Type::Shape;
        item->shape = std::move(shape);
    } else if (type == QLatin1String("textbox")) {
        item->type = Item::Type::TextBox;
        item->textBox = std::make_shared<TextBox>(TextBox{rectFromJSON(object.value(QStringLiteral("rect")).toArray()),
                                                          object.value(QStringLiteral("markdown")).toString()});
    } else if (type == QLatin1String("image")) {
        item->type = Item::Type::Image;
        item->image = std::make_shared<ImageRef>(ImageRef{rectFromJSON(object.value(QStringLiteral("rect")).toArray()),
                                                          object.value(QStringLiteral("path")).toString()});
    } else if (type == QLatin1String("group")) {
        item->type = Item::Type::Group;
    } else {
        item->type = Item::Type::Unknown;
    }
    return true;
}

QJsonObject itemToJSON(const Item &item)
{
    if (item.type == Item::Type::Unknown || item.type == Item::Type::Group)
        return item.raw; // read back verbatim: no key added, none changed (spec section 5, pass-through)
    QJsonObject object = item.raw; // unknown keys first; the model's keys overwrite
    object.insert(QStringLiteral("t"), double(item.t));
    object.insert(QStringLiteral("layer"), layerName(item.layer));
    switch (item.type) {
    case Item::Type::Stroke: {
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("type"), QStringLiteral("stroke"));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("style"), styleToJSON(item.stroke->style));
        QJsonArray points;
        for (const StrokePoint &p : item.stroke->points) {
            points.append(number(p.x));
            points.append(number(p.y));
            points.append(number(p.pressure));
        }
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("points"), points);
        break;
    }
    case Item::Type::Shape: {
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("type"), QStringLiteral("shape"));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("kind"), shapeKindName(item.shape->kind));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("style"), styleToJSON(item.shape->style));
        QJsonArray points;
        for (const QPointF &p : item.shape->points) {
            points.append(number(p.x()));
            points.append(number(p.y()));
        }
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("points"), points);
        break;
    }
    case Item::Type::TextBox:
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("type"), QStringLiteral("textbox"));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("rect"), rectToJSON(item.textBox->rect));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("markdown"), item.textBox->markdown);
        break;
    case Item::Type::Image:
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("type"), QStringLiteral("image"));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("rect"), rectToJSON(item.image->rect));
        object.insert(QStringLiteral("path"), item.image->path);
        break;
    case Item::Type::Group:
    case Item::Type::Unknown:
        break; // returned verbatim above
    }
    return object;
}

} // namespace

double roundThree(double value) { return std::round(value * 1000.0) / 1000.0; }

// "#rrggbb", or "#rrggbbaa" when translucent (CSS order; QColor's own
// HexArgb form is "#aarrggbb", so both directions are spelled out here).
QString colorName(const QColor &color)
{
    if (color.alpha() == 255)
        return color.name(QColor::HexRgb);
    return color.name(QColor::HexRgb) + QString::number(color.alpha(), 16).rightJustified(2, QLatin1Char('0'));
}

QColor colorFromName(const QString &name, const QColor &fallback)
{
    if (name.size() == 9 && name.startsWith(QLatin1Char('#'))) {
        bool ok = false;
        const int alpha = name.mid(7).toInt(&ok, 16);
        QColor color = QColor::fromString(name.left(7));
        if (ok && color.isValid()) {
            color.setAlpha(alpha);
            return color;
        }
        return fallback;
    }
    return QColor::isValidColorName(name) ? QColor::fromString(name) : fallback;
}

QJsonObject settingsToJSON(const DocumentSettings &s)
{
    QJsonObject object = s.extra;
    object.insert(QStringLiteral("format"), QJsonObject{{QStringLiteral("width"), number(s.format.width)},
                                                        {QStringLiteral("height"), number(s.format.height)},
                                                        {QStringLiteral("dpi"), number(s.format.dpi)}});
    const QJsonValue patternSize = s.background.patternSize.width() == s.background.patternSize.height()
        ? QJsonValue(number(s.background.patternSize.width()))
        : QJsonValue(QJsonArray{number(s.background.patternSize.width()), number(s.background.patternSize.height())});
    object.insert(QStringLiteral("background"), QJsonObject{{QStringLiteral("color"), colorName(s.background.color)},
                                                            {QStringLiteral("pattern"), patternName(s.background.pattern)},
                                                            {QStringLiteral("patternSize"), patternSize},
                                                            {QStringLiteral("patternColor"), colorName(s.background.patternColor)}});
    object.insert(QStringLiteral("layout"), layoutName(s.layout));
    object.insert(QStringLiteral("extent"), QJsonObject{{QStringLiteral("x"), number(s.extent.x())},
                                                        {QStringLiteral("y"), number(s.extent.y())},
                                                        {QStringLiteral("width"), number(s.extent.width())},
                                                        {QStringLiteral("height"), number(s.extent.height())}});
    return object;
}

DocumentSettings settingsFromJSON(const QJsonObject &object)
{
    DocumentSettings s;
    const QJsonObject format = object.value(QStringLiteral("format")).toObject();
    s.format.width = format.value(QStringLiteral("width")).toDouble(s.format.width);
    s.format.height = format.value(QStringLiteral("height")).toDouble(s.format.height);
    s.format.dpi = format.value(QStringLiteral("dpi")).toDouble(s.format.dpi);
    const QJsonObject background = object.value(QStringLiteral("background")).toObject();
    s.background.color = colorFromName(background.value(QStringLiteral("color")).toString(), s.background.color);
    s.background.pattern = patternFromName(background.value(QStringLiteral("pattern")).toString(QStringLiteral("lines")));
    const QJsonValue size = background.value(QStringLiteral("patternSize"));
    if (size.isArray() && size.toArray().size() == 2)
        s.background.patternSize = QSizeF(size.toArray()[0].toDouble(32), size.toArray()[1].toDouble(32));
    else if (size.isDouble())
        s.background.patternSize = QSizeF(size.toDouble(), size.toDouble());
    s.background.patternColor = colorFromName(background.value(QStringLiteral("patternColor")).toString(), s.background.patternColor);
    s.layout = layoutFromName(object.value(QStringLiteral("layout")).toString(QStringLiteral("continuousVertical")));
    const QJsonObject extent = object.value(QStringLiteral("extent")).toObject();
    if (!extent.isEmpty())
        s.extent = QRectF(extent.value(QStringLiteral("x")).toDouble(), extent.value(QStringLiteral("y")).toDouble(),
                          extent.value(QStringLiteral("width")).toDouble(s.format.width),
                          extent.value(QStringLiteral("height")).toDouble(s.format.height));
    else
        s.extent = QRectF(0, 0, s.format.width, s.format.height);
    for (auto it = object.begin(); it != object.end(); ++it)
        if (!kDocumentKeys.contains(it.key()))
            s.extra.insert(it.key(), it.value());
    return s;
}

InkReadResult readInk(const QByteArray &input, InkDocument &out, const QString &nameForErrors)
{
    InkReadResult result;
    const QString name = nameForErrors.isEmpty() ? QStringLiteral("the ink file") : nameForErrors;
    QByteArray bytes = input;
    if (bytes.size() >= 2 && quint8(bytes[0]) == 0x1f && quint8(bytes[1]) == 0x8b) {
        QByteArray inflated;
        QString why;
        if (!inflateGzip(bytes, &inflated, &why)) {
            result.error = QStringLiteral("%1 could not be read: %2").arg(name, why);
            return result;
        }
        bytes = inflated;
    }
    QJsonParseError parseError;
    const QJsonDocument json = QJsonDocument::fromJson(bytes, &parseError);
    if (!json.isObject()) {
        result.error = QStringLiteral("%1 could not be read: %2 at byte %3").arg(name, parseError.errorString()).arg(parseError.offset);
        return result;
    }
    const QJsonObject top = json.object();
    if (top.value(QStringLiteral("format")).toString() != QLatin1String("notolith-ink")) {
        result.error = QStringLiteral("%1 is not a Notolith ink file").arg(name);
        return result;
    }
    result.version = top.value(QStringLiteral("version")).toInt(0);
    if (result.version < 1) {
        result.error = QStringLiteral("%1 has no version").arg(name);
        return result;
    }
    result.readOnly = result.version > 1;
    std::vector<Item> items;
    for (const QJsonValue &value : top.value(QStringLiteral("items")).toArray()) {
        Item item;
        QString why;
        if (!itemFromJSON(value.toObject(), &item, &why)) {
            result.error = QStringLiteral("%1 could not be read: %2").arg(name, why);
            return result;
        }
        items.push_back(std::move(item));
    }
    QJsonObject extraTop;
    for (auto it = top.begin(); it != top.end(); ++it)
        if (!kTopKeys.contains(it.key()))
            extraTop.insert(it.key(), it.value());
    out.reset(settingsFromJSON(top.value(QStringLiteral("document")).toObject()), std::move(items), extraTop);
    result.ok = true;
    return result;
}

QByteArray writeInk(const InkDocument &document)
{
    QJsonObject top = document.extraTopLevel(); // unknown top-level keys first; the format's own keys overwrite
    top.insert(QStringLiteral("format"), QStringLiteral("notolith-ink"));
    top.insert(QStringLiteral("version"), 1);
    top.insert(QStringLiteral("document"), settingsToJSON(document.settings()));
    QJsonArray items;
    for (const Item &item : document.items())
        items.append(itemToJSON(item));
    top.insert(QStringLiteral("items"), items);
    // R4: no hand-rolled emitter. QJsonDocument writes keys sorted and each number in its shortest form.
    return QJsonDocument(top).toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact);
}

bool loadInkFile(const QString &path, InkDocument &out, InkReadResult *result)
{
    InkReadResult local;
    InkReadResult &r = result ? *result : local;
    QFile file(path);
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly)) {
        r = InkReadResult{};
        r.error = QStringLiteral("%1 could not be opened: %2").arg(QFileInfo(path).fileName(), file.errorString());
        return false;
    }
    r = readInk(file.readAll(), out, QFileInfo(path).fileName());
    return r.ok;
}

bool saveInkFile(const QString &path, const InkDocument &document, QString *error)
{
    QSaveFile file(path);
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly)) {
        if (error) *error = file.errorString();
        return false;
    }
    const QByteArray bytes = writeInk(document);
    if (file.write(bytes) != bytes.size() || !file.commit()) {
        if (error) *error = file.errorString();
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

```cpp
    }

/// Max per-channel difference and the share of pixels that differ by more than 8.
bool imagesMatch(const QImage &a, const QImage &b, QString *why)
{
    if (a.size() != b.size()) {
        *why = QStringLiteral("size %1x%2 vs %3x%4").arg(a.width()).arg(a.height()).arg(b.width()).arg(b.height());
        return false;
    }
    const QImage x = a.convertToFormat(QImage::Format_ARGB32), y = b.convertToFormat(QImage::Format_ARGB32);
    qint64 bad = 0;
    for (int row = 0; row < x.height(); ++row) {
        const QRgb *p = reinterpret_cast<const QRgb *>(x.constScanLine(row));
        const QRgb *q = reinterpret_cast<const QRgb *>(y.constScanLine(row));
        for (int col = 0; col < x.width(); ++col) {
            const int d = std::max({std::abs(qRed(p[col]) - qRed(q[col])), std::abs(qGreen(p[col]) - qGreen(q[col])),
                                    std::abs(qBlue(p[col]) - qBlue(q[col])), std::abs(qAlpha(p[col]) - qAlpha(q[col]))});
            bad += d > 8;
        }
    }
    const double share = double(bad) / double(qint64(x.width()) * x.height());
    *why = QStringLiteral("%1 of pixels differ by more than 8").arg(share, 0, 'f', 4);
    return share < 0.005;
}

void checkGolden(const QString &name, const QImage &image)
{
    const QString path = QStringLiteral(NOTEAPPC_INK_GOLDEN_DIR "/") + name;
    if (qEnvironmentVariableIsSet("NOTEAPPC_UPDATE_GOLDEN")) {
        QDir().mkpath(QStringLiteral(NOTEAPPC_INK_GOLDEN_DIR));
        ASSERT_TRUE(image.save(path));
        GTEST_SKIP() << "golden image written: " << path.toStdString() << " - look at it, then commit it";
    }
    QImage golden(path);
    ASSERT_FALSE(golden.isNull()) << "no golden image at " << path.toStdString() << "; run once with NOTEAPPC_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1";
    QString why;
    EXPECT_TRUE(imagesMatch(image, golden, &why)) << name.toStdString() << ": " << why.toStdString();
}

} // namespace

TEST(StrokeOutlineTest, OutlineIsOnePathPerStrokeFilledOnceWithWindingFill)
{
    Stroke stroke;
    stroke.style = {4.0, Qt::black, PressureCurve::Const};
    stroke.points = {{0, 0, 1}, {10, 0, 1}, {20, 0, 1}};
    const QPainterPath path = strokeOutline(stroke);
    EXPECT_EQ(path.fillRule(), Qt::WindingFill);
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(10, 0)));
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(5, 1.5)));
    EXPECT_FALSE(path.contains(QPointF(5, 2.5)));
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(-1.5, 0))) << "round start cap";
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(21.5, 0))) << "round end cap";
    EXPECT_FALSE(path.contains(QPointF(-2.5, 0)));
    EXPECT_NEAR(path.boundingRect().left(), -2.0, 0.05);
    EXPECT_NEAR(path.boundingRect().right(), 22.0, 0.05);
}

TEST(StrokeOutlineTest, WidthFollowsPressureAlongTheStroke)
{
    Stroke stroke;
    stroke.style = {10.0, Qt::black, PressureCurve::Linear};
    stroke.points = {{0, 0, 0.2}, {100, 0, 1.0}};
    const QPainterPath path = strokeOutline(stroke);
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(0, 0.9)));
    EXPECT_FALSE(path.contains(QPointF(0, 1.1))) << "2 px wide at the start";
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(100, 4.9)));
    EXPECT_FALSE(path.contains(QPointF(100, 5.1))) << "10 px wide at the end";
}

TEST(StrokeOutlineTest, ATapAndCoincidingPointsDrawADotAndNoNaN)
{
    Stroke tap;
    tap.style = {8.0, Qt::black, PressureCurve::Const};
    tap.points = {{50, 50, 1.0}};
    QPainterPath path = strokeOutline(tap);
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(53.5, 50)));
    EXPECT_FALSE(path.contains(QPointF(54.5, 50)));
    tap.points = {{50, 50, 1.0}, {50, 50, 1.0}, {50, 50, 1.0}};
    path = strokeOutline(tap);
    EXPECT_TRUE(path.contains(QPointF(53.5, 50))) << "every point the same: still a dot";
    const QRectF box = path.boundingRect();
    EXPECT_FALSE(std::isnan(box.left()) || std::isnan(box.width()));
    EXPECT_TRUE(strokeOutline(Stroke()).isEmpty());
    Stroke mixed;
    mixed.style = {4.0, Qt::black, PressureCurve::Const};
    mixed.points = {{0, 0, 1}, {0, 0, 1}, {10, 0, 1}}; // a duplicate then a real segment
    EXPECT_TRUE(strokeOutline(mixed).contains(QPointF(5, 0)));
}

TEST(StrokeOutlineTest, PartialOutlineCoversOnlyTheAskedSegments)
{
    Stroke stroke;
    stroke.style = {4.0, Qt::black, PressureCurve::Const};
    stroke.points = {{0, 0, 1}, {10, 0, 1}, {20, 0, 1}, {30, 0, 1}};
    const QPainterPath tail = strokeOutline(stroke, 2, 3);
    EXPECT_TRUE(tail.contains(QPointF(25, 0)));
    EXPECT_FALSE(tail.contains(QPointF(5, 0)));
}

TEST(StrokeOutlineTest, MatchesTheGoldenImagesAtTwoZoomsAndTwoRatios)
{
    const auto strokes = goldenStrokes();
    const QRectF area(0, 0, 300, 200);
    checkGolden(QStringLiteral("outline-z1-d1.png"), renderStrokes(strokes, area, 1.0, 1.0, Qt::white));
    checkGolden(QStringLiteral("outline-z2-d1.png"), renderStrokes(strokes, area, 2.0, 1.0, Qt::white));
    checkGolden(QStringLiteral("outline-z1-d2.png"), renderStrokes(strokes, area, 1.0, 2.0, Qt::white));
    checkGolden(QStringLiteral("outline-z2-d2.png"), renderStrokes(strokes, area, 2.0, 2.0, Qt::white));
    const QImage z2 = renderStrokes(strokes, area, 2.0, 1.0, Qt::white);
    EXPECT_EQ(z2.size(), QSize(600, 400));
    const QImage d2 = renderStrokes(strokes, area, 1.0, 2.0, Qt::white);
    EXPECT_EQ(d2.size(), QSize(600, 400));
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(d2.devicePixelRatio(), 2.0);
}
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/StrokeOutline.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include <QColor>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPainterPath>

#include "InkTypes.h"

class QPainter;

namespace noteAppC::ink {

/// One QPainterPath per stroke, filled once with Qt::WindingFill, so overlaps
/// never darken a translucent colour (spec section 7, Rendering). Rnote's
/// per-segment closed subpaths (a quad at `width * curve(pressure)` with cubic
/// round caps) are appended into that one path.
QPainterPath strokeOutline(const Stroke &stroke);
/// The segments between points `firstPoint` and `lastPoint` inclusive: what
/// the live stroke rasterises per tablet event.
QPainterPath strokeOutline(const Stroke &stroke, size_t firstPoint, size_t lastPoint);
void paintStroke(QPainter &painter, const Stroke &stroke);
/// `documentRect` of the document at `zoom`, into an image of
/// `documentRect.size() * zoom * devicePixelRatio` physical pixels.
QImage renderStrokes(const std::vector<std::shared_ptr<const Stroke>> &strokes, const QRectF &documentRect, double zoom,
                     double devicePixelRatio, const QColor &background);

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/StrokeOutline.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "StrokeOutline.h"

#include <cmath>

#include <QPainter>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

// Ported from Rnote (rnote-compose/src/style/smooth/mod.rs,
// compose_lines_variable_width), copyright the Rnote contributors,
// GPL-3.0-or-later, https://github.com/flxzt/rnote. Rnote composes each pen
// path segment into a closed polygon - offset points at half the width on
// either side, a cubic round cap at both ends whose control points sit 2/3 of
// the width beyond the end - and appends every segment into one path filled
// with the non-zero rule. Only LineTo segments exist in files the default
// builder writes (rnote-study.md section 1.6), so a segment here is one line.
QPainterPath strokeOutline(const Stroke &stroke, size_t firstPoint, size_t lastPoint)
{
    QPainterPath path;
    path.setFillRule(Qt::WindingFill);
    const std::vector<StrokePoint> &pts = stroke.points;
    if (pts.empty() || firstPoint > lastPoint || lastPoint >= pts.size())
        return path;
    bool anySegment = false;
    for (size_t i = firstPoint; i < lastPoint; ++i) {
        const QPointF a(pts[i].x, pts[i].y), b(pts[i + 1].x, pts[i + 1].y);
        const QPointF d = b - a;
        const double length = std::hypot(d.x(), d.y());
        if (length <= 0.0)
            continue; // a duplicate sample has no direction: skipped, as Rnote filters zero-length lines
        anySegment = true;
        const double w0 = stroke.widthAt(i), w1 = stroke.widthAt(i + 1);
        const QPointF dir = d / length;
        const QPointF orth(-dir.y(), dir.x());
        const QPointF aPos = a + orth * (w0 * 0.5), aNeg = a - orth * (w0 * 0.5);
        const QPointF bPos = b + orth * (w1 * 0.5), bNeg = b - orth * (w1 * 0.5);
        if (w0 > 0.0) {
            path.moveTo(aNeg);
            path.cubicTo(aNeg - dir * (w0 * 2.0 / 3.0), aPos - dir * (w0 * 2.0 / 3.0), aPos);
        } else {
            path.moveTo(aPos);
        }
        path.lineTo(bPos);
        if (w1 > 0.0)
            path.cubicTo(bPos + dir * (w1 * 2.0 / 3.0), bNeg + dir * (w1 * 2.0 / 3.0), bNeg);
        else
            path.lineTo(bNeg);
        path.lineTo(aNeg);
        path.closeSubpath();
    }
    if (!anySegment) {
        // A tap, or every point the same: Rnote draws a circle of the start width.
        const double radius = stroke.widthAt(firstPoint) * 0.5;
        if (radius > 0.0)
            path.addEllipse(QPointF(pts[firstPoint].x, pts[firstPoint].y), radius, radius);
    }
    return path;
}

QPainterPath strokeOutline(const Stroke &stroke)
{
    return stroke.points.empty() ? QPainterPath() : strokeOutline(stroke, 0, stroke.points.size() - 1);
}

void paintStroke(QPainter &painter, const Stroke &stroke)
{
    painter.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    painter.setBrush(stroke.style.color);
    painter.drawPath(strokeOutline(stroke));
}

QImage renderStrokes(const std::vector<std::shared_ptr<const Stroke>> &strokes, const QRectF &documentRect, double zoom,
                     double devicePixelRatio, const QColor &background)
{
    const QSize logical(int(std::ceil(documentRect.width() * zoom)), int(std::ceil(documentRect.height() * zoom)));
    QImage image(logical * devicePixelRatio, QImage::Format_ARGB32_Premultiplied);
    image.setDevicePixelRatio(devicePixelRatio);
    image.fill(background);
    QPainter painter(&image);
    painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    painter.scale(zoom, zoom);
    painter.translate(-documentRect.topLeft());
    for (const auto &stroke : strokes)
        if (stroke)
            paintStroke(painter, *stroke);
    return image;
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

### Task 6: Pen input through ink-stroke-modeler

**The spike is done (R14, PADD, 2026-10-22).** `kInputRateHz = 120` (it feeds only the modeler's `min_output_rate`; the wobble timeout stays Rnote's 0.04 s) and `kFractionalScaleOffset = {0, 0}` go into `InkInput.h` here, together with `PressureNormaliser`, which exists because PADD's pen never reports above 0.657 (median 0.21): raw pressure would make every line thin. The other three spike constants are read by Task 7.

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/ext/ink/core/InkInput.h`, `libs/ext/ink/core/InkInput.cpp`
- Modify: `libs/ext/ink/core/CMakeLists.txt` (add the two files)
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Stroke`, `StrokePoint`, `StrokeStyle` (Task 3); `::ink::StrokeModeler` from `ink_stroke_modeler/stroke_modeler.h`, `::ink::StrokeModelParams` from `ink_stroke_modeler/params.h`, `::ink::Input`, `::ink::Result`, `::ink::Time`, `::ink::Duration` from `ink_stroke_modeler/types.h`. **The modeler lives in `namespace ink`; inside `noteAppC::ink` always write `::ink::` or the name resolves to our own namespace.**
- Produces:
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
// Spike results (PADD, 2026-10-22, NOTES.md): tip only, no eraser end, buttons dead, 120 Hz in contact. Change only from a new measurement, never the names or shapes.
inline constexpr Qt::MouseButton kHeldEraserButton = Qt::MiddleButton;  // BTN_STYLUS, the lower barrel button (spec section 7, Sources)
inline constexpr Qt::MouseButton kHeldLassoButton = Qt::RightButton;    // BTN_STYLUS2, the upper barrel button
inline constexpr bool kTrustEraserPointerType = true;                   // the pen's eraser end reports PointerType::Eraser
inline constexpr double kInputRateHz = 120.0;                           // measured on PADD; feeds only the modeler's min_output_rate
inline constexpr QPointF kFractionalScaleOffset{0.0, 0.0};              // subtracted from every tablet event's widget position (Task 7); non-zero only if the spike measured an offset

struct InkSample { QPointF position; double pressure = 0.5; double timeSeconds = 0; };

// R14: raw pressure divided by a learned per-device maximum. Starts at 1.0 (no change). After
// kCalibrationSamples samples above kStrongFraction of the current maximum it takes the peak seen
// as the maximum (this can LOWER it from the 1.0 default: that is the point, PADD peaks at 0.657);
// from then on it only rises, when a harder press exceeds it. Unknown pressure (negative, NaN) passes through.
class PressureNormaliser {
public:
    static constexpr int kCalibrationSamples = 200;
    static constexpr double kStrongFraction = 0.3;
    static constexpr double kMinimumMax = 0.1;   // a broken device reporting ~0 must not divide everything up to 1
    double normalise(double raw);                // min(1, raw / learnedMax()); learns as a side effect
    double learnedMax() const;
    void setLearnedMax(double max);              // a stored value: below 1.0 it counts as already calibrated; not reported as a change
    bool takeChanged();                          // true once after the maximum changed by learning: the canvas persists it
};

class InkInput {
public:
    InkInput();
    ~InkInput();
    void setRawInput(bool raw);                       // bypass the modeler: every sample is a point
    void begin(const InkSample &sample, const StrokeStyle &style);
    void extend(const InkSample &sample);
    void end(const InkSample &sample);
    bool active() const;
    const Stroke &stroke() const;                     // modelled points so far (no prediction)
    const std::vector<StrokePoint> &prediction() const; // predicted tail after the last modelled point; empty in raw mode
    size_t newPointsSinceLastTake() const;            // how many points extend()/end() appended since the last takeNewPoints()
    size_t takeNewPoints();                           // returns the count and resets it: the canvas rasterises exactly those segments
    std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> finish();           // after end(): the finished stroke; the input goes inactive
    int droppedSamples() const;                       // strictly backwards timestamps skipped this stroke (equal timestamps coalesce: the newer replaces the older)
};
}
```

Append to `tests/test_ink.cpp` (add `#include "InkInput.h"`):
```cpp
namespace {

/// A straight pen line from (0,100) to (200,100), `n` samples at `hz` (a 200 Hz pen is faster than PADD's 120), rising pressure.
std::vector<InkSample> syntheticLine(int n, double hz = 200.0)
{
    std::vector<InkSample> out;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
        out.push_back({QPointF(200.0 * i / (n - 1), 100.0), 0.3 + 0.6 * i / (n - 1), i / hz});
    return out;
}

} // namespace

TEST(InkInputTest, ModelledStrokeFollowsTheSamplesAndIsDenserThanTheInput)
{
    InkInput input;
    const auto samples = syntheticLine(40);
    input.begin(samples.front(), StrokeStyle{});
    for (size_t i = 1; i + 1 < samples.size(); ++i)
        input.extend(samples[i]);
    input.end(samples.back());
    const auto stroke = input.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(stroke);
    EXPECT_FALSE(input.active());
    EXPECT_GE(stroke->points.size(), samples.size()) << "min_output_rate 120 at 200 Hz input never drops below the input count";
    for (size_t i = 1; i < stroke->points.size(); ++i) {
        EXPECT_GE(stroke->points[i].x, stroke->points[i - 1].x - 1e-6) << "a straight line stays monotonic";
        EXPECT_NEAR(stroke->points[i].y, 100.0, 0.5);
        EXPECT_GE(stroke->points[i].pressure, 0.0);
        EXPECT_LE(stroke->points[i].pressure, 1.0);
        EXPECT_FALSE(std::isnan(stroke->points[i].x));
    }
    EXPECT_NEAR(stroke->points.back().x, 200.0, 0.5) << "the end-of-stroke iterations reach the pen-up position";
    EXPECT_NEAR(stroke->points.back().pressure, 0.9, 0.05);
}

TEST(InkInputTest, TheInputRateIsPaddsMeasured120Hz)
{
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(kInputRateHz, 120.0);
    EXPECT_EQ(kFractionalScaleOffset, QPointF(0.0, 0.0));
}

// R14: the spike saw 128 pairs of equal timestamps and none backwards at 120 Hz.
TEST(InkInputTest, APaddFeedAt120HzWithEqualTimestampPairsCoalescesWithoutDrops)
{
    InkInput input;
    const double dt = 1.0 / kInputRateHz;
    int pairs = 0;
    double t = 0, x = 0;
    input.begin({QPointF(0, 50), 0.4, 0.0}, StrokeStyle{});
    for (int i = 1; i <= 120; ++i) {
        t += dt;
        x += 1.5;
        input.extend({QPointF(x, 50), 0.4, t});
        if (i % 8 == 0) {
            input.extend({QPointF(x + 0.1, 50), 0.4, t}); // the compositor's equal-timestamp pair: the newer replaces the older
            ++pairs;
        }
    }
    input.end({QPointF(x + 1.5, 50), 0.4, t + dt});
    EXPECT_EQ(pairs, 15);
    EXPECT_EQ(input.droppedSamples(), 0) << "equal timestamps coalesce, they are not drops";
    const auto stroke = input.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(stroke);
    for (const StrokePoint &p : stroke->points) {
        EXPECT_FALSE(std::isnan(p.x));
        EXPECT_FALSE(std::isnan(p.y));
    }
    EXPECT_NEAR(stroke->points.back().x, x + 1.5, 1.0);
}

TEST(InkInputTest, NewPointCountingDrivesIncrementalRasterisation)
{
    InkInput input;
    const auto samples = syntheticLine(10);
    input.begin(samples[0], StrokeStyle{});
    EXPECT_EQ(input.takeNewPoints(), 1u) << "the down sample is a point";
    input.extend(samples[1]);
    input.extend(samples[2]);
    const size_t fresh = input.newPointsSinceLastTake();
    EXPECT_GT(fresh, 0u);
    EXPECT_EQ(input.takeNewPoints(), fresh);
    EXPECT_EQ(input.newPointsSinceLastTake(), 0u);
    EXPECT_EQ(input.stroke().points.size(), 1u + fresh);
}

TEST(InkInputTest, PredictionExtendsPastTheLastModelledPointAndClearsOnEnd)
{
    InkInput input;
    const auto samples = syntheticLine(20);
    input.begin(samples[0], StrokeStyle{});
    for (int i = 1; i < 10; ++i)
        input.extend(samples[i]);
    ASSERT_FALSE(input.prediction().empty());
    EXPECT_GT(input.prediction().back().x, input.stroke().points.back().x) << "the tail runs ahead along the line";
    input.end(samples[10]);
    EXPECT_TRUE(input.prediction().empty());
}

TEST(InkInputTest, NonMonotonicTimestampsAreSkippedAndCounted)
{
    InkInput input;
    auto samples = syntheticLine(10);
    input.begin(samples[0], StrokeStyle{});
    input.extend(samples[1]);
    InkSample back = samples[2];
    back.timeSeconds = samples[0].timeSeconds; // the clock went backwards
    input.extend(back);
    EXPECT_EQ(input.droppedSamples(), 1);
    input.extend(samples[3]);
    input.end(samples[4]);
    EXPECT_EQ(input.droppedSamples(), 1);
    EXPECT_TRUE(input.finish());
}

TEST(InkInputTest, PressureOutsideZeroOneIsClampedAndUnknownBecomesHalf)
{
    InkInput raw; // raw mode keeps every sample, so each pressure is visible
    raw.setRawInput(true);
    raw.begin({QPointF(0, 0), 7.0, 0.0}, StrokeStyle{});
    raw.extend({QPointF(10, 0), -1.0, 0.005});
    raw.end({QPointF(20, 0), 0.5, 0.010});
    const auto exact = raw.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(exact);
    ASSERT_EQ(exact->points.size(), 3u);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(exact->points[0].pressure, 1.0) << "7 clamps to 1";
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(exact->points[1].pressure, 0.5) << "-1 is 'unknown': a mid-width line";
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(exact->points[2].pressure, 0.5);

    InkInput input; // through the modeler every output stays in range
    input.begin({QPointF(0, 0), 7.0, 0.0}, StrokeStyle{});
    input.extend({QPointF(10, 0), -1.0, 0.005});
    input.end({QPointF(20, 0), 0.5, 0.010});
    const auto stroke = input.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(stroke);
    ASSERT_FALSE(stroke->points.empty());
    for (const StrokePoint &p : stroke->points) {
        EXPECT_GE(p.pressure, 0.0);
        EXPECT_LE(p.pressure, 1.0);
    }
}

TEST(InkInputTest, SamplesSharingATimestampCoalesceTheNewerReplacesTheOlder)
{
    InkInput raw; // raw mode: every accepted sample is a point, so the replacement is visible
    raw.setRawInput(true);
    raw.begin({QPointF(0, 0), 0.5, 0.000}, StrokeStyle{});
    raw.extend({QPointF(10, 0), 0.5, 0.005});
    raw.extend({QPointF(11, 0), 0.5, 0.005}); // the same millisecond: replaces the sample above
    raw.extend({QPointF(20, 0), 0.5, 0.010});
    raw.end({QPointF(30, 0), 0.5, 0.015});
    EXPECT_EQ(raw.droppedSamples(), 0) << "coalescing is not a drop";
    const auto exact = raw.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(exact);
    ASSERT_EQ(exact->points.size(), 4u);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(exact->points[1].x, 11.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(exact->points[2].x, 20.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(exact->points[3].x, 30.0);

    InkInput modelled; // through the modeler: finite, and it still ends at the pen-up
    modelled.begin({QPointF(0, 0), 0.5, 0.000}, StrokeStyle{});
    modelled.extend({QPointF(10, 0), 0.5, 0.005});
    modelled.extend({QPointF(11, 0), 0.5, 0.005});
    modelled.extend({QPointF(20, 0), 0.5, 0.010});
    modelled.end({QPointF(30, 0), 0.5, 0.015});
    const auto smoothed = modelled.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(smoothed);
    for (const StrokePoint &p : smoothed->points)
        EXPECT_FALSE(std::isnan(p.x));
    EXPECT_NEAR(smoothed->points.back().x, 30.0, 1.0);
}

// R14: PADD's pen peaks at 0.657 with a median of 0.21. A synthetic device with that range.
TEST(PressureNormaliserTest, BeforeCalibrationPressureIsUntouchedAndNothingIsReportedChanged)
{
    PressureNormaliser normaliser;
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.learnedMax(), 1.0);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.normalise(0.5), 0.5);
    for (int i = 0; i < 1000; ++i)
        normaliser.normalise(0.1); // weak samples (below 0.3 of the maximum) never count towards calibration
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.learnedMax(), 1.0);
    EXPECT_FALSE(normaliser.takeChanged());
}

TEST(PressureNormaliserTest, ADeviceThatTopsOutAt0657ReachesFullPressureOnceCalibrated)
{
    PressureNormaliser normaliser;
    for (int i = 0; i < PressureNormaliser::kCalibrationSamples + 100; ++i)
        normaliser.normalise(0.35 + 0.307 * (i % 100) / 99.0); // 0.35 .. 0.657, all above 0.3 of 1.0
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.learnedMax(), 0.657, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.normalise(0.657), 1.0, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.normalise(0.3285), 0.5, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_TRUE(normaliser.takeChanged());
    EXPECT_FALSE(normaliser.takeChanged()) << "reported once";
}

TEST(PressureNormaliserTest, OneSampleShortOfTheCalibrationCountChangesNothing)
{
    PressureNormaliser normaliser;
    for (int i = 0; i < PressureNormaliser::kCalibrationSamples - 1; ++i)
        normaliser.normalise(0.657);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.learnedMax(), 1.0);
    normaliser.normalise(0.657);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.learnedMax(), 0.657, 1e-9);
}

TEST(PressureNormaliserTest, ALaterHarderPressRaisesTheMaximumAndNeverLowersIt)
{
    PressureNormaliser normaliser;
    for (int i = 0; i < PressureNormaliser::kCalibrationSamples; ++i)
        normaliser.normalise(0.657);
    ASSERT_TRUE(normaliser.takeChanged());
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.normalise(0.7), 1.0);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.learnedMax(), 0.7, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_TRUE(normaliser.takeChanged());
    for (int i = 0; i < 1000; ++i)
        normaliser.normalise(0.35);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.learnedMax(), 0.7, 1e-9) << "light pressing never lowers it again";
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.normalise(0.35), 0.5, 1e-9);
}

TEST(PressureNormaliserTest, AStoredMaximumAppliesFromTheFirstSample)
{
    PressureNormaliser normaliser;
    normaliser.setLearnedMax(0.657);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.normalise(0.3285), 0.5, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_FALSE(normaliser.takeChanged()) << "restoring a stored value is not a change to persist";
    PressureNormaliser untouched;
    untouched.setLearnedMax(1.0); // the default: not learned yet, so it still calibrates
    for (int i = 0; i < PressureNormaliser::kCalibrationSamples; ++i)
        untouched.normalise(0.5);
    EXPECT_NEAR(untouched.learnedMax(), 0.5, 1e-9);
}

TEST(PressureNormaliserTest, UnknownAndOutOfRangePressurePassThroughSafely)
{
    PressureNormaliser normaliser;
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.normalise(-1.0), -1.0) << "unknown stays unknown: InkInput turns it into 0.5";
    EXPECT_TRUE(std::isnan(normaliser.normalise(std::nan(""))));
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(normaliser.normalise(2.0), 1.0);
    normaliser.setLearnedMax(0.0);
    EXPECT_NEAR(normaliser.learnedMax(), PressureNormaliser::kMinimumMax, 1e-12) << "a broken device cannot divide by ~0";
}

TEST(InkInputTest, ATapStaysWhereItLandedAndRawModeKeepsEverySample)
{
    InkInput tap;
    tap.begin({QPointF(5, 5), 0.8, 0.0}, StrokeStyle{});
    tap.end({QPointF(5, 5), 0.8, 0.004});
    const auto dot = tap.finish();
    ASSERT_TRUE(dot);
    ASSERT_GE(dot->points.size(), 1u);
    for (const StrokePoint &p : dot->points) {
        EXPECT_NEAR(p.x, 5.0, 0.05);
        EXPECT_NEAR(p.y, 5.0, 0.05);
    }

    InkInput raw;
    raw.setRawInput(true);
    const auto samples = syntheticLine(7);
    raw.begin(samples[0], StrokeStyle{});
    for (size_t i = 1; i + 1 < samples.size(); ++i)
        raw.extend(samples[i]);
    raw.end(samples.back());
    const auto stroke = raw.finish();
    ASSERT_EQ(stroke->points.size(), samples.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < samples.size(); ++i)
        EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(stroke->points[i].x, samples[i].position.x());
    EXPECT_TRUE(raw.prediction().empty());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkInput.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include <QPointF>
#include <Qt>

#include "InkTypes.h"

namespace noteAppC::ink {

// Spike results (tools/inkspike on PADD, 2026-10-22; the measured values are in
// the session's NOTES.md). Change only from a new measurement, never the names
// or shapes. PADD's pen has a tip only and its buttons do not work, so the held
// buttons are a bonus: every tool is also on the toolbar and on a key (Task 7).
inline constexpr Qt::MouseButton kHeldEraserButton = Qt::MiddleButton; ///< BTN_STYLUS
inline constexpr Qt::MouseButton kHeldLassoButton = Qt::RightButton;   ///< BTN_STYLUS2
inline constexpr bool kTrustEraserPointerType = true;                  ///< no eraser end seen on PADD; harmless where one exists
inline constexpr double kInputRateHz = 120.0;                          ///< PADD in contact; feeds only the modeler's min_output_rate
inline constexpr QPointF kFractionalScaleOffset{0.0, 0.0};             ///< device pixel ratio 2 showed no offset; subtracted from tablet widget positions by InkCanvas

struct InkSample {
    QPointF position; ///< document px
    double pressure = 0.5; ///< 0..1; anything else is clamped, a negative "unknown" becomes 0.5
    double timeSeconds = 0; ///< QInputEvent::timestamp() / 1000.0
};

/// R14 (the PADD spike: pressure never exceeds 0.657, median 0.21). Divides raw
/// pressure by a per-device learned maximum, so a pen that tops out low still
/// draws full-width lines. The canvas keeps one per device name (Task 7).
/// - Starts at 1.0: no change.
/// - After kCalibrationSamples samples above kStrongFraction of the current
///   maximum it takes the peak seen as the maximum. That can LOWER it from the
///   1.0 default, which is the point.
/// - From then on it only rises, when a harder press exceeds it.
/// - Unknown pressure (negative, NaN) passes through; InkInput makes it 0.5.
class PressureNormaliser {
public:
    static constexpr int kCalibrationSamples = 200;
    static constexpr double kStrongFraction = 0.3;
    static constexpr double kMinimumMax = 0.1; ///< a broken device reporting ~0 must not scale everything up to 1

    double normalise(double raw); ///< min(1, raw / learnedMax()); learns as a side effect
    double learnedMax() const { return m_learnedMax; }
    /// A stored value (setting ink.pressureByDevice, Task 16). Below 1.0 it counts as
    /// already calibrated; it is not reported by takeChanged().
    void setLearnedMax(double max);
    bool takeChanged(); ///< true once after learning changed the maximum: the canvas persists it

private:
    double m_learnedMax = 1.0;
    double m_peak = 0.0;
    int m_strongSamples = 0;
    bool m_calibrated = false;
    bool m_changed = false;
};

/// One stroke's samples through ink-stroke-modeler (spec section 7, Input):
/// wobble smoothing, the spring position model and a predicted tail, with
/// Rnote's parameters. Raw mode passes samples straight through.
class InkInput {
public:
    InkInput();
    ~InkInput();
    InkInput(const InkInput &) = delete;
    InkInput &operator=(const InkInput &) = delete;

    void setRawInput(bool raw) { m_raw = raw; }
    void begin(const InkSample &sample, const StrokeStyle &style);
    void extend(const InkSample &sample);
    void end(const InkSample &sample);
    bool active() const { return m_active; }

    const Stroke &stroke() const { return m_stroke; }
    const std::vector<StrokePoint> &prediction() const { return m_prediction; }
    size_t newPointsSinceLastTake() const { return m_newPoints; }
    size_t takeNewPoints();
    std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> finish();
    int droppedSamples() const { return m_dropped; } ///< strictly backwards timestamps skipped

private:
    struct Modeler; // hides the ink_stroke_modeler headers from the canvas
    void feed(const InkSample &sample, int eventType);
    void flushPending();
    void appendPoint(const QPointF &position, double pressure);
    static double cleanPressure(double pressure);

    std::unique_ptr<Modeler> m_modeler;
    Stroke m_stroke;
    std::vector<StrokePoint> m_prediction;
    size_t m_newPoints = 0;
    int m_dropped = 0;
    double m_lastTime = -1;   ///< the newest accepted sample's time, fed or pending
    InkSample m_pending;      ///< the newest move, held until the next sample shows it was not replaced
    bool m_hasPending = false;
    bool m_active = false;
    bool m_raw = false;
};

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkInput.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "InkInput.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

#include <ink_stroke_modeler/params.h>
#include <ink_stroke_modeler/stroke_modeler.h>
#include <ink_stroke_modeler/types.h>

namespace noteAppC::ink {

struct InkInput::Modeler {
    ::ink::StrokeModeler modeler;
    std::vector<::ink::Result> results;
};

namespace {

// Rnote's parameters (rnote-engine/src/pens/pensconfig/brushconfig.rs and
// rnote-study.md section 1.4), in seconds and document px. The wobble
// timeout is Rnote's 0.04 s preset, not derived from kInputRateHz (which only
// sets min_output_rate). The upstream
// defaults for the position modeler stay. `stylus_state_modeler_params.
// max_input_samples`, which Rnote overrides, does not exist at the pinned
// modeler commit; use_stroke_normal_projection is the current knob.
::ink::StrokeModelParams rnoteParams()
{
    ::ink::StrokeModelParams params;
    params.wobble_smoother_params.is_enabled = true;
    params.wobble_smoother_params.timeout = ::ink::Duration(0.04);
    params.wobble_smoother_params.speed_floor = 1.31;
    params.wobble_smoother_params.speed_ceiling = 1.44;
    params.sampling_params.min_output_rate = std::max(120.0, kInputRateHz);
    params.sampling_params.end_of_stroke_stopping_distance = 0.01;
    params.sampling_params.end_of_stroke_max_iterations = 20;
    params.sampling_params.max_outputs_per_call = 200;
    params.stylus_state_modeler_params.use_stroke_normal_projection = true;
    params.prediction_params = ::ink::StrokeEndPredictorParams{};
    return params;
}

} // namespace

double PressureNormaliser::normalise(double raw)
{
    if (std::isnan(raw) || raw < 0.0)
        return raw; // unknown: left for InkInput::cleanPressure
    m_peak = std::max(m_peak, raw);
    if (raw > kStrongFraction * m_learnedMax)
        ++m_strongSamples;
    if (!m_calibrated && m_strongSamples >= kCalibrationSamples) {
        m_learnedMax = std::max(kMinimumMax, m_peak);
        m_calibrated = true;
        m_changed = true;
    } else if (m_calibrated && m_peak > m_learnedMax) {
        m_learnedMax = m_peak; // a harder press than ever: raise, never lower
        m_changed = true;
    }
    return std::min(1.0, raw / m_learnedMax);
}

void PressureNormaliser::setLearnedMax(double max)
{
    m_learnedMax = std::max(kMinimumMax, max);
    m_calibrated = max < 1.0; // 1.0 is the default, not a learned value: such a device still calibrates
    m_peak = m_calibrated ? m_learnedMax : 0.0;
    m_strongSamples = 0;
}

bool PressureNormaliser::takeChanged()
{
    const bool changed = m_changed;
    m_changed = false;
    return changed;
}

InkInput::InkInput() : m_modeler(std::make_unique<Modeler>()) {}
InkInput::~InkInput() = default;

double InkInput::cleanPressure(double pressure)
{
    if (std::isnan(pressure) || pressure < 0.0)
        return 0.5; // "unknown" from a device without pressure: a mid-width line, not a hairline
    return std::min(pressure, 1.0);
}

void InkInput::appendPoint(const QPointF &position, double pressure)
{
    m_stroke.points.push_back({position.x(), position.y(), cleanPressure(pressure)});
    ++m_newPoints;
}

void InkInput::begin(const InkSample &sample, const StrokeStyle &style)
{
    m_stroke = Stroke{};
    m_stroke.style = style;
    m_prediction.clear();
    m_newPoints = 0;
    m_dropped = 0;
    m_lastTime = -1;
    m_hasPending = false;
    m_active = true;
    if (!m_raw) {
        // Reset returns a status only for bad params; ours are constants.
        (void)m_modeler->modeler.Reset(rnoteParams());
    }
    feed(sample, 0);
}

// A move is held back until the next sample arrives (one event, about 5 ms at
// 200 Hz, covered by the predicted tail): a sample that shares its
// predecessor's timestamp - a compositor batching events - must replace it,
// and the modeler cannot take a sample back. A strictly backwards timestamp is
// skipped: the modeler rejects time going backwards.
// ponytail: one event of latency on every move; feed at once and drop the newer duplicate if the bench says 5 ms matters.
void InkInput::extend(const InkSample &sample)
{
    if (!m_active)
        return;
    if (sample.timeSeconds < m_lastTime) {
        ++m_dropped;
        return;
    }
    if (sample.timeSeconds == m_lastTime) {
        if (m_hasPending)
            m_pending = sample; // the newer replaces the older
        else
            ++m_dropped;        // the same instant as the pen-down sample, which is already in the stroke
        return;
    }
    flushPending();
    m_pending = sample;
    m_hasPending = true;
    m_lastTime = sample.timeSeconds;
}

void InkInput::end(const InkSample &sample)
{
    if (!m_active)
        return;
    InkSample up = sample;
    if (up.timeSeconds < m_lastTime) {
        ++m_dropped; // a stale clock on the pen-up: it still ends the stroke, at the last time
        up.timeSeconds = m_lastTime;
    }
    if (m_hasPending && up.timeSeconds == m_pending.timeSeconds)
        m_hasPending = false; // the pen-up replaces a move of the same instant
    else
        flushPending();
    feed(up, 2);
    m_prediction.clear();
    m_active = false;
}

void InkInput::flushPending()
{
    if (!m_hasPending)
        return;
    m_hasPending = false;
    feed(m_pending, 1);
}

void InkInput::feed(const InkSample &sample, int eventType)
{
    if (m_raw) {
        appendPoint(sample.position, sample.pressure);
        m_lastTime = sample.timeSeconds;
        return;
    }
    ::ink::Input input;
    input.event_type = eventType == 0 ? ::ink::Input::EventType::kDown
                     : eventType == 1 ? ::ink::Input::EventType::kMove
                                      : ::ink::Input::EventType::kUp;
    input.position = {float(sample.position.x()), float(sample.position.y())};
    input.time = ::ink::Time(sample.timeSeconds);
    input.pressure = float(cleanPressure(sample.pressure));
    m_modeler->results.clear();
    const absl::Status status = m_modeler->modeler.Update(input, m_modeler->results);
    if (!status.ok()) {
        // A duplicate kDown, or a sample the modeler cannot place: keep the raw
        // point so the stroke never silently loses ink.
        appendPoint(sample.position, sample.pressure);
        ++m_dropped;
        m_lastTime = sample.timeSeconds;
        return;
    }
    for (const ::ink::Result &r : m_modeler->results)
        appendPoint(QPointF(r.position.x, r.position.y), r.pressure);
    if (eventType == 0 && m_stroke.points.empty())
        appendPoint(sample.position, sample.pressure); // the modeler emits the down point only with the first move
    m_lastTime = sample.timeSeconds;
    m_prediction.clear();
    if (eventType != 2) {
        m_modeler->results.clear();
        if (m_modeler->modeler.Predict(m_modeler->results).ok())
            for (const ::ink::Result &r : m_modeler->results)
                m_prediction.push_back({double(r.position.x), double(r.position.y), cleanPressure(r.pressure)});
    }
}

size_t InkInput::takeNewPoints()
{
    const size_t n = m_newPoints;
    m_newPoints = 0;
    return n;
}

std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> InkInput::finish()
{
    if (m_active)
        return nullptr;
    auto done = std::make_shared<Stroke>(std::move(m_stroke));
    m_stroke = Stroke{};
    m_newPoints = 0;
    return done;
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

### Task 7: InkCanvas: one page, pen, eraser, undo/redo

**Spike results used here (R14, PADD):** `kHeldEraserButton`, `kHeldLassoButton`, `kTrustEraserPointerType`, `kFractionalScaleOffset` from `InkInput.h`. PADD's pen has no eraser end and no working buttons, so the held buttons stay as bonus defaults and **every tool is reachable from the toolbar (Task 16) and by a key (here: P pen, E eraser, L lasso, S shape, T text)**. Pressure goes through one `PressureNormaliser` (Task 6) per device name.

- Produces:
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
class InkCanvas : public QWidget {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    enum class Tool { Pen, Eraser, Lasso, Shape, Text }; // Lasso/Shape/Text are Track S1 Tasks 13-15; here they behave as Pen
    Q_ENUM(Tool)
    explicit InkCanvas(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    InkDocument &document(); const InkDocument &document() const;
    void documentReplaced();                 // after a load into document(): resize, repaint
    double zoom() const; void setZoom(double zoom);  // 0.25..8
    Tool tool() const; void setTool(Tool tool);        // emits toolChanged when it changes; the keys P, E, L, S, T call it (R14)
    StrokeStyle penStyle() const; void setPenStyle(const StrokeStyle &style);
    double eraserWidth() const; void setEraserWidth(double documentPx);
    bool drawWithMouse() const; void setDrawWithMouse(bool on);
    void setRawInput(bool raw);
    QPointF toDocument(const QPointF &widgetPos) const; QPointF toWidget(const QPointF &documentPos) const;
    QSize sizeHint() const override;
    bool isDrawing() const;
    void setReadOnly(bool on); bool isReadOnly() const;  // ignores pen, mouse drawing and the edit keys; touch pan, wheel and zoom still work
    // R14: pressure normalisation, one PressureNormaliser per QPointingDevice::name() (tablet pressure only: a mouse stroke stays 0.5)
    double learnedPressure(const QString &device) const;               // 1.0 for a device not seen and not stored
    void setLearnedPressure(const QString &device, double max);        // a stored value (Task 16 reads ink.pressureByDevice)
    void setPressureSeed(double seed);                                 // for a device with no stored value; setting ink.pressureMax, default 1.0 = learn it
public slots:
    bool undo(); bool redo();
signals:
    void documentEdited();                   // after every commit(), undo, redo
    void toolChanged(Tool tool);
    void learnedPressureChanged(const QString &device, double max); // learning moved a device's maximum: the owner persists it
    void zoomChanged(double zoom);
    void panRequested(QPoint deltaWidgetPx); // single-finger touch drag; the scroll area applies it
protected:
    void tabletEvent(QTabletEvent *event) override;
    void mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *event) override;
    void mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent *event) override;
    void mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *event) override;
    bool event(QEvent *event) override;      // QTouchEvent pan + QPinchGesture zoom
    void keyPressEvent(QKeyEvent *event) override;
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event) override;
};
}
```

`tests/InkTestSupport.h` (new; shared by `test_ink`, Task 8's window tests and Task 21's `test_hwfont`, so no test file copies these helpers):
```cpp
#pragma once

// Synthetic pen and touch input and a paint-rect recorder for the ink tests.
#include <QApplication>
#include <QEvent>
#include <QPaintEvent>
#include <QPointingDevice>
#include <QRect>
#include <QTabletEvent>
#include <QWidget>

namespace inktest {

inline QPointingDevice *stylus(QPointingDevice::PointerType type = QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen)
{
    static QPointingDevice pen(QStringLiteral("test pen"), 1, QInputDevice::DeviceType::Stylus, QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen,
                               QInputDevice::Capability::Position | QInputDevice::Capability::Pressure, 1, 2);
    static QPointingDevice eraser(QStringLiteral("test eraser"), 2, QInputDevice::DeviceType::Stylus,
                                  QPointingDevice::PointerType::Eraser,
                                  QInputDevice::Capability::Position | QInputDevice::Capability::Pressure, 1, 2);
    return type == QPointingDevice::PointerType::Eraser ? &eraser : &pen;
}

inline QPointingDevice *finger()
{
    static QPointingDevice touch(QStringLiteral("test touch"), 3, QInputDevice::DeviceType::TouchScreen,
                                 QPointingDevice::PointerType::Finger, QInputDevice::Capability::Position, 10, 0);
    return &touch;
}

inline void tablet(QWidget *w, QEvent::Type type, QPointF pos, double pressure, qint64 ms,
                   Qt::MouseButtons buttons = Qt::LeftButton,
                   QPointingDevice::PointerType pointer = QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen)
{
    QTabletEvent event(type, stylus(pointer), pos, w->mapToGlobal(pos.toPoint()), pressure, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, Qt::NoModifier,
                       type == QEvent::TabletMove ? Qt::NoButton : Qt::LeftButton, buttons);
    event.setTimestamp(quint64(ms));
    QApplication::sendEvent(w, &event);
}

/// A pen stroke from `a` to `b` in widget px, 10 samples 5 ms apart, starting at `ms`, at a constant raw `pressure`.
inline void penStroke(QWidget *w, QPointF a, QPointF b, qint64 ms, Qt::MouseButtons buttons = Qt::LeftButton,
                      QPointingDevice::PointerType pointer = QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen, double pressure = 0.5)
{
    tablet(w, QEvent::TabletPress, a, pressure, ms, buttons, pointer);
    for (int i = 1; i < 9; ++i)
        tablet(w, QEvent::TabletMove, a + (b - a) * (i / 9.0), pressure, ms + i * 5, buttons, pointer);
    tablet(w, QEvent::TabletRelease, b, pressure, ms + 45, Qt::NoButton, pointer);
}

/// Records the rect of every paint event `watched` receives: what a repaint
/// actually covered, without a test hook in the widget under test.
class PaintRects : public QObject {
public:
    explicit PaintRects(QWidget *watched) : QObject(watched) { watched->installEventFilter(this); }
    QList<QRect> rects;
    QRect united() const
    {
        QRect all;
        for (const QRect &r : rects)
            all |= r;
        return all;
    }

protected:
    bool eventFilter(QObject *, QEvent *event) override
    {
        if (event->type() == QEvent::Paint)
            rects << static_cast<QPaintEvent *>(event)->rect();
        return false;
    }
};

} // namespace inktest
```

```cpp
namespace {

struct CanvasFixture {
    InkCanvas canvas;
    CanvasFixture()
    {
        DocumentSettings settings;
        settings.format = {400, 300, 96};
        settings.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 400, 300);
        settings.layout = LayoutKind::Fixed;
        canvas.document().setSettings(settings);
        canvas.document().markSaved();
        canvas.documentReplaced();
        canvas.resize(400, 300);
        canvas.show();
        QTest::qWaitForWindowExposed(&canvas);
    }
};

} // namespace

TEST(InkCanvasTest, APenStrokeBecomesOneCommittedItemWithTheCurrentStyle)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    StrokeStyle style;
    style.width = 3.0;
    style.color = QColor(0x00, 0x50, 0xc0);
    f.canvas.setPenStyle(style);
    QSignalSpy edited(&f.canvas, &InkCanvas::documentEdited);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 20), QPointF(120, 60), 1000);
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    const Item &item = f.canvas.document().items()[0];
    EXPECT_EQ(item.type, Item::Type::Stroke);
    EXPECT_EQ(item.stroke->style, style);
    EXPECT_GE(item.stroke->points.size(), 10u);
    EXPECT_NEAR(item.stroke->points.front().x, 20.0, 1.0);
    EXPECT_NEAR(item.stroke->points.back().x, 120.0, 1.0);
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().canUndo());
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().isModified());
    EXPECT_EQ(edited.count(), 1);
    EXPECT_FALSE(f.canvas.isDrawing());
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, ZoomMapsWidgetToDocumentCoordinates)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    f.canvas.setZoom(2.0);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.sizeHint(), QSize(800, 600));
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.toDocument(QPointF(100, 50)), QPointF(50, 25));
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.toWidget(QPointF(50, 25)), QPointF(100, 50));
    f.canvas.resize(f.canvas.sizeHint());
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 100), QPointF(300, 100), 1000);
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    EXPECT_NEAR(f.canvas.document().items()[0].stroke->points.front().x, 50.0, 1.0) << "stored in document px";
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, TheEraserRemovesWholeStrokesItTouches)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 50), QPointF(200, 50), 1000);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 200), QPointF(200, 200), 2000);
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 2u);
    f.canvas.setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 30), QPointF(100, 70), 3000); // crosses the first stroke only
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    EXPECT_NEAR(f.canvas.document().items()[0].stroke->points.front().y, 200.0, 1.0);
    f.canvas.setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Pen);
    // The pen's eraser end erases while the pen tool is selected (kTrustEraserPointerType stays
    // true: PADD has no eraser end, other pens do). The plain tip with the Eraser tool, above,
    // is the way in that needs no hardware; this branch only keeps the test honest if the
    // constant is ever flipped.
    if (kTrustEraserPointerType) {
        penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 180), QPointF(100, 220), 4000, Qt::LeftButton, QPointingDevice::PointerType::Eraser);
    } else {
        f.canvas.setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser);
        penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 180), QPointF(100, 220), 4000);
        f.canvas.setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Pen);
    }
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 0u);
    // A held barrel button erases too.
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 100), QPointF(200, 100), 5000);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 80), QPointF(100, 120), 6000, Qt::LeftButton | kHeldEraserButton);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 0u);
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, UndoRedoFromTheKeyboard)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 50), QPointF(200, 50), 1000);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 100), QPointF(200, 100), 2000);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_Z, Qt::ControlModifier);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_Z, Qt::ControlModifier | Qt::ShiftModifier);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 2u);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_Z, Qt::ControlModifier);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_Y, Qt::ControlModifier);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 2u);
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, EraseEverythingAutosaveThenUndoBringsTheStrokesBack)
{
    // Review Focus item 5.
    CanvasFixture f;
    QTemporaryDir tmp;
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 50), QPointF(200, 50), 1000);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 100), QPointF(200, 100), 2000);
    f.canvas.setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 30), QPointF(100, 120), 3000); // crosses both
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 0u);
    ASSERT_TRUE(saveInkFile(tmp.filePath(QStringLiteral("n.ink")), f.canvas.document()));
    f.canvas.document().markSaved(); // what InkView::save does
    EXPECT_FALSE(f.canvas.document().isModified());
    ASSERT_TRUE(f.canvas.undo());
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 2u);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().itemsIntersecting(QRectF(0, 0, 400, 300)).size(), 2u) << "the spatial grid came back";
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().isModified()) << "the file holds the erased state";
    const QImage frame = f.canvas.grab().toImage();
    EXPECT_NE(frame.pixelColor(100, 50), QColor(Qt::white)) << "the canvas repainted the strokes";
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, MouseDrawsOnlyWithDrawWithMouseOn)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    QTest::mousePress(&f.canvas, Qt::LeftButton, Qt::NoModifier, QPoint(20, 20));
    QTest::mouseMove(&f.canvas, QPoint(100, 60));
    QTest::mouseRelease(&f.canvas, Qt::LeftButton, Qt::NoModifier, QPoint(100, 60));
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().items().empty());
    f.canvas.setDrawWithMouse(true);
    QTest::mousePress(&f.canvas, Qt::LeftButton, Qt::NoModifier, QPoint(20, 20));
    QTest::mouseMove(&f.canvas, QPoint(60, 40));
    QTest::mouseMove(&f.canvas, QPoint(100, 60));
    QTest::mouseRelease(&f.canvas, Qt::LeftButton, Qt::NoModifier, QPoint(100, 60));
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    for (const StrokePoint &p : f.canvas.document().items()[0].stroke->points)
        EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(p.pressure, 0.5) << "a mouse has no pressure";
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, TouchPansAndNeverDraws)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    QSignalSpy pans(&f.canvas, &InkCanvas::panRequested);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).press(0, QPoint(100, 100), &f.canvas);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).move(0, QPoint(130, 90), &f.canvas);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).release(0, QPoint(130, 90), &f.canvas);
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().items().empty());
    ASSERT_GE(pans.count(), 1);
    EXPECT_EQ(pans.last().at(0).toPoint(), QPoint(30, -10));
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, TouchPanFollowsTheFingerWhileTheScrollAreaMovesTheCanvas)
{
    // The scroll area moves the canvas under the finger. Widget-local positions
    // would then report no movement and the pan would oscillate; global ones do not.
    CanvasFixture f;
    QSignalSpy pans(&f.canvas, &InkCanvas::panRequested);
    const QPoint origin = f.canvas.mapToGlobal(QPoint(0, 0));
    const auto local = [&](QPoint global) { return f.canvas.mapFromGlobal(global); };
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).press(0, local(origin + QPoint(100, 100)), &f.canvas);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).move(0, local(origin + QPoint(130, 90)), &f.canvas);
    ASSERT_GE(pans.count(), 1);
    EXPECT_EQ(pans.last().at(0).toPoint(), QPoint(30, -10));
    f.canvas.move(f.canvas.pos() + QPoint(30, -10)); // what the scroll area does: the content follows the finger
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).move(0, local(origin + QPoint(160, 80)), &f.canvas);
    EXPECT_EQ(pans.last().at(0).toPoint(), QPoint(30, -10)) << "the finger moved 30 px on screen again";
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).release(0, local(origin + QPoint(160, 80)), &f.canvas);
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().items().empty());
}

// R14: the PADD pen never reports above 0.657. After the calibration samples the strokes reach full pressure.
TEST(InkCanvasTest, PressureIsNormalisedByTheLearnedMaximumOfTheDevice)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    QSignalSpy learned(&f.canvas, &InkCanvas::learnedPressureChanged);
    for (int i = 0; i < 25; ++i) // 10 samples a stroke: the 200 calibration samples are done in the 20th
        penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 10 + 10 * i), QPointF(200, 10 + 10 * i), 1000 + i * 100, Qt::LeftButton,
                  QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen, 0.657);
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 25u);
    EXPECT_NEAR(f.canvas.document().items().front().stroke->points.front().pressure, 0.657, 0.05) << "before calibration: raw";
    EXPECT_NEAR(f.canvas.document().items().back().stroke->points.front().pressure, 1.0, 0.05) << "after: full pressure";
    ASSERT_GE(learned.count(), 1);
    EXPECT_EQ(learned.last().at(0).toString(), QStringLiteral("test pen"));
    EXPECT_NEAR(learned.last().at(1).toDouble(), 0.657, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_NEAR(f.canvas.learnedPressure(QStringLiteral("test pen")), 0.657, 1e-9);
    EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(f.canvas.learnedPressure(QStringLiteral("some other pen")), 1.0);
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, AStoredMaximumOrTheSeedAppliesFromTheFirstStroke)
{
    {
        CanvasFixture f;
        f.canvas.setLearnedPressure(QStringLiteral("test pen"), 0.657);
        penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 20), QPointF(120, 20), 1000, Qt::LeftButton, QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen, 0.3285);
        ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
        EXPECT_NEAR(f.canvas.document().items()[0].stroke->points.front().pressure, 0.5, 0.05);
    }
    CanvasFixture g; // the seed (ink.pressureMax) is for a device nothing is stored for
    g.canvas.setPressureSeed(0.657);
    penStroke(&g.canvas, QPointF(20, 20), QPointF(120, 20), 1000, Qt::LeftButton, QPointingDevice::PointerType::Pen, 0.3285);
    ASSERT_EQ(g.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    EXPECT_NEAR(g.canvas.document().items()[0].stroke->points.front().pressure, 0.5, 0.05);
    g.canvas.setDrawWithMouse(true); // a mouse has no pressure and is not scaled
    QTest::mousePress(&g.canvas, Qt::LeftButton, Qt::NoModifier, QPoint(20, 80));
    QTest::mouseMove(&g.canvas, QPoint(60, 80));
    QTest::mouseRelease(&g.canvas, Qt::LeftButton, Qt::NoModifier, QPoint(100, 80));
    ASSERT_EQ(g.canvas.document().items().size(), 2u);
    for (const StrokePoint &p : g.canvas.document().items()[1].stroke->points)
        EXPECT_DOUBLE_EQ(p.pressure, 0.5);
}

// R14: PADD's pen has no eraser end and no working buttons, so nothing may need them.
TEST(InkCanvasTest, EveryToolIsReachableFromTheKeyboardAndATipAloneDrawsAndErases)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    QSignalSpy changed(&f.canvas, &InkCanvas::toolChanged);
    const std::pair<int, InkCanvas::Tool> keys[] = {{Qt::Key_E, InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser}, {Qt::Key_L, InkCanvas::Tool::Lasso},
                                                    {Qt::Key_S, InkCanvas::Tool::Shape}, {Qt::Key_T, InkCanvas::Tool::Text},
                                                    {Qt::Key_P, InkCanvas::Tool::Pen}};
    for (const auto &[key, tool] : keys) {
        QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key(key));
        EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.tool(), tool);
    }
    EXPECT_EQ(changed.count(), 5);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_P); // already the pen
    EXPECT_EQ(changed.count(), 5) << "no change, no signal";
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 50), QPointF(200, 50), 1000); // the tip alone: no button, no eraser end
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_E);
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(100, 30), QPointF(100, 70), 2000);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 0u);
    f.canvas.setReadOnly(true);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_P);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.tool(), InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser) << "a read-only canvas ignores the tool keys";
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, LiveStrokeRepaintsOnlyTheNewSegment)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    PaintRects paints(&f.canvas);
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletPress, QPointF(20, 20), 0.5, 1000);
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(40, 20), 0.5, 1005);
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(50, 20), 0.5, 1010);
    QCoreApplication::processEvents(); // the first paints (the first is the whole canvas)
    paints.rects.clear();
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(60, 20), 0.5, 1015);
    QCoreApplication::processEvents();
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.isDrawing());
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.document().items().empty()) << "nothing is committed until pen-up";
    ASSERT_FALSE(paints.rects.isEmpty());
    EXPECT_LT(paints.united().width(), f.canvas.width() / 2) << "a 10 px segment never repaints the canvas";
    EXPECT_LT(paints.united().height(), f.canvas.height() / 2);
    // The real invariant lives in the bench (Track S1 Task 18): a 200 Hz feed costs under 1 ms per sample.
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, PenUpRepaintsWhereThePredictedTailWas)
{
    // The tail is painted straight onto the widget; pen-up must repaint its
    // area or it stays on screen as a ghost. (T6's PredictionExtends... test
    // pins that the tail exists for a steady line; with no tail this is vacuous.)
    CanvasFixture f;
    PaintRects paints(&f.canvas);
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletPress, QPointF(20, 100), 0.5, 1000);
    for (int i = 1; i <= 8; ++i)
        tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(20 + 10 * i, 100), 0.5, 1000 + 5 * i);
    QCoreApplication::processEvents();
    paints.rects.clear();
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(110, 100), 0.5, 1045);
    QCoreApplication::processEvents();
    ASSERT_FALSE(paints.rects.isEmpty());
    const int tailRight = paints.united().right();
    paints.rects.clear();
    tablet(&f.canvas, QEvent::TabletRelease, QPointF(110, 100), 0.5, 1050, Qt::NoButton);
    QCoreApplication::processEvents();
    ASSERT_FALSE(paints.rects.isEmpty());
    EXPECT_GE(paints.united().right(), tailRight) << "pen-up repaints out to where the tail was";
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, TabletTrackingIsOnSoHoverReachesTheCanvas)
{
    InkCanvas canvas;
    EXPECT_TRUE(canvas.hasTabletTracking());
}

TEST(InkCanvasTest, AReadOnlyCanvasIgnoresThePenAndTheKeysButStillPans)
{
    CanvasFixture f;
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 50), QPointF(200, 50), 1000);
    ASSERT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    f.canvas.setReadOnly(true);
    EXPECT_TRUE(f.canvas.isEnabled()) << "disabling the widget would swallow wheel and touch too";
    penStroke(&f.canvas, QPointF(20, 100), QPointF(200, 100), 2000);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u);
    QTest::keyClick(&f.canvas, Qt::Key_Z, Qt::ControlModifier);
    EXPECT_EQ(f.canvas.document().items().size(), 1u) << "undo is an edit";
    EXPECT_FALSE(f.canvas.undo());
    QSignalSpy pans(&f.canvas, &InkCanvas::panRequested);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).press(0, QPoint(100, 100), &f.canvas);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).move(0, QPoint(130, 90), &f.canvas);
    QTest::touchEvent(&f.canvas, finger()).release(0, QPoint(130, 90), &f.canvas);
    EXPECT_GE(pans.count(), 1);
}
```
