- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkCanvas.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <QImage>
#include <QWidget>

#include <map>

#include "InkDocument.h"
#include "InkInput.h"

namespace noteAppC::ink {

/// The drawing surface (spec section 7, Canvas): a plain QWidget sized to the
/// document at the zoom, living inside InkView's QScrollArea. Tablet events
/// do not route through QAbstractScrollArea::viewportEvent, which is why the
/// canvas is the widget and not the scroll area. Paints committed items from
/// the document (Track S1 Task 11 adds the tile cache) and the live stroke
/// from an overlay image that only the newest segment is rasterised into.
class InkCanvas : public QWidget {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    enum class Tool { Pen, Eraser, Lasso, Shape, Text };
    Q_ENUM(Tool)

    explicit InkCanvas(QWidget *parent = nullptr);

    InkDocument &document() { return m_document; }
    const InkDocument &document() const { return m_document; }
    void documentReplaced();

    double zoom() const { return m_zoom; }
    void setZoom(double zoom);
    Tool tool() const { return m_tool; }
    void setTool(Tool tool);
    StrokeStyle penStyle() const { return m_penStyle; }
    void setPenStyle(const StrokeStyle &style) { m_penStyle = style; }
    double eraserWidth() const { return m_eraserWidth; }
    void setEraserWidth(double documentPx) { m_eraserWidth = documentPx; }
    bool drawWithMouse() const { return m_drawWithMouse; }
    void setDrawWithMouse(bool on) { m_drawWithMouse = on; }
    void setRawInput(bool raw) { m_input.setRawInput(raw); }

    QPointF toDocument(const QPointF &widgetPos) const { return widgetPos / m_zoom; }
    QPointF toWidget(const QPointF &documentPos) const { return documentPos * m_zoom; }
    QSize sizeHint() const override;
    bool isDrawing() const { return m_drawing != Drawing::None; }
    /// A read-only canvas (a file written by a newer Notolith) ignores the pen,
    /// mouse drawing and the edit keys but keeps touch pan, wheel and zoom:
    /// disabling the widget would block those too.
    void setReadOnly(bool on) { m_readOnly = on; }
    bool isReadOnly() const { return m_readOnly; }

    /// R14: pressure is divided by a learned maximum per QPointingDevice::name()
    /// (PressureNormaliser, InkInput.h). Tablet pressure only: a mouse stroke stays 0.5.
    double learnedPressure(const QString &device) const;
    void setLearnedPressure(const QString &device, double max);
    void setPressureSeed(double seed) { m_pressureSeed = seed; } ///< for a device nothing is stored for

public slots:
    bool undo();
    bool redo();

signals:
    void documentEdited();
    void toolChanged(Tool tool);
    void learnedPressureChanged(const QString &device, double max);
    void zoomChanged(double zoom);
    void panRequested(QPoint deltaWidgetPx);

protected:
    void tabletEvent(QTabletEvent *event) override;
    void mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *event) override;
    void mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent *event) override;
    void mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *event) override;
    bool event(QEvent *event) override;
    void keyPressEvent(QKeyEvent *event) override;
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event) override;

private:
    enum class Drawing { None, Pen, Eraser };
    void pressAt(const QPointF &widgetPos, double pressure, double seconds, bool erase);
    void moveTo(const QPointF &widgetPos, double pressure, double seconds);
    void releaseAt(const QPointF &widgetPos, double pressure, double seconds);
    void rasteriseNewSegments();
    void eraseAround(const QPointF &documentPos);
    void finishEdit();
    QRect widgetRect(const QRectF &documentRect) const;
    PressureNormaliser &normaliserFor(const QString &device);

    InkDocument m_document;
    InkInput m_input;
    Tool m_tool = Tool::Pen;
    StrokeStyle m_penStyle;
    double m_eraserWidth = 20.0;
    double m_zoom = 1.0;
    bool m_drawWithMouse = false;
    Drawing m_drawing = Drawing::None;
    bool m_tabletActive = false; ///< Qt synthesises mouse events from unaccepted tablet events; we accept, this guards the rest
    QImage m_live;               ///< the live stroke, widget px * dpr, transparent
    QRectF m_lastPrediction;     ///< document px: the predicted tail as last painted; repainted when it moves and at pen-up
    bool m_erasedSomething = false;
    bool m_readOnly = false;
    QPointF m_lastTouch;
    bool m_touchPanning = false;
    std::map<QString, PressureNormaliser> m_pressure; ///< by QPointingDevice::name()
    double m_pressureSeed = 1.0;
};

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/core/InkCanvas.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "InkCanvas.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <map>
#include <utility>

#include <QGestureEvent>
#include <QKeyEvent>
#include <QMouseEvent>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPaintEvent>
#include <QPinchGesture>
#include <QTabletEvent>
#include <QTouchEvent>

#include "StrokeOutline.h"

namespace noteAppC::ink {

InkCanvas::InkCanvas(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setFocusPolicy(Qt::StrongFocus);
    setTabletTracking(true); // hover events while the pen is near the tablet (spec section 7, Sources)
    setAttribute(Qt::WA_AcceptTouchEvents);
    setAttribute(Qt::WA_OpaquePaintEvent);
    grabGesture(Qt::PinchGesture);
    setCursor(Qt::CrossCursor);
    documentReplaced();
}

QSize InkCanvas::sizeHint() const
{
    const QRectF extent = m_document.settings().extent;
    return QSize(int(std::ceil(extent.width() * m_zoom)), int(std::ceil(extent.height() * m_zoom)));
}

void InkCanvas::documentReplaced()
{
    m_live = QImage();
    resize(sizeHint());
    updateGeometry();
    update();
}

void InkCanvas::setZoom(double zoom)
{
    zoom = std::clamp(zoom, 0.25, 8.0);
    if (qFuzzyCompare(zoom, m_zoom))
        return;
    m_zoom = zoom;
    m_live = QImage();
    resize(sizeHint());
    updateGeometry();
    update();
    emit zoomChanged(m_zoom);
}

QRect InkCanvas::widgetRect(const QRectF &documentRect) const
{
    return QRectF(documentRect.topLeft() * m_zoom, documentRect.size() * m_zoom).toAlignedRect().adjusted(-2, -2, 2, 2);
}

void InkCanvas::setTool(Tool tool)
{
    if (tool == m_tool)
        return;
    m_tool = tool;
    emit toolChanged(m_tool);
}

PressureNormaliser &InkCanvas::normaliserFor(const QString &device)
{
    auto it = m_pressure.find(device);
    if (it == m_pressure.end()) {
        it = m_pressure.emplace(device, PressureNormaliser{}).first;
        it->second.setLearnedMax(m_pressureSeed); // 1.0 = nothing known: learn it
    }
    return it->second;
}

double InkCanvas::learnedPressure(const QString &device) const
{
    const auto it = m_pressure.find(device);
    return it == m_pressure.end() ? 1.0 : it->second.learnedMax();
}

void InkCanvas::setLearnedPressure(const QString &device, double max)
{
    m_pressure[device].setLearnedMax(max);
}

// --- input -----------------------------------------------------------------

void InkCanvas::tabletEvent(QTabletEvent *event)
{
    event->accept(); // no synthesised mouse events for this pen
    if (m_readOnly)
        return;
    const double seconds = event->timestamp() / 1000.0;
    // R14: this device's learned maximum turns raw pressure into 0..1 (PADD never exceeds 0.657).
    const QString device = event->pointingDevice() ? event->pointingDevice()->name() : QString();
    PressureNormaliser &normaliser = normaliserFor(device);
    const double pressure = normaliser.normalise(event->pressure());
    struct Persist { // after the switch: tell the owner once when learning moved the maximum
        InkCanvas *canvas; PressureNormaliser &normaliser; const QString &device;
        ~Persist() { if (normaliser.takeChanged()) emit canvas->learnedPressureChanged(device, normaliser.learnedMax()); }
    } persist{this, normaliser, device};
    const QPointF pos = event->position() - kFractionalScaleOffset;
    const bool eraseByDevice = kTrustEraserPointerType && event->pointerType() == QPointingDevice::PointerType::Eraser;
    const bool eraseByButton = event->buttons().testFlag(kHeldEraserButton);
    switch (event->type()) {
    case QEvent::TabletPress:
        m_tabletActive = true;
        pressAt(pos, pressure, seconds, eraseByDevice || eraseByButton || m_tool == Tool::Eraser);
        break;
    case QEvent::TabletMove:
        if (m_tabletActive)
            moveTo(pos, pressure, seconds);
        break;
    case QEvent::TabletRelease:
        if (m_tabletActive)
            releaseAt(pos, pressure, seconds);
        m_tabletActive = false;
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

void InkCanvas::mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *event)
{
    if (m_readOnly || !m_drawWithMouse || m_tabletActive || event->button() != Qt::LeftButton)
        return QWidget::mousePressEvent(event);
    pressAt(event->position(), 0.5, event->timestamp() / 1000.0, m_tool == Tool::Eraser);
}

void InkCanvas::mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent *event)
{
    if (m_drawing == Drawing::None || m_tabletActive)
        return QWidget::mouseMoveEvent(event);
    moveTo(event->position(), 0.5, event->timestamp() / 1000.0);
}

void InkCanvas::mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *event)
{
    if (m_drawing == Drawing::None || m_tabletActive)
        return QWidget::mouseReleaseEvent(event);
    releaseAt(event->position(), 0.5, event->timestamp() / 1000.0);
}

bool InkCanvas::event(QEvent *event)
{
    switch (event->type()) {
    case QEvent::TouchBegin:
    case QEvent::TouchUpdate:
    case QEvent::TouchEnd: {
        auto *touch = static_cast<QTouchEvent *>(event);
        touch->accept();
        const auto &points = touch->points();
        if (points.size() == 1) {
            const QPointF now = points[0].globalPosition(); // not position(): the scroll area moves this widget under the finger
            if (event->type() == QEvent::TouchBegin) {
                m_touchPanning = true;
            } else if (m_touchPanning && event->type() == QEvent::TouchUpdate) {
                emit panRequested((now - m_lastTouch).toPoint());
            }
            m_lastTouch = now;
        }
        if (event->type() == QEvent::TouchEnd)
            m_touchPanning = false;
        return true; // touch is pan and zoom, never ink (spec section 7, Input)
    }
    case QEvent::Gesture: {
        auto *gesture = static_cast<QGestureEvent *>(event);
        if (auto *pinch = static_cast<QPinchGesture *>(gesture->gesture(Qt::PinchGesture))) {
            if (pinch->changeFlags().testFlag(QPinchGesture::ScaleFactorChanged))
                setZoom(m_zoom * pinch->scaleFactor());
            m_touchPanning = false;
        }
        return true;
    }
    default:
        return QWidget::event(event);
    }
}

namespace {
bool toolForKey(int key, InkCanvas::Tool *tool)
{
    switch (key) {
    case Qt::Key_P: *tool = InkCanvas::Tool::Pen; return true;
    case Qt::Key_E: *tool = InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser; return true;
    case Qt::Key_L: *tool = InkCanvas::Tool::Lasso; return true;
    case Qt::Key_S: *tool = InkCanvas::Tool::Shape; return true;
    case Qt::Key_T: *tool = InkCanvas::Tool::Text; return true;
    default: return false;
    }
}
} // namespace

void InkCanvas::keyPressEvent(QKeyEvent *event)
{
    InkCanvas::Tool keyTool = InkCanvas::Tool::Pen;
    if (m_readOnly) {
        QWidget::keyPressEvent(event);
    } else if (event->matches(QKeySequence::Undo)) {
        undo();
    } else if (event->matches(QKeySequence::Redo) || (event->key() == Qt::Key_Y && event->modifiers() == Qt::ControlModifier)) {
        redo();
    } else if (event->modifiers() == Qt::NoModifier && toolForKey(event->key(), &keyTool)) {
        setTool(keyTool); // R14: every tool without pen hardware (PADD's pen has no eraser end and dead buttons)
    } else {
        QWidget::keyPressEvent(event);
    }
}

// --- the stroke in flight ----------------------------------------------------

void InkCanvas::pressAt(const QPointF &widgetPos, double pressure, double seconds, bool erase)
{
    if (m_drawing != Drawing::None)
        releaseAt(widgetPos, pressure, seconds); // a press without the release: close the old stroke first
    setFocus(Qt::MouseFocusReason);
    if (erase) {
        m_drawing = Drawing::Eraser;
        m_erasedSomething = false;
        eraseAround(toDocument(widgetPos));
        return;
    }
    m_drawing = Drawing::Pen;
    if (m_live.size() != size() * devicePixelRatio()) {
        m_live = QImage(size() * devicePixelRatio(), QImage::Format_ARGB32_Premultiplied);
        m_live.setDevicePixelRatio(devicePixelRatio());
    }
    m_live.fill(Qt::transparent);
    m_input.begin({toDocument(widgetPos), pressure, seconds}, m_penStyle);
    rasteriseNewSegments();
}

void InkCanvas::moveTo(const QPointF &widgetPos, double pressure, double seconds)
{
    if (m_drawing == Drawing::Eraser) {
        eraseAround(toDocument(widgetPos));
        return;
    }
    m_input.extend({toDocument(widgetPos), pressure, seconds});
    rasteriseNewSegments();
}

void InkCanvas::releaseAt(const QPointF &widgetPos, double pressure, double seconds)
{
    if (m_drawing == Drawing::Eraser) {
        eraseAround(toDocument(widgetPos));
        m_drawing = Drawing::None;
        if (m_erasedSomething)
            finishEdit();
        return;
    }
    if (m_drawing != Drawing::Pen)
        return;
    m_input.end({toDocument(widgetPos), pressure, seconds});
    m_input.takeNewPoints();
    std::shared_ptr<const Stroke> stroke = m_input.finish();
    m_drawing = Drawing::None;
    const QRectF tail = std::exchange(m_lastPrediction, QRectF()); // the tail goes with the pen: repaint where it was
    if (stroke && !stroke->points.empty()) {
        // Pen strokes are always Layer::User; Layer::Highlighter is only read from .rnote (Spec 2).
        // A highlighter tool does nothing over handwriting (spec section 2), so none exists here.
        m_document.addStroke(stroke, Layer::User);
        if (m_document.growToContain(stroke->bounds()))
            documentReplaced();
        finishEdit();
        update(widgetRect(stroke->bounds() | tail));
    } else {
        update();
    }
}

void InkCanvas::rasteriseNewSegments()
{
    const size_t fresh = m_input.takeNewPoints();
    const Stroke &stroke = m_input.stroke();
    if (fresh == 0 || stroke.points.empty())
        return;
    // The segments ending at the new points: from the last point of the
    // previous batch, so nothing is missed between batches.
    const size_t last = stroke.points.size() - 1;
    const size_t first = last + 1 >= fresh + 1 ? last - fresh : 0;
    const QPainterPath path = strokeOutline(stroke, first, last);
    QPainter painter(&m_live);
    painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    painter.scale(m_zoom, m_zoom);
    painter.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    painter.setBrush(stroke.style.color);
    painter.drawPath(path);
    painter.end();
    QRectF tail; // the predicted tail's box: repainted now, and again where it was when it moves
    for (const StrokePoint &p : m_input.prediction())
        tail |= QRectF(p.x - stroke.style.width, p.y - stroke.style.width, 2 * stroke.style.width, 2 * stroke.style.width);
    const QRectF dirty = path.boundingRect() | tail | m_lastPrediction; // a null rect adds nothing to a union
    m_lastPrediction = tail;
    update(widgetRect(dirty));
}

void InkCanvas::eraseAround(const QPointF &documentPos)
{
    const double r = m_eraserWidth * 0.5;
    const QRectF area(documentPos.x() - r, documentPos.y() - r, 2 * r, 2 * r);
    QPainterPath circle;
    circle.addEllipse(area);
    QRectF dirty;
    for (qint64 t : m_document.itemsIntersecting(area)) {
        const Item *item = m_document.item(t);
        if (!item)
            continue;
        const bool hit = item->type == Item::Type::Stroke ? strokeOutline(*item->stroke).intersects(circle)
                                                          : item->bounds().intersects(area);
        if (!hit)
            continue;
        dirty |= item->bounds();
        m_document.removeItem(t);
        m_erasedSomething = true;
    }
    if (dirty.isValid())
        update(widgetRect(dirty));
}

void InkCanvas::finishEdit()
{
    m_document.commit();
    emit documentEdited();
}

bool InkCanvas::undo()
{
    if (m_readOnly || !m_document.undo())
        return false;
    documentReplaced();
    emit documentEdited();
    return true;
}

bool InkCanvas::redo()
{
    if (m_readOnly || !m_document.redo())
        return false;
    documentReplaced();
    emit documentEdited();
    return true;
}

// --- painting ------------------------------------------------------------------

void InkCanvas::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event)
{
    QPainter painter(this);
    painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    painter.fillRect(event->rect(), m_document.settings().background.color);
    // Track S1 Task 10 paints the page pattern here; Task 11 replaces the
    // item loop with the tile cache. In A0 the exposed rect's items are
    // painted straight from the document.
    painter.save();
    painter.scale(m_zoom, m_zoom);
    const QRectF exposed(QPointF(event->rect().topLeft()) / m_zoom, QSizeF(event->rect().size()) / m_zoom);
    for (const Item *item : m_document.paintOrder(exposed.adjusted(-4, -4, 4, 4))) {
        if (item->type == Item::Type::Stroke)
            paintStroke(painter, *item->stroke);
        else if (item->type == Item::Type::Shape || item->type == Item::Type::TextBox || item->type == Item::Type::Image)
            painter.fillRect(item->bounds(), QColor(0, 0, 0, 12)); // placeholder until Tasks 14-15 draw them
    }
    if (m_drawing == Drawing::Pen) {
        const auto &prediction = m_input.prediction();
        if (!prediction.empty()) {
            Stroke tail;
            tail.style = m_input.stroke().style;
            tail.points.push_back(m_input.stroke().points.back());
            tail.points.insert(tail.points.end(), prediction.begin(), prediction.end());
            painter.setOpacity(0.6);
            paintStroke(painter, tail);
            painter.setOpacity(1.0);
        }
    }
    painter.restore();
    if (m_drawing == Drawing::Pen && !m_live.isNull())
        painter.drawImage(event->rect(), m_live, QRectF(QPointF(event->rect().topLeft()) * devicePixelRatio(),
                                                        QSizeF(event->rect().size()) * devicePixelRatio()));
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

```cpp
TEST_F(WindowTest, AnInkFileOpensInTheInkViewAndSavesThroughTheWindow)
{
    write(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"),
          "{\"format\":\"notolith-ink\",\"version\":1,\"document\":{},\"items\":[\n"
          "{\"type\":\"stroke\",\"t\":1,\"layer\":\"user\",\"style\":{\"width\":2,\"color\":\"#1e1e1e\",\"pressureCurve\":\"linear\"},"
          "\"points\":[10,10,0.5,50,50,0.5]}\n]}\n");
    MainWindow window;
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.show();
    ASSERT_TRUE(QTest::qWaitForWindowExposed(&window));
    window.scanModulesNow();
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.modules()) << "the module registry exists after the scan";
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.modules()->moduleForSuffix(path(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")))) << "notolith-ink was found in CMAKE_BINARY_DIR/modules";
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.openNote(path(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"))));
    QWidget *view = nullptr;
    WAIT_TRUE((view = window.findChild<QWidget *>(QStringLiteral("inkView"))) != nullptr, 5000);
    auto *moduleView = qobject_cast<noteAppC::ModuleView *>(view);
    ASSERT_NE(moduleView, nullptr);
    EXPECT_TRUE(window.modules()->isLoaded(QStringLiteral("ink")));
    auto *tabs = window.findChild<QTabBar *>(QStringLiteral("noteTabs"));
    ASSERT_NE(tabs, nullptr);
    EXPECT_EQ(tabs->tabText(tabs->currentIndex()), QStringLiteral("Sketch"));
    auto *canvas = view->findChild<QWidget *>(QStringLiteral("inkCanvas"));
    ASSERT_TRUE(canvas);
    EXPECT_GT(canvas->height(), 1000) << "an A4 page at zoom 1";
    // Draw through the module view's canvas with a synthetic pen, then save with the window's command.
    using inktest::tablet;
    tablet(canvas, QEvent::TabletPress, QPointF(100, 100), 0.5, 1000);
    tablet(canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(150, 120), 0.5, 1005);
    tablet(canvas, QEvent::TabletMove, QPointF(200, 140), 0.5, 1010);
    tablet(canvas, QEvent::TabletRelease, QPointF(200, 140), 0.5, 1015, Qt::NoButton);
    WAIT_TRUE(moduleView->isModified(), 5000);
    EXPECT_TRUE(tabs->tabText(tabs->currentIndex()).contains(QStringLiteral("Sketch"))) << "the modified marker keeps the name";
    command(window, "editor:save-file")->trigger();
    WAIT_TRUE(!moduleView->isModified(), 5000);
    QFile saved(path(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")));
    ASSERT_TRUE(saved.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly));
    const QByteArray bytes = saved.readAll();
    EXPECT_EQ(bytes.count("\"type\":\"stroke\""), 2) << "the old stroke and the new one";
    EXPECT_TRUE(bytes.contains("\"format\":\"notolith-ink\"")) << "compact JSON, keys sorted (R4): the format key is not first";
}

TEST_F(WindowTest, WithInkOffAnInkFileIsNotAModuleFile)
{
    write(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"), "{\"format\":\"notolith-ink\",\"version\":1,\"document\":{},\"items\":[]}\n");
    MainWindow window;
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.show();
    ASSERT_TRUE(QTest::qWaitForWindowExposed(&window));
    window.scanModulesNow();
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.modules()->moduleForSuffix(path(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"))));

    noteAppC::ToggleSwitch *toggle = nullptr;
    ASSERT_TRUE(QTest::qWaitFor([&] {
        toggle = settingsWidget<noteAppC::ToggleSwitch>(window, QStringLiteral("settingsToggle_plugins.ink"));
        return toggle != nullptr;
    }, 3000)) << "the module's feature row is in Settings > Plugins";
    toggle->setChecked(false);
    WAIT_TRUE(!window.settings().boolean(Settings::featureKey(QStringLiteral("ink"))), 5000);
    EXPECT_FALSE(window.modules()->moduleForSuffix(path(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"))));
    command(window, "workspace:new-tab")->trigger();
    EXPECT_FALSE(window.openNote(path(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")))) << "with the feature off a .ink is not a view file";
    EXPECT_FALSE(window.modules()->isLoaded(QStringLiteral("ink"))) << "never loaded: nothing in this test used it";
}

TEST_F(WindowTest, NewHandwrittenNoteIsCreatedBesideTheActiveNoteElseInTheVaultRoot)
{
    ASSERT_TRUE(QDir(dir.path()).mkpath(QStringLiteral("Sub")));

    WAIT_TRUE(QFileInfo::exists(beside), 5000);
    EXPECT_FALSE(QFileInfo::exists(path(QStringLiteral("Handwritten note.ink")))) << "not the vault root while a note in Sub is active";
    QFile created(beside);

    command(window, "workspace:new-tab")->trigger(); // an empty tab: no active note
    command(window, "ink:new")->trigger();
    WAIT_TRUE(QFileInfo::exists(path(QStringLiteral("Handwritten note.ink"))), 5000);
}
```

`libs/ext/ink/InkView.h`:
```cpp
#pragma once

#include <QScrollArea>

#include "ModuleHost.h"
#include "core/InkCanvas.h"

class QToolBar;

namespace noteAppC::ink {

/// The handwritten-note tab: a scroll area over the canvas and a small
/// toolbar (pen, eraser, undo, redo; Track S1 Task 16 grows it to every tool:
/// R14, PADD's pen has no eraser end and no working buttons). Load and
/// save go through InkFormat; a file whose version is above 1 opens
/// read-only and says so in the toolbar.
class InkView : public ModuleView {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit InkView(const noteAppC::Settings *settings, QWidget *parent = nullptr);

    bool load(const QString &path, QString *error) override;
    bool save(QString *error) override;
    bool isModified() const override { return m_canvas->document().isModified(); }
    void setTheme(const Theme &theme) override;
    void applySettings(const noteAppC::Settings &settings) override;

    InkCanvas *canvas() const { return m_canvas; }
    QString path() const { return m_path; }
    bool isReadOnly() const { return m_readOnly; }

private:
    void onEdited();

    const noteAppC::Settings *m_settings;
    QScrollArea *m_area;
    InkCanvas *m_canvas;
    QToolBar *m_toolBar;
    QAction *m_penAction;
    QAction *m_eraserAction;
    QAction *m_undoAction;
    QAction *m_redoAction;
    QString m_path;
    bool m_readOnly = false;
    bool m_lastModified = false;
};

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```

`libs/ext/ink/InkView.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "InkView.h"

#include <QAction>
#include <QActionGroup>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QScrollBar>
#include <QToolBar>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

#include "Settings.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Vault.h"
#include "core/InkFormat.h"

namespace noteAppC::ink {

InkView::InkView(const Settings *settings, QWidget *parent)
    : ModuleView(parent), m_settings(settings), m_area(new QScrollArea(this)), m_canvas(new InkCanvas),
      m_toolBar(new QToolBar(this))
{
    setObjectName(QStringLiteral("inkView"));
    m_canvas->setObjectName(QStringLiteral("inkCanvas"));
    m_area->setWidget(m_canvas);
    m_area->setAlignment(Qt::AlignHCenter | Qt::AlignTop);
    m_area->setWidgetResizable(false);

    auto *tools = new QActionGroup(this);
    m_penAction = tools->addAction(tr("Pen"));
    m_eraserAction = tools->addAction(tr("Eraser"));
    for (QAction *a : {m_penAction, m_eraserAction})
        a->setCheckable(true);
    m_penAction->setChecked(true);
    connect(m_penAction, &QAction::triggered, this, [this] { m_canvas->setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Pen); });
    connect(m_eraserAction, &QAction::triggered, this, [this] { m_canvas->setTool(InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser); });
    m_undoAction = new QAction(tr("Undo"), this);
    m_redoAction = new QAction(tr("Redo"), this);
    connect(m_undoAction, &QAction::triggered, m_canvas, &InkCanvas::undo);
    connect(m_redoAction, &QAction::triggered, m_canvas, &InkCanvas::redo);
    m_toolBar->addActions({m_penAction, m_eraserAction});
    m_toolBar->addSeparator();
    m_toolBar->addActions({m_undoAction, m_redoAction});

    auto *layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    layout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    layout->setSpacing(0);
    layout->addWidget(m_toolBar);
    layout->addWidget(m_area, 1);

    connect(m_canvas, &InkCanvas::documentEdited, this, &InkView::onEdited);
    connect(m_canvas, &InkCanvas::toolChanged, this, [this](InkCanvas::Tool tool) { // the keys P and E (R14) move the toolbar too
        m_penAction->setChecked(tool != InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser);
        m_eraserAction->setChecked(tool == InkCanvas::Tool::Eraser);
    });
    connect(m_canvas, &InkCanvas::panRequested, this, [this](QPoint delta) {
        m_area->horizontalScrollBar()->setValue(m_area->horizontalScrollBar()->value() - delta.x());
        m_area->verticalScrollBar()->setValue(m_area->verticalScrollBar()->value() - delta.y());
    });
    if (m_settings)
        applySettings(*m_settings); // the window calls applySettings() again after every change
    onEdited();
}

void InkView::applySettings(const Settings &settings)
{
    m_canvas->setDrawWithMouse(settings.value(QStringLiteral("ink.drawWithMouse")).toBool());
    m_canvas->setRawInput(settings.value(QStringLiteral("ink.rawInput")).toBool());
}

void InkView::onEdited()
{
    m_undoAction->setEnabled(m_canvas->document().canUndo() && !m_readOnly);
    m_redoAction->setEnabled(m_canvas->document().canRedo() && !m_readOnly);
    const bool modified = isModified();
    if (modified != m_lastModified) {
        m_lastModified = modified;
        emit modificationChanged(modified);
    }
}

bool InkView::load(const QString &path, QString *error)
{
    InkReadResult result;
    if (!loadInkFile(path, m_canvas->document(), &result)) {
        if (error) *error = result.error;
        return false;
    }
    m_path = path;
    m_readOnly = result.readOnly;
    m_canvas->setReadOnly(m_readOnly); // not setEnabled(false): a disabled widget would swallow wheel and touch pan too
    if (m_readOnly)
        emit statusMessage(tr("%1 was written by a newer Notolith; opened read-only").arg(QFileInfo(path).fileName()), 6000);
    m_canvas->documentReplaced();
    m_lastModified = false;
    onEdited();
    return true;
}

bool InkView::save(QString *error)
{
    if (m_readOnly) {
        if (error) *error = tr("read-only: written by a newer Notolith");
        return false;
    }
    // The vault's atomic replace (spec section 5): writeInk() builds the bytes, the host writes them.
    if (!Vault::writeBytes(m_path, writeInk(m_canvas->document()), error))
        return false;
    m_canvas->document().markSaved();
    onEdited();
    return true;
}

void InkView::setTheme(const Theme &theme)
{
    QPalette palette = m_area->viewport()->palette();
    palette.setColor(QPalette::Window, theme.color(QStringLiteral("--background-secondary")));
    m_area->viewport()->setPalette(palette);
    m_area->viewport()->setAutoFillBackground(true);
}

} // namespace noteAppC::ink
```
`Settings` is a plain class (`Settings.h:44`, no QObject, no signal); live changes reach the view through `ModuleView::applySettings`, which the window's settings pass calls (Task 2). `Settings::value(const QString &) const` is at `Settings.h:68`.

`libs/ext/ink/InkModule.cpp`:
```cpp
// The handwritten-note module (spec section 4): what Notolith dlopens when a
// .ink file is opened or the "New handwritten note" command runs. Only this
// file knows it is a plugin; InkView and the core are plain Qt.
#include <QDir>
#include <QObject>
#include <QtPlugin>

#include "InkView.h"
#include "ModuleHost.h"
#include "NotolithModule.h"
#include "Vault.h"
#include "core/InkFormat.h"

// Global namespace on purpose: Q_PLUGIN_METADATA and moc want the plugin class
// plain, so every host type is spelled out.
class InkModule : public QObject, public noteAppC::NotolithModule {
    Q_OBJECT
    Q_PLUGIN_METADATA(IID NotolithModule_iid FILE "manifest.json")
    Q_INTERFACES(noteAppC::NotolithModule)
public:
    void attach(noteAppC::ModuleHost &host) override
    {
        host.registerView({QStringLiteral("ink")}, [&host](const QString &, QWidget *parent) -> noteAppC::ModuleView * {
            return new noteAppC::ink::InkView(host.settings, parent);
        });
        host.registerSetting({QStringLiteral("ink.drawWithMouse"), false, QObject::tr("Draw with the mouse"),
                              QObject::tr("Off, the mouse only scrolls and selects; a pen always draws."), {}, 0, 0});
        host.registerSetting({QStringLiteral("ink.rawInput"), false, QObject::tr("Raw pen input"),
                              QObject::tr("Skip the stroke modeler: every tablet sample becomes a point. For comparing."), {}, 0, 0});
        host.registerCommand(QStringLiteral("ink:new"), QObject::tr("New handwritten note"), [&host] {
            const QString folder = host.currentFolder ? host.currentFolder() : QString(); // beside the active note, else the vault root
            if (folder.isEmpty())
                return;
            const QDir root(folder);
            QString name = QStringLiteral("Handwritten note.ink");
            for (int n = 2; root.exists(name); ++n)
                name = QStringLiteral("Handwritten note %1.ink").arg(n);
            noteAppC::ink::InkDocument empty;
            QString error;
            if (!noteAppC::Vault::writeBytes(root.filePath(name), noteAppC::ink::writeInk(empty), &error)) {
                host.showStatus(QObject::tr("Could not create %1: %2").arg(name, error), 6000);
                return;
            }
            host.openFile(root.filePath(name));
        });
    }
};

#include "InkModule.moc"
```
`ModuleHost::currentFolder` (Task 1, wired in Task 2) is the active note's folder, else the vault root. `Vault::writeBytes` is static (Vault.h:84); `host.vault` is no longer needed here.

Append to `libs/ext/ink/CMakeLists.txt`:
```cmake
# The tab (InkView) as a static library the module links; Track S1's bench and
# index tests link it too, without dlopen.
# Headers only from the host's static libraries: a second copy of Settings
# inside the .so would consult its own defaults() map, not the one the host
# filled with registerDefaults. The symbols resolve from the executable.
add_library(noteAppC_ink STATIC InkView.cpp InkView.h)
set_target_properties(noteAppC_ink PROPERTIES AUTOMOC ON POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)
target_include_directories(noteAppC_ink PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}
    $<TARGET_PROPERTY:noteAppC_settings,INTERFACE_INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES>
    $<TARGET_PROPERTY:noteAppC_theme,INTERFACE_INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES>
    $<TARGET_PROPERTY:noteAppC_vault,INTERFACE_INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES>)
target_link_libraries(noteAppC_ink PUBLIC notolith-inkcore noteAppC_modules_headers Qt6::Widgets)

# The module itself: only this target is a Qt plugin. Host symbols (Settings,
# Theme, Vault, ModuleView's vtable) resolve from the executable at load time
# (ENABLE_EXPORTS on every host binary, ResolveAllSymbolsHint in the registry).
configure_file(manifest.json.in ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/manifest.json @ONLY)
add_library(notolith-ink MODULE InkModule.cpp)
set_target_properties(notolith-ink PROPERTIES
    AUTOMOC ON
    PREFIX ""
    LIBRARY_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules
    INSTALL_RPATH "$ORIGIN"
    BUILD_RPATH_USE_ORIGIN ON)
target_include_directories(notolith-ink PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}) # manifest.json for Q_PLUGIN_METADATA
target_link_libraries(notolith-ink PRIVATE noteAppC_ink)
install(TARGETS notolith-ink LIBRARY DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/noteAppC/modules)
```
`noteAppC_settings`, `noteAppC_theme`, `noteAppC_vault` are the host's static library targets (`libs/*/CMakeLists.txt` line 1 each). A MODULE library on Linux may leave symbols undefined at link time; an undefined reference that survives to `dlopen` (the registry's `ResolveAllSymbolsHint` surfaces it as the load error) means a header the module uses is not compiled into the host, not a missing library.

`tests/CMakeLists.txt`: in the `window` test's environment (Task 2 made it `set_property(TEST window APPEND PROPERTY ENVIRONMENT ...)`), change the module dirs to `NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/fakemodules:${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules`, and add:
```cmake

endif()
# Track S1b's glyph studio (libs/ext/hwfont): S1b creates the directory and
# tests/test_hwfont.cpp; until noteAppC_hwfont exists this block does nothing.
# test_hwfont drives the studio view and the module through the registry, so it
# links the host (noteAppC_ui) and the static hwfont library only: the module
# is loaded from ${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules, never linked.
if(TARGET noteAppC_hwfont)
    add_executable(test_hwfont test_hwfont.cpp IsolatedHome.cpp)
    target_link_libraries(test_hwfont PRIVATE noteAppC_hwfont noteAppC_ui Qt6::Test GTest::gtest)
    set_target_properties(test_hwfont PROPERTIES ENABLE_EXPORTS ON BUILD_RPATH "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules")
    add_test(NAME hwfont COMMAND test_hwfont)
    set_tests_properties(hwfont PROPERTIES ENVIRONMENT "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen;NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules")
    add_dependencies(test_hwfont notolith-hwfont)
endif()
```
Register `hwfont` above the isolated-home `foreach` so it gets its XDG home like the others (the registration sits behind `if(TARGET noteAppC_hwfont)`, so a missing S1b changes nothing).

`ModuleHost::showTab` shows a view with no file (the glyph studio, Task 21). Today the tab never shows the modified mark, `editor:save-file` does nothing for it (`saveLeaf` returns at the empty path before the `ModuleView` branch) and closing or quitting never asks. The host fixes that here, in A0, because S1b may not edit `MainWindow.cpp`. The fake module gets a file-less view to test it with.

`tests/fakemodule/manifest.json.in`: add a third command to `commands`: `{"id": "@FAKE_ID@:tab", "title": "Fake: open a file-less tab"}`. `tests/fakemodule/FakeModule.cpp`: add above `FakeModule` (inside the anonymous namespace):

```cpp
/// A view with no file, shown with host.showTab (what the glyph studio is). The dynamic property
/// "modified" is its state, so a window test drives it without a header from the module; every
/// save() appends a line to the file named by NOTEAPPC_FAKE_TAB_SAVES, which survives the view.
class FakeTab : public noteAppC::ModuleView {
public:
    explicit FakeTab(QWidget *parent) : ModuleView(parent)
    {
        setObjectName(QStringLiteral("fakeTab"));
        setProperty("modified", false);
    }
    bool load(const QString &, QString *) override { return true; }
    bool save(QString *) override
    {
        const QByteArray saves = qgetenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_TAB_SAVES");
        if (!saves.isEmpty()) {
            QFile file(QString::fromLocal8Bit(saves));
            if (file.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Append))
                file.write("saved\n");
        }
        setProperty("modified", false);
        emit modificationChanged(false);
        return true;
    }
    bool isModified() const override { return property("modified").toBool(); }
    void setTheme(const noteAppC::Theme &) override {}
};
```
and in `attach`, after the `fake:where` command:

```cpp

        host.registerCommand(QStringLiteral(FAKE_ID ":tab"), QStringLiteral("Fake: open a file-less tab"),
                             [showTab] { if (showTab) showTab(new FakeTab(nullptr), QStringLiteral("Fake tab")); });
```

```cpp
// R6: a file-less module tab shows the modified mark, the save command calls its save(), and
// closing it saves it like a note (autosave is on by default) without asking.
TEST_F(WindowTest, AFileLessModuleTabShowsTheModifiedMarkAndIsSavedByTheSaveCommandAndOnClose)
{
    QTemporaryDir marks;
    const QString saves = marks.filePath(QStringLiteral("saves"));
    qputenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_TAB_SAVES", saves.toLocal8Bit());
    auto unset = qScopeGuard([] { qunsetenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_TAB_SAVES"); });
    const auto savedCount = [&saves] {
        QFile file(saves);
        return file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly) ? int(file.readAll().count("saved")) : 0;
    };
    MainWindow window;
    window.show();
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.scanModulesNow();
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.settings().boolean(QStringLiteral("files.autosave"))) << "the default: closing saves instead of asking";
    command(window, "fake:tab")->trigger();
    auto *view = window.findChild<noteAppC::ModuleView *>(QStringLiteral("fakeTab"));
    ASSERT_NE(view, nullptr);
    ASSERT_FALSE(mainBars(window).isEmpty());
    QTabBar *tabs = mainBars(window).first();
    EXPECT_EQ(tabs->tabText(tabs->currentIndex()), QStringLiteral("Fake tab"));

    view->setProperty("modified", true);
    emit view->modificationChanged(true);
    EXPECT_EQ(tabs->tabText(tabs->currentIndex()), QStringLiteral("Fake tab *")) << "the asterisk";
    command(window, "editor:save-file")->trigger();
    EXPECT_EQ(savedCount(), 1) << "the save command reaches the view's save()";
    EXPECT_EQ(tabs->tabText(tabs->currentIndex()), QStringLiteral("Fake tab"));

    view->setProperty("modified", true);
    emit view->modificationChanged(true);
    bool asked = false;
    QTimer watch;
    QObject::connect(&watch, &QTimer::timeout, [&asked] {
        if (auto *box = qobject_cast<QMessageBox *>(QApplication::activeModalWidget())) {
            asked = true;
            box->button(QMessageBox::Cancel)->click();
        }
    });
    watch.start(20);
    command(window, "workspace:close")->trigger();
    EXPECT_FALSE(asked) << "autosave on: leaving saves, nothing asks";
    EXPECT_EQ(savedCount(), 2) << "closing the modified tab saved it";
}

TEST_F(WindowTest, AFileLessModuleTabAsksBeforeClosingWhenAutosaveIsOffAndSavesOnSave)
{
    QTemporaryDir config;
    const QByteArray previous = qgetenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME");
    qputenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", config.path().toUtf8());
    auto restore = qScopeGuard([&previous] { qputenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", previous); });
    Settings off = Settings::load();
    off.setValue(QStringLiteral("files.autosave"), false);
    ASSERT_TRUE(off.save());
    QTemporaryDir marks;
    const QString saves = marks.filePath(QStringLiteral("saves"));
    qputenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_TAB_SAVES", saves.toLocal8Bit());
    auto unset = qScopeGuard([] { qunsetenv("NOTEAPPC_FAKE_TAB_SAVES"); });
    MainWindow window;
    window.show();
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.scanModulesNow();
    command(window, "fake:tab")->trigger();
    auto *view = window.findChild<noteAppC::ModuleView *>(QStringLiteral("fakeTab"));
    ASSERT_NE(view, nullptr);
    view->setProperty("modified", true);
    emit view->modificationChanged(true);
    QString question;
    QTimer watch;
    QObject::connect(&watch, &QTimer::timeout, [&question] {
        if (auto *box = qobject_cast<QMessageBox *>(QApplication::activeModalWidget())) {
            question = box->text();
            box->button(QMessageBox::Save)->click();
        }
    });
    watch.start(20);
    command(window, "workspace:close")->trigger();
    EXPECT_TRUE(question.contains(QStringLiteral("Fake tab"))) << "asked, naming the tab: " << question.toStdString();
    QFile file(saves);
    ASSERT_TRUE(file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly));
    EXPECT_EQ(file.readAll().count("saved"), 1) << "Save called the view's save()";
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: PLUGINS.md paragraph**

After the "Tier 1" section in `docs/PLUGINS.md`, add:
```markdown
### Tier 1b: loadable modules - compiled against the host, dlopen'd on first use

A module is a Qt plugin (`QPluginLoader`) found in `<libdir>/noteAppC/modules`
(`NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS` replaces it). Its manifest is read without loading it; the library
loads the first time one of its suffixes is opened or one of its commands runs. Nothing else
loads a module: indexing never does. Two packages ship: `notolith-ink` (the ink core library
and the ink module) and `notolith-hwfont` (depends on `notolith-ink`); the manifest has no
dependency list, so a module whose core library is missing fails to load with the loader's
message, shown in Settings. A module must match the host's ABI tag
(`version-token-qtversion`, the token random per build directory); a second module claiming
a suffix an earlier one claims is refused. The mechanism is in `libs/modules/`; the first two
modules are documented in [INK.md](INK.md). Spec:
`superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-ink-core-design.md` section 4.
```

### Task 9: Page layouts

**Files:**
- Modify: `libs/ext/ink/core/InkDocument.cpp` (`growToContain`, `pageCount`, `pageRect`), `InkDocument.h` (one new method)
- Modify: `libs/ext/ink/core/InkCanvas.cpp` (`paintEvent` page gaps, `sizeHint` for infinite layouts), `InkCanvas.h`
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: the constant `InkDocument::kPageGap = 16.0` (document px between pages in ContinuousVertical; no `pageGap()` accessor: the canvas and the tests read the constant); the gap is painted in the window colour with a 1 px border and a 4 px shadow under each page; `growToContain` for `SemiInfinite` (grows right and down to the bounds plus one page width/height margin; never left or up), `Infinite` (grows in all four directions; `extent` may have negative x/y); `pageCount()` is 1 for SemiInfinite/Infinite (one sheet).
- `InkCanvas::sizeHint()` includes the gaps: `extent.height * zoom + (pageCount-1) * kPageGap * zoom` for ContinuousVertical; `toDocument/toWidget` account for the gap of the page under the point (a point in a gap maps to the page above's bottom edge).

- [ ] **Step 1: Tests**
```cpp
TEST(InkLayoutTest, SemiInfiniteGrowsRightAndDownOnly)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    DocumentSettings s;
    s.layout = LayoutKind::SemiInfinite;
    s.format = {100, 100, 96};
    s.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 100, 100);
    doc.setSettings(s);
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.growToContain(QRectF(150, 20, 10, 10)));
    EXPECT_GE(doc.settings().extent.right(), 160 + 100) << "a page width of margin past the ink";
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.settings().extent.left(), 0);
    EXPECT_FALSE(doc.growToContain(QRectF(-50, -50, 10, 10))) << "never left or up";
    EXPECT_EQ(doc.pageCount(), 1);
}

TEST(InkLayoutTest, InfiniteGrowsEveryWay)
{
    InkDocument doc;
    DocumentSettings s;
    s.layout = LayoutKind::Infinite;
    s.format = {100, 100, 96};
    s.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 100, 100);
    doc.setSettings(s);
    EXPECT_TRUE(doc.growToContain(QRectF(-50, -50, 10, 10)));
    EXPECT_LE(doc.settings().extent.left(), -150);
    EXPECT_LE(doc.settings().extent.top(), -150);
}

TEST(InkLayoutTest, CanvasAccountsForPageGapsInContinuousVertical)
{
    InkCanvas canvas;
    DocumentSettings s;
    s.format = {100, 200, 96};
    s.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 100, 400); // two pages
    canvas.document().setSettings(s);
    canvas.documentReplaced();
    EXPECT_EQ(canvas.sizeHint(), QSize(100, 400 + int(InkDocument::kPageGap)));
    EXPECT_EQ(canvas.toWidget(QPointF(10, 210)), QPointF(10, 210 + InkDocument::kPageGap));
    EXPECT_EQ(canvas.toDocument(QPointF(10, 210 + InkDocument::kPageGap)), QPointF(10, 210));
    EXPECT_EQ(canvas.toDocument(QPointF(10, 205)), QPointF(10, 200)) << "in the gap: the page above's bottom edge";
}

TEST(InkLayoutTest, GapPixelsBelowAPageCarryTheShadow)
{
    InkCanvas canvas;
    DocumentSettings s;
    s.format = {100, 200, 96};
    s.extent = QRectF(0, 0, 100, 400);
    canvas.document().setSettings(s);
    canvas.documentReplaced();
    canvas.resize(canvas.sizeHint());
    const QImage shot = canvas.grab().toImage();
    const QColor window = canvas.palette().color(QPalette::Window);
    const int gapTop = 200;                              // the first page's bottom edge, widget px at zoom 1
    const int gapBottom = 200 + int(InkDocument::kPageGap) - 1;
    EXPECT_EQ(shot.pixelColor(50, gapBottom), window) << "the far side of the gap is the plain window colour";
    EXPECT_LT(shot.pixelColor(50, gapTop + 1).lightness(), window.lightness()) << "the shadow darkens the gap just under the page";
    EXPECT_LT(shot.pixelColor(50, gapTop + 1).lightness(), shot.pixelColor(50, gapTop + 3).lightness() + 1)
        << "and fades with distance";
}
```

Infinite extents with negative origin: `InkCanvas` keeps an `m_origin = -extent.topLeft()` offset applied in `toDocument/toWidget`; ContinuousVertical `toWidget(p)` adds `floor(p.y/format.height) * kPageGap` to y, `toDocument` subtracts page gaps by walking pages (`pageIndex = floor(y / (height + gap))`, `inPage = y - pageIndex*(height+gap)`, clamp `inPage` to `[0, height]`). `paintEvent` fills the gaps with `palette().color(QPalette::Window)` and the pages with the background colour; each page gets a 1 px border (`palette().color(QPalette::Mid)`) and, inside the gap below it, a 4 px shadow (`QLinearGradient` from `QColor(0, 0, 0, 40)` to transparent, clipped to the gap rect); the item loop translates per page: paint once with `painter.translate(0, pageIndex*gap)` per visible page, clipping to that page's rect.

### Task 10: Page backgrounds (patterns)

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/ext/ink/core/PagePatterns.h`, `PagePatterns.cpp`; goldens `fixtures/ink/golden/pattern-<name>.png` (11: none, lines, grid, dots, linesMargin, gridMajor, musicStaff, calligraphy, seyes, cornell, hexGrid; at zoom 1 dpr 1, 240x160 of an A4 top-left corner)
- Modify: `core/CMakeLists.txt`, `InkCanvas.cpp` (call `paintPattern` after the fill, before items)
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
/// Paints the pattern of `background` over `pageRect` (document px) into a painter already scaled to the zoom, clipped to `exposed`.
void paintPattern(QPainter &painter, const Background &background, const QRectF &pageRect, const QRectF &exposed, double zoom);

/// The indices of the lines a pattern must draw: lines sit at pageRect.top() + k * period, k >= 0, inside the page,
/// and a line is wanted when it, padded by a line's half width (kLinePad = 1.0), meets the exposed rect. Empty
/// (last < first) when none is. Pure: this is what keeps a 10 000 px page from looping over its whole height.
struct LineRange { int first = 0; int last = -1; bool isEmpty() const { return last < first; } bool operator==(const LineRange &) const = default; };
LineRange visibleLineRange(const QRectF &exposed, const QRectF &pageRect, double period);
}
```
Constants from Rnote (`rnote-engine/src/document/background.rs` and the fork's additions, GPL attribution in the file header): `kLineWidth = 0.5`, `kDotsWidth = 1.5`, `kOverlayLineWidth = 1.0`, `kMajorInterval = 5`, `kMinorOpacity = 0.5`, `kStaffPeriodFactor = 10`. Patterns: `Lines` (horizontal every `patternSize.height`), `Grid`, `Dots` (at intersections, `kDotsWidth` squares), `LinesMargin` (lines + vertical margin line at `x = patternSize.width` in `kOverlayLineWidth`), `GridMajor` (minor lines at `kMinorOpacity`, every 5th at full opacity and 2x width), `MusicStaff` (5 lines `patternSize.height` apart, staff period `10 * height`), `Calligraphy` (per period of `patternSize.height`: solid at 0, dashed `4 4` at 0.3, baseline 2x width at 0.6, dashed at 0.8), `Seyes` (`patternSize.width` squares in full colour, three lighter horizontal subdivisions per square at `kMinorOpacity`), `Cornell` (vertical margin at `patternSize.width`, horizontal summary line at `pageRect.bottom() - 0.2 * pageRect.height()`, both `kOverlayLineWidth`, plus lines), `HexGrid` (pointy-top hexagons of radius `patternSize.height / 2`: cell width `sqrt(3)*r`, row period `1.5*r`, odd rows offset half a width). Line widths are document px, as in Rnote: zoom scales them. Everything is drawn with `painter.setPen(QPen(color, width))` and `drawLine`/`drawRect`/`drawPolygon` on the clipped exposed range only: every pattern takes its first and last line index from `visibleLineRange` (once per axis; `HexGrid` uses it for rows at period `1.5 * r` and widens the column range by one cell), never loops over the whole page. `visibleLineRange`: `first = max(0, ceil((exposed.top() - pageRect.top() - kLinePad) / period))`, `last = min(floor((pageRect.height())/period), floor((exposed.bottom() - pageRect.top() + kLinePad) / period))`, empty when the exposed rect lies above or below the page.

### Task 11: Tile cache (LRU byte budget), zoom settle, off-thread parse

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/ext/ink/core/TileCache.h`, `TileCache.cpp`
- Modify: `InkCanvas.{h,cpp}` (paint through the cache; zoom settle timer; `invalidate(QRectF)` on edits; `setTileCacheMB(int)` forwards to `TileCache::setMaxBytes`), `InkDocument.{h,cpp}` (`dirtySince(revision)` is not needed: the canvas knows every edit it makes; undo/redo invalidate everything), `InkView.{h,cpp}` (load off-thread above `kOffThreadParseBytes`; reads the setting `ink.tileCacheMB` and calls `canvas->setTileCacheMB`, R5), `core/CMakeLists.txt` (links `Qt6::Concurrent`)
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp`, `tests/test_window.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
class TileCache : public QObject {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    static constexpr int kTileSize = 512; // device px
    static constexpr qsizetype kDefaultMaxBytes = 64 * 1024 * 1024; // R5: the setting `ink.tileCacheMB`, default 64
    explicit TileCache(qsizetype maxBytes = kDefaultMaxBytes, QObject *parent = nullptr);
    void setMaxBytes(qsizetype maxBytes);                         // lowering it evicts least-recently-painted tiles at once
    void setScale(double zoom, double devicePixelRatio);          // a change makes every tile stale; images stay until replaced
    void invalidate(const QRectF &documentRect);                  // tiles touching it become stale (a render in flight for one is discarded)
    void invalidateAll();

    struct Lookup { const QImage *image = nullptr; bool fresh = false; double scale = 1.0; }; // scale: the zoom the image was rendered at
    /// UI thread. Never renders. `image` is null when the tile was never rendered; `fresh` is false when it is stale:
    /// the canvas then paints it scaled (blurry, instant) or the page background, and calls request().
    /// Non-const on purpose (R5): a hit moves the tile to the most-recently-painted end of the LRU list, so
    /// `find` is called exactly once per tile per paint, by the code that paints it.
    Lookup find(int tx, int ty);
    /// UI thread. Starts `job` on the thread pool unless this tile already has a render in flight; the result is
    /// stored when it finishes (only if the tile was not invalidated and the scale not changed meanwhile - a
    /// per-tile serial and a global generation counter, both read when the job starts) and tileReady is emitted.
    /// `job` must be pure data: it paints strokes and shapes from an immutable snapshot of the items (their payloads
    /// are shared_ptr<const>), never touches a widget, a QPixmap or the document.
    bool request(int tx, int ty, std::function<QImage()> job);
    bool isPending(int tx, int ty) const;
    int pendingCount() const;
    qsizetype bytes() const; int count() const;
signals:
    void tileReady(int tx, int ty);                               // UI thread: the canvas does update(tileRect)
};
}
```
LRU (R5) by `std::list` + `QHash<QPair<int,int>, iterator>`: the front is the most recently *painted* tile (`find` splices the hit to the front; a finished render is inserted at the front), eviction walks from the back while `bytes() > maxBytes` and drops the least recently painted tile; a tile with a render in flight is never evicted. The budget is the setting `ink.tileCacheMB` (default 64, so `64 * 1024 * 1024` bytes; at 512 x 512 x 4 B = 1 MiB a tile that is 64 tiles, a full-HD screen at DPR 2 needs about 16): `InkView` reads it when it opens a file and on every `settingChanged`, and calls `canvas->setTileCacheMB(mb)`, which clamps to 8..1024 MB and calls `TileCache::setMaxBytes`. Rasterising a tile on the UI thread would stall the pen: a pan over 50 000 strokes needs several 512 px tiles at once. So the canvas never renders in `paintEvent`: it draws what `find()` has (the fresh tile; or the stale one scaled by `zoom / lookup.scale`, blurry but instant; or only the page background), calls `request()` for every tile that is not fresh and not pending, and repaints just that tile's rect on `tileReady`. Thread rule (R5): stroke and shape rasterisation runs off the UI thread because `QImage` + `QPainter` is thread-safe; text boxes and anything that uses `QWidget::grab` or a `QTextDocument` with widget fonts (images through `QPixmap` included) are rendered on the UI thread only, never in a tile job - the canvas paints them over the tiles from the per-(t, zoom, dpr) pixmap cache (Task 15). Zoom: `setZoom` during a pinch sets `m_pendingZoom` and repaints by scaling the *current* tiles (`painter.scale(pending/current)`) - blurry but instant; a `QTimer` single-shot 400 ms after the last zoom change calls `setScale` (every tile becomes stale) and the sharp tiles arrive from the pool one by one (`kZoomSettleMs = 400`). Edits: `releaseAt` calls `m_tiles.invalidate(stroke->bounds())`; `eraseAround` invalidates each removed item's bounds; undo/redo `invalidateAll()`. Off-thread parse in `InkView::load`: if `QFileInfo(path).size() > kOffThreadParseBytes`, read bytes on the UI thread (cheap), run `readInk` into a fresh `InkDocument` on `QtConcurrent::run`, swap it in via `QFutureWatcher::finished` (`m_canvas->document() = std::move(parsed)` - add `InkDocument(InkDocument&&)` default), show "Loading..." in the toolbar meanwhile; `save()` while loading returns false with "still loading". Since `InkDocument` holds `std::deque<Snapshot>` of shared pointers it is movable by default.

- [ ] **Step 1: Tests** (every wait is `WAIT_TRUE(expr, 5000)`; no wall-clock assertion anywhere, PERFORMANCE.md rule 3; gates for deterministic ordering are `QSemaphore`s inside the jobs):
  - `TileCacheTest.RendersOnceAndServesFromCache` (a job counter: `request` twice before the first finishes starts one job; after `WAIT_TRUE(find(0,0).fresh, 5000)` a third `request` is not needed and `find` returns the image without another job).
  - `TileCacheTest.TheJobRunsOffTheUIThread` (the job records `QThread::currentThread()`; it differs from `QThread::currentThread()` of the test, and `tileReady` arrives on the test thread).
  - `TileCacheTest.InvalidateMarksOnlyTouchedTilesStale` (4 tiles rendered; `invalidate` a rect inside tile (0,0): only that one is `!fresh`, its image is still returned for the blurry paint).
  - `TileCacheTest.AResultFinishedAfterAnInvalidateIsDiscarded` (the job blocks on a semaphore; `invalidate` the tile; release; the tile is not fresh and `isPending` is false, so the canvas requests it again with the new content; same for `setScale`).
  - `TileCacheTest.EvictsLeastRecentlyPaintedPastTheByteBudget` (R5; cap at 3 tiles' bytes, render tiles 1, 2, 3, `find(1)` (painted again), render 4: tile 2 is gone, tiles 1, 3, 4 stay and `bytes() <= cap`):
    ```cpp
    TEST(TileCacheTest, EvictsLeastRecentlyPaintedPastTheByteBudget)
    {
        const qsizetype tile = TileCache::kTileSize * TileCache::kTileSize * 4;
        TileCache cache(3 * tile);
        QSignalSpy ready(&cache, &TileCache::tileReady);
        const auto render = [&](int tx) {
            cache.request(tx, 0, [] { return QImage(TileCache::kTileSize, TileCache::kTileSize, QImage::Format_ARGB32_Premultiplied); });
            WAIT_TRUE(cache.find(tx, 0).fresh, 5000);
        };
        render(1); render(2); render(3);
        ASSERT_NE(cache.find(1, 0).image, nullptr); // 1 is now the most recently painted; 2 the least
        render(4);
        EXPECT_EQ(cache.find(2, 0).image, nullptr);
        EXPECT_NE(cache.find(1, 0).image, nullptr);
        EXPECT_NE(cache.find(3, 0).image, nullptr);
        EXPECT_NE(cache.find(4, 0).image, nullptr);
        EXPECT_LE(cache.bytes(), 3 * tile);
    }
    ```
  - `TileCacheTest.LoweringTheBudgetEvictsAtOnce` (R5; four tiles rendered under a 4-tile cap, `setMaxBytes(2 * tile)`: `count() == 2` and the two kept are the two most recently painted).
  - `InkCanvasTest.TheTileCacheFollowsTheSettingInMB` (R5; `canvas.setTileCacheMB(2)`: `canvas.tileCacheBytes() == 2 * 1024 * 1024`; `setTileCacheMB(0)` clamps to 8 MB; add `qsizetype tileCacheBytes() const` as a test hook returning `m_tiles.maxBytes()`; also add `qsizetype maxBytes() const` to `TileCache`).

  - `InkCanvasTest.EditsInvalidateOnlyTheirTiles` (a 2000x2000 doc at zoom 1 has 16 tiles; `grab()` until `pendingTiles() == 0`, draw one short stroke in the top-left tile, `staleTileCount()` is 1..4, not 16).
  - `InkViewTest.ABigFileParsesOffTheUIThreadAndSavesAfter` (write a 300 KB .ink with ~3 000 strokes; `load()` returns true at once with `isLoading()` true; `WAIT_TRUE(!view.isLoading(), 5000)`; items count matches; `save()` succeeds) - `test_ink` links only inkcore and InkView needs the host's Settings and Theme, so this one goes in `tests/test_window.cpp` (host linked, `NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS` set): open the big file through `window.openNote`, find `inkView`, `WAIT_TRUE` on the toolbar not showing "Loading...", then `command(window, "editor:save-file")`.

### Task 12: Eraser split mode

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/ext/ink/core/InkTools.h`, `InkTools.cpp` (pure functions over the model; Tasks 13-15 add to it)
- Modify: `InkCanvas.{h,cpp}` (`enum class EraserMode { Stroke, Split }`, `setEraserMode`, `eraseAround` dispatch), `InkView.cpp` (toolbar toggle "Erase whole strokes / Erase parts"), `core/CMakeLists.txt` (add `InkTools.cpp InkTools.h` to `notolith-inkcore`)
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
struct SplitResult { bool hit = false; std::vector<std::shared_ptr<const Stroke>> pieces; }; // pieces keep the style; empty pieces -> the stroke is gone
/// Rnote's split_colliding_strokes: segments whose outline meets `eraser` are cut out; runs of >= 2 points survive as new strokes.
SplitResult splitStroke(const Stroke &stroke, const QPainterPath &eraser);
}
```
Ported from `rnote-engine/src/store/trash_comp.rs` (GPL attribution). Hit test per segment: `strokeOutline(stroke, i, i+1).intersects(eraser)`. Surviving runs: maximal index ranges with no hit segment and at least 2 points. In `InkCanvas::eraseAround` for Split: for each hit item, `replaceItem(t, firstPiece)` and `addItem` for the rest (same layer), or `removeItem` when no pieces. Each piece is a new item with a new `t` except the first, which keeps `t` so the undo snapshot diff is small.

### Task 13: Lasso and rectangle selection: move, resize, delete, copy, paste

**Files:**
- Modify: `InkTools.{h,cpp}` (`selectItems`, `transformItem`), `InkCanvas.{h,cpp}` (Tool::Lasso with `enum class SelectShape { Lasso, Rect }`, selection state, handles, Delete key, Ctrl+C/Ctrl+V, `kHeldLassoButton` → temporary lasso), `InkView.cpp` (toolbar: Select, lasso/rect toggle)
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::ink {
// InkCanvas (public): void setHeldLassoEnabled(bool on); bool isHeldLassoEnabled() const;
std::vector<qint64> selectItems(const InkDocument &doc, const QPainterPath &region);        // items whose bounds centre is inside, or whose outline intersects for strokes (Rnote: any point inside)
Item transformItem(const Item &item, const QTransform &transform);                           // new shared payloads, same t/layer/raw
QByteArray itemsToClipboardJSON(const std::vector<const Item *> &items);                     // the .ink item lines, MIME "application/x-notolith-ink+json"
std::vector<Item> itemsFromClipboardJSON(const QByteArray &bytes, bool *ok);
}
```
Canvas: a drag with Tool::Lasso draws the lasso path (or a rect) in the overlay (dashed `QPen`, `palette().highlight()`); on release `m_selection = selectItems(...)`; selection bounds painted with 8 handles (6 px squares); drag inside moves (`QTransform::fromTranslate`), drag a handle scales about the opposite corner (`QTransform` translate-scale-translate), Delete removes, Ctrl+C puts JSON on `QApplication::clipboard()` with the custom MIME plus `text/plain` of the textbox contents, Ctrl+V pastes at the view centre offset +20,+20 with new `t`s, Escape clears. Every move/resize/delete/paste is one `commit()`. Holding `kHeldLassoButton` on the pen while in Pen tool does a lasso for that stroke only - unless `setHeldLassoEnabled(false)` (default true): then the held button changes nothing and the pen draws as usual. The glyph studio (Task 21) turns it off, because a glyph cell has no selection and uses the barrel click to save. Ctrl+left-drag with the mouse starts a lasso in any tool (also with `drawWithMouse` off: the mouse otherwise scrolls), so the mouse user can select without switching tools.

### Task 15: Text boxes

**Files:**
- Modify: `InkCanvas.{h,cpp}` (Tool::Text: a click on empty page makes a new box, a single click on an existing box edits it (spec section 7: no double-click needed with a pen); `beginEditingText(t)` creates a child editor over the rect, Escape/focus-out commits; rendering through a `std::function<QPixmap(const QString &markdown, const QSizeF &physicalSize)> m_textRenderer` the view installs), `InkView.cpp` (installs the renderer by building the host's `MarkdownEditor` directly, as `libs/ext/canvas/CanvasRender.cpp:377` does for a canvas text card: `setTheme`, `setMode(Reading)`, both scrollbar policies off, `resize(physicalSize)`, `setNoteText(markdown)`, `finishParse()`, then `grab()`; `CanvasView::makeContentEditor` is private to the canvas plugin and is not reused; the module links the host's editor symbols through the executable), `InkFormat` unchanged.
- Test: `tests/test_ink.cpp` (canvas with a stub renderer that paints the text with `QPainter::drawText`), `tests/test_window.cpp` (a real render: the box shows `[[Welcome]]` as a link-coloured pixel).

Rendering cost rule: text pixmaps are cached per (t, zoom, dpr) in a `QHash` on the canvas and dropped with the tile on `invalidate`; never rendered during a pen move. The pixmap is produced and painted on the UI thread over the tiles (R5, Task 11): a `QPixmap`, a `QWidget::grab` and a `MarkdownEditor` with widget fonts are GUI-thread objects, so the tile worker never sees a text box; only strokes and shapes (`QImage` + `QPainter`, thread-safe) are rasterised off-thread.

**Files:**
- Modify: `InkView.{h,cpp}` (the constructor parameter becomes the non-const `noteAppC::Settings *settings`, which is `ModuleHost::settings`: the view writes `ink.drawWithMouse` and `ink.pressureByDevice` through it; Task 8's `const Settings *` and `applySettings(const Settings &)` stay for reading), `InkModule.cpp` (more `registerSetting` rows and `Settings::registerDefaults` for the two hidden keys), `manifest.json.in` (no change), `core/InkTypes.h` (no change)
- Test: `tests/test_window.cpp`

Toolbar (left to right): Pen, Eraser (with Stroke/Split toggle), Select (Lasso/Rect), Shape (menu), Text - **R14: every tool is reachable from the toolbar and from the keyboard (P, E, L, S, T, Task 7) without any pen hardware**, because PADD's pen has no eraser end and its barrel buttons do not work; the held-button and eraser-end behaviour stay as a bonus. Each tool action is checkable, in one `QActionGroup`, has the objectName `inkTool_pen`, `inkTool_eraser`, `inkTool_lasso`, `inkTool_shape`, `inkTool_text` (Task 8's two actions get theirs here), a tooltip that names its key ("Pen (P)"), and follows `InkCanvas::toolChanged`, so a key press moves the toolbar and a toolbar click moves the canvas; the Select button's menu sets `SelectShape::Lasso` or `Rect` and the Shape button's menu the `ShapeKind`; a checkable "Draw with mouse" toggle bound to `ink.drawWithMouse` (flipping it writes the setting through `host.settings->setValue`, which the window applies at once; the Settings row stays in sync), separator, pen width (QDoubleSpinBox 0.5-40 doc px, step 0.5), colour (6 swatches from theme tokens `--text-normal`, `--color-red`, `--color-blue`, `--color-green`, `--color-yellow`, `--color-purple`, all opaque; no translucent highlighter swatch, the highlighter arrives with Spec 4 and does nothing over handwriting), pressure curve combo, separator, Undo, Redo, separator, zoom 50/100/200% combo, "Page" menu (page format combo A4/A5/Letter/Custom with width and height in mm, page background colour swatch from the theme tokens, layout combo Fixed/Continuous/Semi-infinite/Infinite, pattern combo with the 11 names, pattern size spin, "Add page" for Fixed/Continuous). Page changes are one `commit()` each. Settings rows registered in `attach` (so Settings > Plugins > Handwritten notes shows them, via Task 2's `setModules`): `ink.drawWithMouse`, `ink.rawInput` (A0), `ink.defaultLayout` (combo, "continuousVertical"), `ink.defaultPattern` (combo, "lines"), `ink.defaultPenWidth` (a double row: `SettingRow` with `decimals` 1, step 0.5, range 0.5-40, default 2.0, shown as a `QDoubleSpinBox` through `SettingsPage::addDoubleSpinRow`, Task 2), `ink.eraserWidth` (an integer spin 4-100, 20), `ink.tileCacheMB` (R5: an integer spin 8-1024, default 64, "Handwriting tile cache (MB)"; `InkView::applySettings` calls `canvas->setTileCacheMB`, Task 11). Two keys have no row (R14) and are registered with `Settings::registerDefaults` inside `attach`, so the single `adoptStored()` after `attach` (Task 2b M5) reads their stored lines: `ink.pressureMax` (double, default 1.0: the seed for a pen with no stored maximum, `canvas->setPressureSeed(settings.value("ink.pressureMax").toDouble())`; 1.0 means "learn it") and `ink.pressureByDevice` (a string holding a compact JSON object `{"<QPointingDevice::name()>": <learned max>}`, default empty). `InkView` reads `ink.pressureByDevice` once in its constructor (`QJsonDocument::fromJson`, every entry through `canvas->setLearnedPressure(device, value)`; a malformed string is ignored) and connects `InkCanvas::learnedPressureChanged(device, max)` to a slot that updates that one entry and writes the whole object back with `m_settings->setValue("ink.pressureByDevice", QString::fromUtf8(QJsonDocument(object).toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact)))` followed by `m_settings->save()` (a learned maximum changes a handful of times per pen, so saving at once costs nothing). A learned value therefore survives a restart and applies from the first sample of the next session (Task 6's `AStoredMaximumAppliesFromTheFirstSample`). New notes (`ink:new`) take the defaults. Autosave: `modificationChanged(true)` from the view arms the window's autosave (Task 2 wired it); after `save()` the view emits `modificationChanged(false)`. Mouse behaviour with `drawWithMouse` off: left-drag over the canvas scrolls the area (hand cursor); the wheel scrolls, Ctrl+wheel zooms by 10% steps (through the settle timer).

### Task 17: Seam 6: the host reads .ink text boxes (R1)

The index never loads a module (R1). `.ink` is a Notolith file format, so the host knows how to pull its text out, exactly as it does for `.ipynb`: `MainWindow::updateNotebookIndex()` registers a foreign reader for the suffix `ink`, and the reader parses the file with `QJsonDocument` and returns the text boxes' markdown. No module is loaded, the host does not link `notolith-inkcore`, and a vault that holds `.ink` files costs the keystroke path and first paint nothing.

**Files:**
- Modify: `libs/index/Indexer.h` (`using ForeignReader = NotebookReader; void setForeignReader(const QStringList &suffixes, ForeignReader reader); bool isForeign(const QString &path) const;` and `parseFile` takes a `const QHash<QString, ForeignReader> &` keyed by lower-case suffix instead of one reader; `setNotebookReader(r)` stays as a one-line wrapper over `setForeignReader({"ipynb"}, r)`, and `setNotebookReader(nullptr)` clears the reader, so every existing caller works unchanged), `libs/index/Indexer.cpp` (lines 129, 171-178, 212-215, 224, 265, 296-304, 450-470 generalised: `isForeign` looks the lower-cased suffix up in `m_foreignReaders`; like `setNotebookReader` today, `setForeignReader` rebuilds when a suffix gains or loses its reader and the vault is valid)
- Create: `libs/ui/InkTextBoxes.h`, `libs/ui/InkTextBoxes.cpp`: `std::optional<QString> inkTextBoxes(const QByteArray &bytes)` in namespace `noteAppC`. It sniffs the gzip magic (`1f 8b`) and inflates with zlib (`inflateInit2(&stream, 15 + 16)`, about fifteen lines, the same call Task 4's `inflateGzip` makes; the host cannot link inkcore, so the call is repeated here), parses with `QJsonDocument::fromJson`, returns `std::nullopt` unless the top level is an object with `"format": "notolith-ink"`, then joins the `"markdown"` string of every item whose `"type"` is `"textbox"` with `"\n\n"` (items in file order; an item that is not an object, has another type or has no string `markdown` is skipped; a file with no text boxes returns an empty string, which is a note with no text, not `nullopt`). The key names `format`, `items`, `type`, `textbox`, `markdown` are string literals duplicated from the `.ink` v1 schema (Task 4, `docs/INK.md`); the file carries a comment pointing at the schema, and a version above 1 is read the same way (unknown keys are ignored).
- Modify: `libs/ui/CMakeLists.txt` (add the two files; `find_package(ZLIB REQUIRED)` and link `ZLIB::ZLIB` privately; zlib already ships with Qt's own dependencies on every supported machine)
- Modify: `libs/ui/MainWindow.cpp` - **the one S1 edit to a shared file**: in `updateNotebookIndex()` (line 3094), after the `#ifdef NOTEAPPC_HAVE_NOTEBOOK` block and outside it, add
  ```cpp
      // R1: a .ink file is a note whose text is its text boxes while the ink feature is on (installed and not switched
      // off in Settings > Plugins); off, it is a plain file, exactly like a notebook with the notebooks feature off.
      // Read host-side with QJsonDocument: indexing never loads the module.
      m_indexer->setForeignReader({QStringLiteral("ink")}, featureOn("ink") ? Indexer::ForeignReader(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes) : Indexer::ForeignReader());
  ```
  plus `#include "InkTextBoxes.h"`. `updateNotebookIndex()` is already called from `applyFeatures()` (MainWindow.cpp:3089), which `onModulesScanned()` runs after it registers the feature (Task 2), so the reader appears when the scan finds the package and disappears when the switch is turned off; the `Indexer` rebuilds by itself on each change. `MainWindow.cpp:3098`'s `setNotebookReader` call is untouched (the wrapper keeps it working). `m_moduleHost.registerIndexReader` stays `nullptr` as Task 2b left it: no module indexes files (R1), and the member is not touched here (`libs/modules/ModuleHost.h` belongs to A0).
- Test: `tests/test_index.cpp`, `tests/test_inktextboxes.cpp` (new; registered in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` above the isolated-home `foreach` block, `target_link_libraries(test_inktextboxes PRIVATE noteAppC_ui Qt6::Test GTest::gtest_main ZLIB::ZLIB)`, ctest name `inktextboxes`), `tests/test_window.cpp`

- [ ] **Step 1: Tests** (R13: build them and record the RED run before any implementation exists; `test_inktextboxes` does not even link until `InkTextBoxes.cpp` exists, which is the RED).

`tests/test_inktextboxes.cpp`:
```cpp
#include <gtest/gtest.h>

#include <zlib.h>

#include "InkTextBoxes.h"

namespace {

QByteArray gzipped(const QByteArray &plain)
{
    z_stream stream{};
    deflateInit2(&stream, Z_DEFAULT_COMPRESSION, Z_DEFLATED, 15 + 16, 8, Z_DEFAULT_STRATEGY);
    QByteArray out(int(deflateBound(&stream, uLong(plain.size()))) + 32, '\0');
    stream.next_in = reinterpret_cast<Bytef *>(const_cast<char *>(plain.constData()));
    stream.avail_in = uInt(plain.size());
    stream.next_out = reinterpret_cast<Bytef *>(out.data());
    stream.avail_out = uInt(out.size());
    deflate(&stream, Z_FINISH);
    out.truncate(int(stream.total_out));
    deflateEnd(&stream);
    return out;
}

const QByteArray kFile = R"({"format":"notolith-ink","version":1,"document":{},"items":[)"
                         R"({"type":"stroke","t":1,"layer":"user","points":[0,0,0.5,5,5,0.5]},)"
                         R"({"type":"textbox","t":2,"layer":"user","rect":[10,10,200,40],"markdown":"See [[Lecture 3]] #exam"},)"
                         R"({"type":"shape","t":3,"layer":"user","kind":"line","points":[0,0,9,9]},)"
                         R"({"type":"textbox","t":4,"layer":"user","rect":[10,80,200,40],"markdown":"second box"}]})";

} // namespace

TEST(InkTextBoxesTest, JoinsEveryTextBoxInFileOrderAndSkipsEverythingElse)
{
    const auto text = noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(kFile);
    ASSERT_TRUE(text.has_value());
    EXPECT_EQ(*text, QStringLiteral("See [[Lecture 3]] #exam\n\nsecond box"));
}

TEST(InkTextBoxesTest, ReadsAGzipFileLikeAPlainOne)
{
    EXPECT_EQ(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(gzipped(kFile)), noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(kFile));
    ASSERT_TRUE(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(gzipped(kFile)).has_value());
}

TEST(InkTextBoxesTest, AFileWithoutTextBoxesIsANoteWithNoText)
{
    const auto text = noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(R"({"format":"notolith-ink","version":1,"document":{},"items":[]})");
    ASSERT_TRUE(text.has_value());
    EXPECT_TRUE(text->isEmpty());
}

TEST(InkTextBoxesTest, TruncatedForeignAndMalformedInputIsNotAnInkNote)
{
    EXPECT_FALSE(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(kFile.left(kFile.size() / 2)).has_value()) << "cut mid-write by a sync client";
    EXPECT_FALSE(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(gzipped(kFile).left(20)).has_value()) << "a truncated gzip member";
    EXPECT_FALSE(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(R"({"format":"something-else","items":[{"type":"textbox","markdown":"x"}]})").has_value());
    EXPECT_FALSE(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes("[1,2,3]").has_value());
    EXPECT_FALSE(noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(QByteArray()).has_value());
}

TEST(InkTextBoxesTest, ItemsOfOtherShapesAreSkippedNotFatal)
{
    const auto text = noteAppC::inkTextBoxes(
        R"({"format":"notolith-ink","version":2,"items":[7,"x",{"type":"textbox"},{"type":"textbox","markdown":5},)"
        R"({"type":"textbox","markdown":"kept","future":true}]})");
    ASSERT_TRUE(text.has_value());
    EXPECT_EQ(*text, QStringLiteral("kept"));
}
```
`tests/test_index.cpp` (beside `ANotebookIsIndexedByItsTextWhileAReaderIsSet`, same scan lambda style):
```cpp
TEST_F(IndexTest, AForeignReaderMakesASuffixANoteAndNotebooksStillWork)
{
    write(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"), "stands in for an ink file");
    write(QStringLiteral("Projects/Analysis.ipynb"), "{\"cells\": \"stands in for a notebook\"}");
    Index index;
    Indexer indexer(&index);
    {
        QSignalSpy done(&indexer, &Indexer::finished);
        indexer.setVault(vault);
        ASSERT_TRUE(done.wait(20000));
    }
    EXPECT_FALSE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"))) << "without a reader the suffix is an attachment";
    EXPECT_EQ(index.resolve(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")), QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")) << "and it resolves as one";

    QSignalSpy inkOn(&indexer, &Indexer::finished);
    indexer.setForeignReader({QStringLiteral("ink")}, [](const QByteArray &bytes) -> std::optional<QString> {
        return bytes.contains("stands in") ? std::optional<QString>(QStringLiteral("See [[Home]] #inked\n")) : std::nullopt;
    });
    ASSERT_TRUE(inkOn.wait(20000)) << "adding a reader rescans by itself";
    EXPECT_TRUE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")));
    EXPECT_EQ(index.notesWithTag(QStringLiteral("inked")), QStringList{QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")});
    bool fromSketch = false;
    for (const Backlink &link : index.backlinks(vault.absolute(QStringLiteral("Home.md"))))
        fromSketch = fromSketch || link.fromPath == QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink");
    EXPECT_TRUE(fromSketch);
    EXPECT_FALSE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Projects/Analysis.ipynb"))) << "the notebook has no reader of its own here";

    QSignalSpy nbOn(&indexer, &Indexer::finished);
    indexer.setNotebookReader([](const QByteArray &) -> std::optional<QString> { return QStringLiteral("zeppelin\n"); });
    ASSERT_TRUE(nbOn.wait(20000));
    EXPECT_TRUE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Projects/Analysis.ipynb")));
    EXPECT_TRUE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"))) << "setNotebookReader leaves the ink reader alone";

    QSignalSpy nbOff(&indexer, &Indexer::finished);
    indexer.setNotebookReader(nullptr);
    ASSERT_TRUE(nbOff.wait(20000));
    EXPECT_FALSE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Projects/Analysis.ipynb")));
    EXPECT_TRUE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"))) << "and clearing the notebook reader leaves it too";

    QSignalSpy inkOff(&indexer, &Indexer::finished);
    indexer.setForeignReader({QStringLiteral("ink")}, nullptr);
    ASSERT_TRUE(inkOff.wait(20000));
    EXPECT_FALSE(index.allPaths().contains(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink")));
}
```

```cpp
// R1: the index reads .ink text boxes host-side. The module is never loaded for it.
TEST_F(WindowTest, InkTextBoxLinksShowInBacklinksWithoutLoadingTheModule)
{
    write(QStringLiteral("Sketch.ink"),
          "{\"format\":\"notolith-ink\",\"version\":1,\"document\":{},\"items\":[\n"
          "{\"type\":\"textbox\",\"t\":1,\"layer\":\"user\",\"rect\":[10,10,300,40],\"markdown\":\"see [[Welcome]]\"}\n]}\n");
    MainWindow window;
    window.openVault(dir.path());
    window.show();

    window.scanModulesNow(); // registers the ink feature (installed = on): the host reader appears with it
    ASSERT_TRUE(window.openNote(path(QStringLiteral("Welcome.md"))));
    auto *pane = window.findChild<QWidget *>(QStringLiteral("backlinksPane"));
    ASSERT_NE(pane, nullptr);
    auto *list = pane->findChild<QTreeWidget *>();
    ASSERT_NE(list, nullptr);

}
```
