### Task 18: Bench gates and the PERFORMANCE.md row

**Files:**
- Modify: `tools/bench/Bench.cpp` (new phase `ink`, inserted after the `scroll` block at line 1858; `tools/bench/CMakeLists.txt` links `noteAppC_ink` under `if(TARGET noteAppC_ink)` with `NOTEAPPC_HAVE_INK`), `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` ("Where it stands" table rows + a "Handwriting" subsection under the rules, including the R8 numbers)

Measurements 1-7 (each a `Timing` through `reduce`, gates checked in `--json` by the orchestrator, not asserted in the bench); measurement 8 is a before/after comparison of two builds (R8):
1. `Ink pen sample` - an `InkCanvas` 1200x800 at zoom 1 with a 10 000-stroke document (synthetic: 100 x 100 grid of 30-point squiggles); feed 200 Hz synthetic `QTabletEvent`s for a 400-sample stroke; time from `sendEvent` of each move to the end of the `paintEvent` it triggers (`QCoreApplication::processEvents()` after each; measure the processEvents + sendEvent pair). Gate: median < 1 ms.
2. `Ink scrolled frame, 10k strokes` - the same canvas inside a `QScrollArea`, scroll by one viewport height, `paintedFrame(viewport)`. Gate: median < 4 ms (after the tile cache warms: discard the first 3).
3. `Ink scrolled frame, 50k strokes` - 5x the strokes; report the ratio to (2). Gate: ratio < 2 (the tile cache makes it view-bound, not item-bound).
4. `Ink zoom settle` - `setZoom(2.0)` then time until the sharp frame (`tilesScale() == 2`): `kZoomSettleMs` + the re-render; report the re-render part. Gate: < 100 ms.
5. `Cold start with module dir` vs `Cold start without` - `benchColdStart` twice, once with `NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS` pointing at `${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules` and once at an empty dir; gate: difference < 1 ms median (the scan is after first paint, so first paint cannot see it; this measures that nothing leaked before it).
6. `Ink open, 300 KB` - `InkView::load` of a 3 000-stroke file until `isLoading()` false; reported, no gate (the UI-thread part is the gate: the `load()` call itself returns in < 2 ms).
7. `Keystroke through the window, module dir present vs empty` - the existing `benchWindowKeystroke` (Bench.cpp:487) on the 1 MB note, twice: once with `NOTEAPPC_MODULE_DIRS` at `${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/modules` and once at an empty dir, **after the module scan has finished** (wait for the scan, then measure; spec criterion 2: the scan and the registered suffixes/commands must not slow typing). Gate: the ratio of the two medians < 1.10. Run each condition three times alternately and take the median of each.
8. **Cold start and executable size, main versus this branch (R8).** `ENABLE_EXPORTS` makes the `noteAppC` executable export its whole dynamic symbol table so modules can resolve host classes from it; that may cost start-up time (dynamic symbol relocation) and size. Measure it, do not guess: build `main` in its own worktree and build dir (`git -C /tmp/noteAppC-wt-s1 worktree add /tmp/noteAppC-wt-base main`; CONFIGURE `-S /tmp/noteAppC-wt-base -B /tmp/noteAppC-ink-build-base`; BUILD `--target noteAppC noteAppC-bench`), then, three times alternately for each build, `/tmp/noteAppC-ink-build-base/tools/bench/noteAppC-bench --phase start --json` and the same from `/tmp/noteAppC-ink-build-s1`, and `stat -c %s` on both `noteAppC` executables (and `size` for the text/data split). Report the median cold start and the byte size of both, and the two ratios, in PERFORMANCE.md. **Gate: cold start regression <= 5 % and size growth <= 10 %.** If either fails, switch to `-fvisibility=hidden` for the host libraries plus an export macro (`NOTEAPPC_EXPORT`, defined from a generated `libs/modules/Export.h` as `__attribute__((visibility("default")))`) on exactly the host classes and functions the modules use (`ModuleView`, `ModuleHost`, `MarkdownEditor`, `Theme`, `Vault`, `Settings`, the symbols the link errors name), and drop `ENABLE_EXPORTS` only if the dynamic table is then empty enough; re-measure, and commit the change as its own `perf(modules):` commit. If both pass, no change is made.

**Interfaces:**
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::hwfont {
struct GlyphTarget { char32_t codepoint; int wanted; };               // how many samples the font wants for this glyph
/// The default set: ASCII letters and digits, Latin-1 punctuation, and č š ž Č Š Ž. Letters want 3 samples, everything else 1 (settings hwfont.samplesPerLetter / hwfont.samplesPerPunctuation).
std::vector<GlyphTarget> defaultGlyphSet(int perLetter, int perPunctuation);
QString glyphDirectoryName(char32_t codepoint);                       // "0061" - four lower-case hex digits, six when needed
bool codepointFromDirectoryName(const QString &name, char32_t *out);

struct GlyphMetrics { double baseline; double xHeight; double ascender; }; // document px within the sample page
class GlyphStore {
public:
    GlyphStore(const QString &vaultRoot, const QString &fontName);     // <vaultRoot>/Handwriting/<fontName>/
    QString fontDirectory() const;
    int sampleCount(char32_t codepoint) const;                          // files <dir>/<hex>/<n>.ink
    QString nextSamplePath(char32_t codepoint) const;                   // the lowest free n
    bool saveSample(char32_t codepoint, const ink::InkDocument &sample, const GlyphMetrics &metrics, QString *error); // copies `sample`'s settings into a scratch InkDocument, writes extra["glyph"] = {codepoint, baseline, xHeight, ascender} there, mkpath, then writeInk() + Vault::writeBytes; `sample` is not modified
    std::vector<std::pair<GlyphTarget, int>> progress(const std::vector<GlyphTarget> &set) const; // (target, have), needs-more first, then by codepoint
    static ink::DocumentSettings samplePage();                          // Fixed, 400 x 400, pattern None, white
    static GlyphMetrics defaultMetrics();                               // baseline 280, xHeight 160, ascender 100 on the 400 px page
};
}
```

### Task 21: GlyphStudio view and the hwfont module

**Files:**
- Create: `libs/ext/hwfont/GlyphStudio.h`, `GlyphStudio.cpp`, `HwfontModule.cpp`, `manifest.json.in`, `README.md`
- Modify: `libs/ext/hwfont/CMakeLists.txt` (appends the studio sources to `noteAppC_hwfont` with `target_sources`, the manifest `configure_file` and the `notolith-hwfont` module target; this task, not Task 20, creates the module target)
- Test: `tests/test_hwfont.cpp`, `tests/test_window.cpp`? **No** - S1b does not own test_window.cpp. The window-level test lives in `test_hwfont.cpp`, which links `noteAppC_ui` (Task 8's registration) and constructs a `MainWindow` itself with the same `WAIT_TRUE(expr, timeoutMs)` helper (`#include "WaitFor.h"`) and the shared pen helpers (`#include "InkTestSupport.h"`, Task 7: `inktest::tablet`, `penStroke`, `stylus`; nothing is copied).

**Interfaces:**
```cpp
namespace noteAppC::hwfont {
class GlyphStudio : public ModuleView {           // objectName "glyphStudio"
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    GlyphStudio(ModuleHost &host, const QString &fontName, QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    bool load(const QString &, QString *) override { return true; }   // not file-backed: opened by the command
    bool save(QString *error) override;           // R6: writes the current cell (as saveCurrentAndAdvance, without moving on when the cell is empty); the host calls it on close and quit
    bool isModified() const override;             // R6: the cell holds strokes not saved or discarded: the tab shows the modified mark
    void setTheme(const Theme &theme) override;
    char32_t currentTarget() const;
    void saveCurrentAndAdvance();                 // Enter, the tick button, or the pen's barrel click (kHeldLassoButton press+release, consumed)
    void discardCurrent();                        // Backspace, the Discard button: clears the cell without saving
    void retarget(char32_t codepoint);            // click in the glyph list
    ink::InkCanvas *canvas() const;               // objectName "glyphCanvas"
};
}
```
Layout: left column = target label (the glyph large, in the UI font, plus "2 of 3"), the 400x400 `InkCanvas` on `GlyphStore::samplePage()` with guide lines painted by a thin `QWidget` overlay (ascender, x-height, baseline from `defaultMetrics()` in the theme's `--text-faint`), buttons Save (Enter), Discard (Backspace), Undo stroke (Ctrl+Z; Ctrl+Shift+Z and Ctrl+Y redo - the canvas's own keys, which the studio leaves alone; Escape does nothing); the studio calls `canvas->setHeldLassoEnabled(false)` (Task 13: a glyph cell has no selection, and the held barrel button must not turn a stroke into a lasso); right column = `QListWidget` of `progress()` rows ("a 1/3", needs-more first, done ones greyed), clicking retargets. Save: `store.saveSample(target, canvas->document(), metrics)`, clear the canvas (`document().reset(samplePage(), {}, {})`, `documentReplaced()`), advance to the next needs-more target, refresh the list, `statusMessage("Saved a 2/3")`. The barrel button: `InkCanvas` has no barrel-click signal and S1b may not add one (that is an inkcore change). The studio installs an event filter on the canvas and detects the click there: a `TabletPress` with `kHeldLassoButton` in `buttons()` followed by a `TabletRelease` within 4 px is a click - the filter **consumes both events** (returns true), so the canvas never sees them and draws nothing - and calls `saveCurrentAndAdvance()`. A press with the button held that then moves more than 4 px is not a click and is also consumed (no lasso in a cell). Settings rows registered by the module: `hwfont.fontName` (`SettingRow` has no free-text kind: a combo of the existing `Handwriting/*` directories plus "Default"; default "Default"), `hwfont.samplesPerLetter` (spin 1-10, 3), `hwfont.samplesPerPunctuation` (spin 1-10, 1). Command `handwriting-font:studio` "Open glyph studio" (the prefix is the feature id, so `applyFeatures` greys it when the feature is off, F10) → `host.showTab(new GlyphStudio(host, font), tr("Glyph studio"))`. `HwfontModule.cpp` spells every host type as `noteAppC::...` and uses `Q_INTERFACES(noteAppC::NotolithModule)`, like `InkModule.cpp`. Manifest: `id "hwfont"`, `feature "handwriting-font"`, `name "Handwriting font studio"`, `suffixes []`, `commands [{"id":"handwriting-font:studio","title":"Open glyph studio"}]`, `abi "@NOTEAPPC_ABI_TAG@"` (the ABI tag of R7: version, build-directory token, Qt version). There is no `dependencies` key (R2): the module links `notolith-inkcore`, which the package `notolith-ink` installs and the package `notolith-hwfont` depends on; with `notolith-ink` missing the load fails with the dynamic linker's message (`QLibrary::ResolveAllSymbolsHint`) and Settings shows it through `ModuleRegistry::loadFailure`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Tests**: `GlyphStudioTest.SaveAdvancesAndWritesTheFile` (a `ModuleHost` built by hand in the test with lambdas recording calls, `vault` pointing at a `Vault` on a QTemporaryDir; studio for font "Test"; `penStroke(...)` from `InkTestSupport.h` on `glyphCanvas`; `saveCurrentAndAdvance()`; `QFile::exists(dir/Handwriting/Test/0061/1.ink)`; `currentTarget()` moved on; the canvas is empty; **the glyph list's row for 'a' now reads "a 1/3"**, found through the `QListWidget` item text), `GlyphStudioTest.RetargetFromTheListAndDiscard` (click the 'b' row: `currentTarget() == 'b'`; draw, Backspace: the cell is empty and nothing was written), `GlyphStudioTest.EnterSavesBackspaceDiscardsAndCtrlZUndoesAStroke` (Enter saves one file; two strokes, `Ctrl+Z` leaves one, `Ctrl+Shift+Z` and `Ctrl+Y` restore it; Backspace clears), `GlyphStudioTest.TheBarrelClickSavesAndNeverDraws` (`tablet(canvas, TabletPress, p, 0.5, t, kHeldLassoButton)` then `TabletRelease` at the same point: one file written, the cell's document stays empty, and with the button held a stroke longer than 4 px draws nothing and selects nothing), `HwfontWindowTest.TheCommandOpensTheStudioTabAndSavesIntoTheVault` (a `MainWindow` on a temp vault, `scanModulesNow()`, `window.command(QStringLiteral("handwriting-font:studio"))` present (Task 2's public lookup) and `window.modules()->moduleForCommand("handwriting-font:studio")` present (`libnotolith-inkcore.so` is next to it in the build tree, so it loads); trigger it; `WAIT_TRUE(findChild<QWidget*>("glyphStudio"), 5000)`; stroke + Enter; the file exists under the vault; `tabTitle` is "Glyph studio").
  Plus the R6 test: `GlyphStudioTest.AnUnsavedCellIsModifiedAndSaveWritesIt` (a stroke: `isModified()` true; `save(&error)` returns true and the file exists; `isModified()` false; an empty cell: `isModified()` false and `save` writes nothing and returns true), and, in the window, `HwfontWindowTest.TheStudioTabShowsTheModifiedMarkAndCloseSavesTheCell` (draw a stroke in the open studio tab: the tab text ends with the modified mark the host uses for file tabs; close the tab with autosave off and a stubbed save-prompt answer "Save", exactly as Task 8 Step 5's file-less tab test does: the sample file exists afterwards).
- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**
```bash
git add libs/ext/hwfont/CMakeLists.txt libs/ext/hwfont/GlyphStudio.h libs/ext/hwfont/GlyphStudio.cpp libs/ext/hwfont/HwfontModule.cpp libs/ext/hwfont/manifest.json.in libs/ext/hwfont/README.md tests/test_hwfont.cpp
git commit -m "feat(hwfont): glyph studio module - targets, guides, save-and-advance, progress list

Co-Authored-By: <executing model's name> <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```
- [ ] **Step 6 (after S1 lands):** rebase `ink/s1b` on `ink/s1` (or `main` once it holds S1), move `libs/ext/hwfont/README.md` into the `## Glyph studio` section of `docs/INK.md`, delete the README, commit `docs(hwfont): glyph studio section in INK.md`.

## Self-review notes (written against spec sections 5-12 after drafting)

- Spec 5 (format): compact JSON written by `QJsonDocument` (R4: three decimals, sorted keys, no hand-rolled emitter), gzip read, pass-through, version > 1 read-only - Task 4. Images (`ImageRef`) round-trip in Task 4 but nothing draws or inserts them: spec 12 lists image insertion as out of scope for v1, so the placeholder fill in `paintEvent` stays.
- Spec 7 (model, input, rendering, canvas): Tasks 3, 5, 6, 7, 11. The spec's "QAbstractScrollArea holding InkCanvas" is realised as `QScrollArea` + `QWidget` (Task 7 header comment says why).
- Spec 4 (modules): Task 1 gate test first, Task 2 window seams, Task 8 real module. `registerTabletHandler` exists in `ModuleHost` (Task 1) but no module uses it in this plan; it stays as the declared seam, unimplemented in the window beyond storing the handler.
- Spec 9 (studio): Tasks 20-21. The barrel-button save goes through an event filter so S1b never edits inkcore.
- Spec 10 (A0): Tasks 1-8 + checkpoint.
- Spec 11 (perf gates): Task 18, numbers into PERFORMANCE.md; Task 11 is the mechanism.
- Spec 6 (seams): the spec's table numbers 1 view factories by suffix (Tasks 2/8), 2 tablet routing in the editor (declared only: `registerTabletHandler` stores a handler, Spec 3 wires it), 6 the foreign-file index reader (Task 17). Seams 3, 4 and 5 are Specs 3 and 4. Commands, settings rows, tabs and the theme are not numbered seams: they are `ModuleHost` registrations (`registerCommand` 2/8, `registerSetting` 2/8/16, `showTab` 2/21, `Theme` through `setTheme` 2/8). Seam 6 is host-side (R1): the host reads `.ink` text boxes itself with `QJsonDocument` (Task 17); indexing never loads a module and no manifest key or `Index` query exists for it.
- Consistency: `InkDocument::kPageGap` is introduced in Task 9 and used by Task 9's test only; A0 tests use `Fixed` single pages. `tilesScale()`, `pendingTiles()`, `staleTileCount()` and `setTileJobHook()` (Task 11) are test hooks, named as such; paint-rect assertions use `inktest::PaintRects` (an event filter, Task 7), not a hook in the canvas. Task 8's host names were checked against the headers after drafting: `Settings` has no change signal (hence `ModuleView::applySettings`), `Vault::root()`, `openNote`, `editor:save-file`, tab bar `noteTabs`.
- Depth: Tasks 0-8 (the spike and milestone A0) carry full test and implementation code. Tasks 9-21 carry interfaces, named tests with their assertions, the algorithm and the commit message, not line-by-line code; the executor of each writes the code against those, in the same TDD shape as Tasks 3-7.
- Commit trailer: every commit step ends with `Co-Authored-By: <executing model's name> <noreply@anthropic.com>`; the executor writes its own model name there. Every commit step lists explicit `git add` paths; no `git add -A`, no `commit -a`.
